# Realism — the apartment hub (world/), CQC (cqc/), Interstate (interstate/)

The Studio office is gone (Nov 8): root index.html redirects to world/index.html, the apartment. Sections below about the office, its bundle and add-ons are history.

Read `docs/ROADMAP.md` first ("Read this first"). Work one phase at a time; wait for user confirmation between phases.

## Project facts (verified)
- Vite build output only. Main bundle `assets/index-B2A-NN9Y.js` (minified). Add-ons: `addons/editor.js`, `addons/rally.js` (readable).
- Patch built files directly. Keep `__vite__mapDeps` (top of the main bundle) consistent with any chunk you add or remove.
- Interstate lives in `interstate/` as its own page (Phase 3). Its source of truth was the user's single-file `Interstate.html` (build 1.26).
- `?safe=1` skips saved room scripts and object scripts (Phase 2). The Minimal UI preset it should force comes with UI Settings (Phase 5).
- `/__studio/session` 404 on a static host is expected (a dev-server probe in the bundle).
- Serve locally: `python3 -m http.server` in this folder, open `index.html`.

Deleted: `assets/highway-*.js`, `assets/range-*.js`, `assets/rangeOrigin-*.js`, `assets/home-*.js`, `models/traffic/`, `models/g17/`, `models/awp/`, `sfx/rifle_shot.wav`, `sfx/bolt.wav`.
Kept: desk pistol (`models/service_pistol`) and its sounds: pistol_shot, dry_fire, mag_out, mag_in, brass.
Edited:
- `index.html`: removed duplicate outer html wrapper and the rangeOrigin preload.
- `assets/index-B2A-NN9Y.js`: removed range controller, Home loader, AR HOME button, highway map loader, Glock/AWM loading + desk placement + ammo pouches, weapon table (`Xs`) and .338 cartridge, rifle_shot/bolt from sound loader, Studio rows (Glock, AWM, Shooting range); rebuilt `__vite__mapDeps` (now handPreview, three.core, handModel).

### Phase 2 — Safety (done, awaiting headset check)
Added `addons/sandbox.js` (loaded before editor.js): a dry run of each room script in a warm Web Worker against a stub API (1.5 s limit after the worker starts; a hung worker is killed), and a time guard injected into every loop and function (acorn, via `globalThis.__studioParse`).
Budgets: script body 250 ms, each onFrame call 6 ms, timers/events 50 ms. A tripped guard keeps throwing, so try/catch cannot swallow it.
`editor.lastRun` in storage: `pending` while a kept script runs, `ok` after 3 s clean, `failed:<why>` on any error/timeout. Anything but `ok` on load means the saved script is not run (message in the status bar).
Edited:
- `index.html`: loads `addons/sandbox.js`; safe-start note on the start card.
- `assets/index-B2A-NN9Y.js`: exposes the acorn parser as `globalThis.__studioParse`; object-script guard (`_d`) also stops a call after 50 ms; object scripts do not start in safe mode.
- `addons/editor.js`: sandboxed async `runCode`; Game Editor tabs are Ask Claude / Room script / Object scripts (the old Code workbench, drawn inside the editor); Code and the old Assistant icons removed from the dock; `openApp("workbench")` opens Game Editor on Object scripts; window now 1840x910.

### Phase 3 — Interstate (done, awaiting headset check)
- New `interstate/index.html`: the uploaded single-file Interstate, with the stray outer html wrapper removed. Its 5 inline data blocks are now files beside it (`saloon-player.json`, `saloon-traffic.json`, `traffic-cars.json`, `hands.json`, `concept.json (removed in Interstate round 3: only the Lexus and the BMW are drivable)`); the game's `loadAsset` fetches them when no inline copy exists. Page is 0.85 MB instead of 10.4 MB.
- three.js r169 served locally from `interstate/vendor/three` (only the files it imports, minified core; since the R6 also the meshopt decoder); fonts from `interstate/vendor/fonts` (latin subsets, OFL). No external requests.
- VR quality default is Performance (was Balanced). Existing saves keep their choice.
- "Back to studio" on the main menu and the pause menu: saves, ends any VR session, goes to `../index.html`.
- `addons/rally.js`: door list is Keep it shut / Rally / Interstate, with a painted highway preview. Opening the door to Interstate fades, ends the VR session and navigates the whole page to `./interstate/index.html` (VR is only granted to the top page). `window.__rally.goInterstate()` added. Shorter door texts so three cards fit.

### Phase 4 — Performance (done, awaiting headset check)
Measured texture memory (scene textures + shadow map, mips counted, Quest path `?q=xr`): about 454 MB before, 208 MB after. Biggest wins were not in the roadmap list.
- Terrace reflection map: was a 96 MB PMREM made from the 4k sky; now made from a 1024-wide HDR copy (6 MB). `__envSmall` must copy `flipY` (the UltraHDR loader sets it) or the reflection is upside down.
- Sky: on Quest (`nm==="xr"`) the HDR sky is box-filtered 4096x2048 -> 2048x1024 in linear half-float at load (`__halfImg`), 64 -> 16 MB. Desktop keeps 4k. The .hdr.jpg is a gain-map JPEG; never re-save it with an image tool.
- Files resized (linear-light Lanczos; normal maps renormalised): oak 4096x1024 -> 2048x512, concrete 2048 -> 1024 (diff, nor, orm).
- Already present, verified: shadow maps update only on demand (`autoUpdate=false`), physics bodies sleep (`setCanSleep(true)`), textures loaded once per URL and shared.
- New `addons/perf.js`: warning at 80 physics props, new spawns refused at 100 (Game Editor `addThrowable`, object-script `spawnCopy`); storage `perf.propLimit=off` disables the stop. Foveation 0.5 in VR, 0.75 when the Profiler average frame time is over 12.5 ms for 2 s, back after 15 s under 9.5 ms.
- Bundle: `window.__studio.profiler` exposed; `spawnCopy` checks `__studioProps.full()`.
Still above the 120 MB target: the 1k model textures (lamp, plant, clock, pistol, ~64 MB) and 8-bit textures in general. Getting under 120 MB without losing detail needs KTX2/UASTC (about 4x less memory at the same resolution).

### Phase 5A — UI Settings, Editor mode, Console, Status bar (done, awaiting headset check)
New `addons/ui.js` (loaded last):
- Settings store `window.__studioUI` (storage `ui.settings`): console, statusBar, editorHelpers, propLimit. Presets Minimal (Console only), Comfort, Full, Reset (keeps Prop limit). `?safe=1` forces Minimal without changing what is stored. Page: System app, "UI Settings" tab (window grows to 780x800 when the tab opens). New features add a row to `FEATURES` and keys to `PRESETS`.
- Editor mode (`window.__studioEditorMode.on`): Game Editor window open and not minimised, and Rally mode is "room". Selection outlines and Profiler bounds/collider/frustum helpers are put on camera layer 30, enabled only in Editor mode with "Editor helpers" on (XR cameras inherit the layer mask). `editor.worldTool` is wrapped so the in-world move tool only works in Editor mode.
- Console app (dock, before System): lists `__studio.log` (the game's logger; it already captures console.warn/error and page errors). Filters, Follow, Copy, Clear, wheel/drag/PageUp/PageDown. Room script log() and failures go to it.
- Status bar: Editor mode / Play, prop count, frame health (smooth <= 11 ms, tight <= 13.9 ms, over) drawn by wrapping `os.drawMenuBar`; checked every 250 ms, redraws only when a value changes.
- `addons/perf.js` reads Prop limit from UI Settings.

### Phase 5B — Undo and Play/Stop (done, awaiting headset check)
- Already in the engine (verified): one Undo history (`__studio.editor.history`, 300 steps) for Studio edits; runtime modes play / paused / edit; Stop puts back what object scripts change (prop positions, non-prop transforms, visibility, colours, light intensity, spawned copies).
- `addons/editor.js`: each room-script run (Run, Ask Claude) is one step in that same history. Before a run the room is snapshotted (parents, visibility, transforms, mesh material refs, material values and maps, lights, background/environment/fog, exposure; physics props and the camera rig keep their place). Undo = remove the run's add()s, restore the snapshot, re-run the previous script (no worker check, it passed before) and show it in the Room script tab. Redo re-runs. A run that fails part-way restores the snapshot and the previous script, so the room is unchanged.
- Game Editor header: Undo, Redo, Play, Pause, Stop on every tab (switches "Undo and Redo buttons", "Play, Pause and Stop" in UI Settings; off in Minimal).
- `addons/ui.js`: Status bar shows Playing / Paused / Stopped and Editor mode separately; editor helpers are hidden while the game plays.
- Note: objects a script adds with scene.add instead of add() are not removed by Undo.

### Phase 5C — Inspector, Outliner, Snapping, Saved layout (done, awaiting headset check)
- Found: the Studio app already is a scene editor (viewport, Outliner with search/layers/multi-select, Inspector with Transform, Material, Light, Physics, Components, snap, add shapes, Duplicate/Delete/Frame, Save/Load). The roadmap's "no Inspector" was wrong; Phase 8 extends this Inspector.
- One editor: Studio is the Game Editor's "Scene" tab (drawn inside it like Object scripts; tabs Ask Claude / Scene / Room script / Object scripts). Studio's dock icon is removed; `openApp("studio")` opens that tab. The 3D preview is composited by the monitor shader from `vp.rect`, so it works embedded.
- New `addons/scene.js`: show/hide and lock buttons on every Outliner row, each an Undo step, remembered in `editor.outliner`. Hidden physics props stop colliding. Lock sets `entity.userLocked`; bundle lock check is now `(locked||userLocked)`, so moves, deletes and physics edits are refused (colour, light and rename still allowed). Snap grid/angle remembered in `editor.snap`.
- Bundle: angle snap cycles free / 5 / 15 / 45 / 90 degrees; Outliner status text moved left to make room for the buttons.
- `addons/ui.js`: "Saved window layout" switch. Windows and the Game Editor tab are stored in `ui.layout` and reopened (clamped on screen) once the add-on apps exist; nothing is saved before the restore.

### Phase 5D part 1 — Command palette, UI scale, Pointer feedback, Autosave and export (done, awaiting headset check)
- Found: the engine autosaves scene edits (`studio.autosave`, 1.5 s after a change) but only re-applies them on "Recover". New `addons/project.js` applies them at load ("Restore autosave on start", default on, never in safe start) and moves them into `editor.committed`, so they are the starting point, not Undo steps. `scene.js` waits for this, because `editor.load()` resets the scene first.
- Export / Import (`window.__studioProject`): one JSON file `{format:"studio-project", version:1, scene:<ops>, os:{editor.code, editor.keep, editor.outliner, editor.snap, ui.settings, door.dest}}`. Import validates, keeps the current project in `project.backup`, writes and reloads; "Undo the last import" restores it. Download uses an <a download>; in an immersive session the browser may hold it until you leave VR.
- New `addons/palette.js`: Commands window (no dock icon), Ctrl+K anywhere on the computer or the ⌘ button in the Game Editor header. Actions: Play/Pause/Stop, Undo/Redo, editor tabs, apps, travel, presets, every UI switch and choice, snap steps, export/import. Phase 6 adds the registry as a second source.
- `addons/ui.js`: UI scale 100 / 115 / 130 % (130 is the most the 1300 px editor fits). The OS lays out on a smaller os.W x os.H and draws with a transform; `wm.desk` and the Scene preview's `vp.osW/osH` follow. Newly opened windows are kept fully on screen. Pointer labels: "Pick up <name>" / "Use the computer" under the desktop dot, from the engine's own per-frame `desktop.pick()` (wrapped, no extra ray). Settings page scrolls (wheel). New groups Display, Saving.
- Palette window is not stored in the saved layout.
- Part 2 (wrist tablet, VR laser tooltips, recenter, quick undo) is next.

### Phase 5D part 2 — Wrist tablet and VR helpers (done, awaiting headset check)
- New `addons/tablet.js` (VR + Editor mode + "Wrist tablet" switch, group VR; off in Minimal). Panel 17 x 11.7 cm (512x352 canvas) above the left wrist (tracked-hand wrist joint, or controller grip + 8 cm toward the wrist), lifted 7.5 cm, always facing the head. Folded tab "▸ Tools" by default; state in `ui.tablet.open`. Buttons: Undo, Redo, Recenter, Play/Pause, Stop, Door (cycles `__rally.setDest`), fold; status line and last 3 log lines. Redraws only on change.
- Input: right-hand ray + trigger (or pinch), own edge state from the gamepad (never calls the game's `buttons()`), haptic tick; ignored while that hand holds a gun or a special-kind item. Per-frame work rides on `renderer.render` (main scene + main camera only).
- Meshes use `matrixWorldAutoUpdate = false`; otherwise the scene update overwrites the hand-placed matrix.
- Laser tooltip: "Pick up <name>" above the right pointer dot when it rests on a prop (Editor mode + Pointer labels).
- Recenter: turns the rig about the head so you face the computer; position unchanged.
- `addons/rally.js`: `__rally.dests`, `__rally.dest`, `__rally.setDest(id)`.
- Tested with the bundled IWER emulator (`?emulate`, `window.__iwer`): tab opens on trigger, Door button changes the door, Recenter 114 deg -> 0 deg, head did not move.
- Found (pre-existing): entering VR fetches controller profiles from cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@webxr-input-profiles (three's controller model factory). Needs internet; bundle it later if offline use matters.

### Phase 6 — Command registry and runner (done, awaiting headset check)
- `commands.json` (registry version 1) is generated by `tools/make_registry.py` from Appendix A: 121 commands (99 ready, 22 planned with the phase that brings them). Each: name, group, phrases with [blanks], blank types/ranges, example, reply template, cost tag, editorOnly, undoable, status. Also colours, props (null = arrives with the prop library), enums and the Appendix B physics presets. Edit the generator, re-run it, and ship both files.
- `addons/commands.js` (`window.__studioCommands`): normalises text (articles dropped, number words to digits), matches phrases by regex (literal words score highest, then typed blanks, free-text object names last), checks every blank's type and range, resolves "it/this/them" (selection, else what the desktop dot or right VR ray rests on) and object names, and runs. Scene changes go through `editor.applyBatch`: engine-validated, all-or-nothing, one Undo step, autosaved. Spawned things become the selection; "them" with several selected applies to each. Unknown or misread text answers with up to three suggestions.
- Realism notes: only the Sun and the window skylight are real lights; the downlights and the desk lamp are baked. "lights off/on" therefore acts on lights you add and says so; "night mode" lowers exposure and reflections; "dim/brighter" fall back to exposure.
- Readers of the registry: the Commands window (palette: "Run: ..." row, suggestions, examples, favourites, recent, browse by group), the runner, the Console command line (`help`, `commands for <group>`, up/down history) and later the Claude backend.
- Fixes found while testing: prop count now ignores deleted/hidden props (disabled bodies stay in `physics.props`); Console typing works before its first draw.
- Not yet: voice and Claude (Phase 9); "commands.json in the computer's Files section" (there is no Files app; the file sits at the game root).

### Phase 7 — Prop library and physics presets (done, awaiting headset check)
- New `addons/props.js` (`window.__studioPropLib`, loaded early so autosave replay can build props): 37 library props in Office, Driving, Building, Interactive, Toys and Lights, plus aliases "box stack" (3 cardboard boxes) and "seesaw" (base + plank). Each has a real-world size, mass, friction and bounce, compound colliders, and is built from simple shapes standing on y=0 then centred.
- Textures: generated once per session on canvas (crate planks with nails, cardboard with tape, painted drum with scratches and rust as packed ORM with metal where the paint is gone, cone with reflective bands, tyre tread, hay, book cloth and page edges, road sign, checker flag, bowling pin, diamond plate, brushed steel, rubber, weave, domino) with normal maps from height maps; plus the room's own scans (oak, concrete, plaster, fabric, grit) reused from the scene's materials so they are not uploaded twice. Geometries, textures and materials are shared by every copy. Generation runs in idle time after load.
- Bundle: spawn kind "prop" (`spec.prop`), validated, built by the library, with its own mass/friction/bounce and label; "mat" ops accept key "preset" (`presetOp`: maps + values, undoable, texture repeat from the object's size).
- Material presets for any object ("make it wood/steel/concrete/fabric/painted metal/glass/..."), 19 public presets.
- Props carry behaviours added in the same Undo step: button/switch/lever Interactable, pressure plate and trigger zone Trigger, spawner Spawner, balloon gravity -0.1. The trigger zone is visible only in Editor mode (layer 30) and has no collider.
- New Props app (dock): cards with texture swatches, tap to spawn in front; LOOK and PHYSICS chips for the selection. Command registry: 150 prop words; lamp/spotlight/lantern/neon strip are props; pendulum (needs joints) and effects are planned.
- Lag guards: spawned props are hidden and their bodies disabled while you are in Rally, restored on return; spawned lights cast no shadows.
- Fix: a pending autosave is written when the page is hidden (reload, close, door to Interstate) instead of being lost.

## Phase 8: Inspector (done; headset check needed)
- addons/inspector.js replaces the Scene tab inspector: Simple (10 options) / Advanced (groups A to N), search, sections that fold (remembered), Reset/Copy/Paste, cost tags (High asks once), tip line, material preview, perf badges, validation warnings, history panel ("Undo to here").
- Generic engine op `{op:"set", id, path, value, mi?, li?}` (bundle Fd/Id/Bd patched); paths are whitelisted in `window.__studioSet` (object shadows/render order, material side/wireframe/blend/tiling/offset/rotation/wrap/filter/anisotropy/pattern/reset, light range/cone/shadows, body drag/locks/ccd/ghost/kinematic, meta tags/notes/layer, scene fog/gravity/kill plane). One Undo step each, autosaved, replayed on load.
- New components: ConstantForce, StartVelocity, Pulse, PathMover (registered before the autosave is replayed; project.js waits for them).
- Moving (kinematic) bodies follow their object each frame; kill plane returns fallen props to their start.
- Editor view helpers on layer 30: grid, name tags, colliders (`window.__studioView`, stored in editor.view).
- UI Settings group "Inspector": mode, advanced physics, material preview, perf badges, validation, scene settings.
- Commands now ready: outline, reset_look, drag, lock_axis, ignore_collisions, shadows, teleport, look_at, show_grid/names/colliders, hide_helpers, "make it moving". Still planned: mesh tools (round corners, hollow, taper, detail), joints (pendulum, breakable), grabbable switch, group/ungroup, contrast, throw.
- Headset check needed: teleport fade and look-at turn in VR (comfort), Inspector readability, kinematic platforms.

## Phase 9: Claude backend, Claude in the editor, debug helper, voice (done; headset + live backend check needed)
- backend/worker.js: one Cloudflare Worker (pasted into the dashboard, so plain fetch, no SDK). Secrets ANTHROPIC_API_KEY + ACCESS_CODE; GAME_ORIGIN only; per-device and global rate limits (+ optional LIMITER binding); 64 KB request cap; 60 s timeout; stores nothing. Fetches the game's commands.json and sends every ready command as a tool (+ propose_plan, + optional web search), cached. Model claude-opus-5-5, effort low (auto) / medium (chat), fallbacks "default". /stt uses Workers AI whisper (binding AI). backend/README.md has the clicks.
- addons/claude.js: panel on the Ask Claude tab. Order: offline registry match → backend (quick: commands run a few per frame, one Undo step; job: plan with Approve/Cancel; chat: text) → old claude.ai script writer when not connected. Thinking strip with timer and Cancel; debug info (Console, Profiler, renderer info) attached when Debug is on, or on auto for slow/error questions. /connect, /code, /check, /forget, /clear.
- Voice: push-to-talk (Talk button). Routes in order: browser speech recognition, backend recording (/stt), Quest keyboard mic key. Heard text is shown in the box with "Did you mean" before anything runs.
- commands.js: runCall(name, args) runs a tool call through the same checks; registry version 2.
- Tested with a mocked Anthropic API: access code, origin, unknown command skipped, one Undo step, plan approve. Not yet tested against the real API or on the headset.

## Armory: pistol attachments for the desk P38 (done; headset check needed)
- addons/armory.js (loads right after props.js; project.js waits for it so autosaved parts come back). Props group "Armory": suppressor, laser module, weapon light (both with a barrel-clamp Picatinny mount: one rail, so they swap), micro red dot on the slide (moves with the slide), +3 magazine extension.
- Modelled to the P38's measured geometry (bore axis y 0.067, muzzle z -0.172, barrel r 7.2 mm, slide top 0.0745, grip base -0.050): lathe-turned caps, knurling, engraving, Picatinny teeth and slots, cap and slotted screws, lenses, labels. Generated normal maps (cerakote, knurl, stipple, turned). About 15k triangles with everything fitted.
- Working: suppressor moves the muzzle 163 mm and fires with volume 0.32 / flash 0.12 (bundle exposes __pistolSpec and __cartridges); laser dot via a Rapier ray; weapon light is a SpotLight (no shadow), 260 cd; red dot shows only through its window (CPU check, no stencil buffer); extension makes the magazine 11 rounds. L / B / Y switches laser and light.
- Fitting: let go of a loose part within 7 cm of its place (a blue ghost shows while held near), or "attach the suppressor"; "take off the laser" puts it on the desk; "remove all attachments"; "laser on". One Undo step each; the fitted set is kept in pistol.mods.
- Shooting targets (paper, silhouette, steel gong, popper, cans, scoreboard) are in the registry as "arrive next".

## Interstate: less baked traffic cars, snow trees (done; headset check needed)
- Traffic car shader (§16a carModelAssets): photo shading compressed (paint tint uses sqrt of the photo's relative luminance), photographed highlights cut on glossy parts, glossy paint (roughness x0.42), a clear-coat layer (one extra sharp environment fetch, Fresnel, compressed so the snowy sky cannot blow panels out), glass detected from the mask (glossy, not paint) made dark and reflective. Same in Mobile shading.
- traffic-cars.json paint masks re-made with tools/remask_cars.py (from the original kept in scratchpad dl/): colour-matched gaps next to paint, holes closed, glass and lamps never paint.
- Trees: interstate/snow-trees.glb (the uploaded pack, PNG to WebP by tools/pack_trees.py, 2.3 MB to 625 KB). §13.7a TreeAssets/TreeSystem: one InstancedMesh per kind and distance band, alpha-cut + alpha-to-coverage, both card faces lit like a crown. Planted on the snow banks from 5 m behind the rail (slopes up to ~58 deg), pines mostly, bare/dead trees, bushes near the fence; Quest keeps a forest edge (vegetation floor 0.4). Tree-line card not used (no alpha). None in the tunnel scenery (the default; trees show on Open road). Credits added.
- window.__interstate = app (test handle).

## Audit pass (lint, runtime, visuals)
- Bugs fixed: room-script frame loop got dt = NaN on its first frame ('last' was reused by the load check); room scripts and Claude's command batches ran on window.requestAnimationFrame, which stops in VR (now driven by the room's render / a timer); a silent script run left "Checking the script…" in the status bar; the start-up "no runner for …" message listed commands whose runners arrive later (now checked after 15 s, as a warning only if still missing); UI Settings list could draw empty for a frame after a large scroll.
- Visual fixes: Inspector sliders with long labels or cost tags get their own label line (they overlapped the bar); Inspector mode now "Simple / Advanced" everywhere (chips size to their text); stale "More switches arrive with the Inspector" footer replaced; light-colour reply for baked lights corrected.
- New: "fog light / heavy fog / fog off" command (the Inspector had a runner with no registry entry); dead "moving" runner removed (the body command handles it).
- Dead code removed: unused helpers and variables in inspector.js, props.js, rally.js, tablet.js, ui.js; Interstate: old procedural tree builder and material (wind sway moved to the card trees), unused BUILD constant and variables.
- Known and left: two three.js warnings at studio load (toNonIndexed on already non-indexed geometry x8, textures uploaded before their images arrive x31, both in the first 2 s only).

## CQC Loop: office door destination, hand fix, lag (done; headset check needed)
- cqc/index.html is the uploaded CQC_Looper_2.html made local: the 6 embedded GLBs are cqc/models/*.glb (glbData() fetches them), three r170 + addons, Rapier compat 0.14 and fonts are in cqc/vendor/. No CDN.
- Door: rally.js DESTS has "cqc"; goPage(url, msg) replaces goInterstate (both pages); __rally.goCQC(); commands "set the door to cqc", "go to cqc"; palette "Go to CQC Loop". CQC title has "Back to studio" (../index.html).
- Ghost hand on the slide: step 65 FREEGRIP left the rack hand where it closed, inside the slide. RACK_FIT routes the rack role to the fitted rack grip (?rackfit=off restores the old behaviour). Hand veins softened (scribbly skin).
- Lag: gunshot bake made an array per audio sample (hoisted); body cam stills (an extra scene render + GPU readback) are skipped while in the headset; stills develop without per-pixel closures.
- ?dev exposes window.__cqcDev for tests.

## CQC Loop: new enemy characters (step 66; headset check needed)
- The procedural operator (buildOperator, its merged box/lathe parts and materials) is deleted. Enemies are now the two uploaded GLBs: cqc/models/enemy-fsb.glb (FSB operator, Mixamo rig) and enemy-soldier.glb (soldier, Character Creator rig), packed by tools/pack_enemy.py (own rifle + animations dropped, transmission/specular stripped, textures 1024/512 WebP: 23 MB -> 8 MB, 25 MB -> 6 MB). spec.look 0/1 picks the model.
- Each man = invisible DRIVER skeleton (built from the model's own joints; AI, IK, hit capsules, ragdoll unchanged) + the skinned model, synced by enemySkinSync (per-bone frame offsets; arms take their twist from the real elbow bend). Meshes merged per material on one skeleton per man (~10-14 draw calls).
- Realism: aimed from the eye (sights under the aiming eye, long-gun stock in the shoulder, cheek weld, bladed chest; pistol pushed out to arm's length), clavicles protract to reach, support hand slides back only if still out of reach, fingers curl on the gun (trigger finger on the guard) and half open on death, low ready relative to the floor (not the leaning chest), neck damped in the ragdoll. Model arms are brought up to 49 cm shoulder-to-wrist (they were 39/45 cm; real ~55).
- Per-model hit capsules / ragdoll limb lengths (e.hit, e.ragDef). ?dev exposes ENEMY, spawnEnemy, removeEnemy, enemyDie, enemyPose, enemyDress for tests.

## CQC Loop: blood (step 67; headset check needed)
- Body blood: each man's materials are his own copies (enemyBloodMat, shared program key) carrying his wounds; cloth soaks dark maroon around each wound with a ragged edge, runs down (world down, so it follows a body on the floor), wet sheen that dries, black wound core; grows while alive and after death. Exit wounds (addExitWound) on through-shots. enemyBloodUpdate per frame.
- Drag trails: new 'smear1'/'smear2' decals (streaky film along the drag, heavy then thinning), taken from the lowest wounded body part on the floor, with drops shaken off; config smearSpeed/Step/Max/AfterDeath raised.
- Wall runs: big wall spatters get a 'run' decal (drips down the wall). Pools: 512 px, lobed edge, normal map rim (bloodNormalTex), forming even under a man slumped against something. More spatter, satellites, drops, longer bleeding (CONFIG.polish.blood).
- Fixes: a fallen man's head/body could vanish (skinned-mesh bounds measured standing; now one culling sphere per man on his pelvis); ragdoll neck/spine/hips limited to a cone from the death pose (spherical joints have no limits; heads folded into chests).

## Spawns on the terrace, open middle pane (patch 11)
- tools/patch11.py: the engine's inFront() asks globalThis.__spawnPad first (addons/props.js: a 3 x 4 grid of spots on the "terrace" pad, stepping each spawn); the old spot is studio.nearPlayer() (used by Inspector path points). Every editor button, command and Ask Claude spawn lands on the pad.
- The west glazing's middle pane (between the two mullions, below the transom) has no glass and no collider: props (and you) go through. Side panes and the strip above the transom stay glass and solid. Verified: a ball rolls out through the middle, balls aimed at the side panes stop.

## VR hand-to-hand, hand size on held objects (patch 12)
- grabProp: taking something the other hand holds passes it over (both hands used to hold it, the hold fought itself and the 0.4 m safety release dropped it).
- A hand fitted to a held object follows the object's position and rotation, not its scale, so a scaled-up prop no longer makes your hand huge. Settings > VR > "Hands on held objects": Normal size (default) / Scale with object (__studioUI value heldHandSize).
- Verified in IWER: hand-off keeps it held (gravity 0, same height); x4 box -> hand scale 1.00 normal, 4.00 grow.

## CQC Loop: gun handling recheck (step 68s; verified in IWER with scratchpad gunlab.js, headset check needed)
Bugs found and fixed:
- On-grip test always passed (freeGrab compared the fitted frame with itself: rel aliased _fgM), so a gun held by the barrel fired.
- With that fixed, a perfect grip read off-grip: the palm solve shoved the gun up to 6 cm. Now GRIP_SETTLE: a hand closing within 8.5 cm / 1 rad of the grip settles into the fitted firing grip (gun blends in) and is drawn with the fitted grip (driveHeldGun classic path: fingers round the grip, thumb over, finger on the trigger). The free solve drew open, floating hands (fingers start open, palm slab backs the hand off).
- Support hand: closing on the handguard takes the fitted support grip slid along the handguard to where you grabbed (supportZone / g.supZ), drawn wrapped (palm under, fingers up the far side). Pistol support = the fitted cup. One hand holding a rifle by the handguard alone is drawn with that grip (F.asSupport, can't fire).
- Gun held off the grip + the other hand takes the grip: that hand becomes the firing hand, the first the support (was: support, could never fire). Hand-over (firing hand lets go) settles too.
- Recoil spring blew up on a long frame (2 Euler steps): the gun flew away exponentially. Sub-stepped (4 ms) with a runaway guard; same for the enemy flinch / recoil springs (off-screen men are posed a few times a second) and the desktop heft spring.
- M4A1-S magazine: the split Magazine/Rounds meshes kept the whole rifle's vertex buffer, so the magazine's bounds, centre, collider and grab box were 0.9 m: support grabs on the handguard became failed magazine strips. compactGeo keeps only indexed vertices; magLocalBox counts only drawn vertices.
- A pulled magazine re-seated in the same frame (still in the well): it must leave the well first (item.stripFrom).
Lab (6 guns): grip R/L fires, off-angle grips settle, barrel grab can't fire, regrip fires, support-first then grip promotes, two-hand stable (0 mm jitter), rack, magazine out/in, hand-off.

## CQC Loop: enemy ragdoll, blood, voices (step 69; headset check needed)
- Ragdoll: his own limbs collide with each other (joined pairs don't), swing limits on neck/spine/hips/shoulders, 1.3x mass (gear), more damping, the death "muscle" phase runs 0.72x speed: slower, heavier folds, no arms through the chest.
- FSB loose gear (pouches, patches were unskinned meshes left where he died) is attached to the nearest torso/hip/leg/head bone at load (enemyRigidToBones).
- Blood: the main pool starts at the lowest torso/head wound, wounds pump harder and longer (bleedTime 22 s). Enemy materials 20% darker, rougher, less env reflection.
- Voices (cqc/sounds/): grunt1-6 cut from freesound 841313 takareads "strenuous_grunts", grunt7 from 874593 samrad20 "malegrunt03", breaths from 812821 beyercreative "male-heavy-breaths" (user supplied; check each license page and credit if CC-BY). Grunts on hits/deaths at his head (soundAt, occlusion), breathing loop fades in with stress. gun_small_room.mp3 (90967 department64) is converted but not wired yet; the AK range recording (402791) not used.
- Step 69.1: anatomical ragdoll limits (ANAT, ragAnatLimit) in the parent part's frame: hip extension 0.3 rad / flexion 2.2 / side 0.75, knee folds back only (2.5), elbow forward only (2.5), little sideways. A leg folded backwards at the hip (screenshot) can't happen any more.
- Hands (pending the user's OK): pose-driven skin. Per-vertex crease joint (aJnt) + per-hand joint bend (uHandFlex, from the bones each draw, own material copy per hand, shared program): straight = knuckle folds, bent = stretched pale knuckle skin, deeper palm creases; diamond skin lines on the back of the hand, ruddier relaxed knuckles.

## Studio: reset button, prop turning, training enemies, door guard (headset check needed)
- addons/reset.js (`window.__studioReset`): "Reset the whole studio" asks twice (arm, then confirm within 6 s). Clears every `studio.*` localStorage key (autosave, saves, outliner, scripts, UI settings, layout, door, pistol mods, tablet...), sessionStorage, IndexedDB `studio-assets` (imported models), removes enemies, ends any XR session, reloads. setItem for `studio.*` is blocked on the way out so the pagehide autosave flush cannot write back. Buttons: UI Settings (top right of the page and a STUDIO row at the bottom), wrist tablet "Reset studio", palette "Reset the whole studio". CQC/Interstate saves untouched.
- addons/rotate.js (`window.__studioRotate`): wraps `xr.handLogic` / `xr.locomotion` and listens to keys; no bundle patch. VR holding a prop (not the pistol/mag kinds): stick X turns about vertical, stick Y tips about the head-right axis, stick pressed + X rolls, B/Y upright (keeps heading); that hand's stick does not walk/snap-turn while it holds (locomotion sees a zero-axes stand-in for it). Two hands: other grip squeezed in the air (holding nothing) turns the prop with the line between the hands. Desktop holding: Q/R turn, Z/C tip, V upright, Alt+wheel turn (Alt+Shift+wheel tip); not holding: Q/R/V run "rotate it ±step" / "stand it upright" (undoable). UI Settings group "Holding props": Turn step (Free/15/45/90, default 45), VR stick while holding (Turn it/Walk), Two-hand turning, Mouse wheel while holding (Push-pull/Rotate).
- addons/enemies.js (`window.__studioEnemies`): CQC's enemy-fsb.glb / enemy-soldier.glb loaded through the bundle's own GLTFLoader chunk (one template per look, skinned clones share geometry/materials; skinned parts merged per material). Arms relaxed from the bind pose by aiming the upper/fore arm bones. Invisible capsule proxies (head/torso/legs) take `userData.onBulletHit`; the skinned meshes have raycast disabled. Damage head 100 / torso 40 / legs 25; flinch on the spine bone; death = scripted topple about the heels away from the shot (not a ragdoll); get back up after 6 s (Props app switch, storage studio.enemies.getUp). Max 6, not saved, castShadow off. Spawn: Props app group "Enemies", palette, tablet "+ Enemy". Remove: Delete with the dot on him, VR B while the right ray points at him, "Remove all enemies".
- Door (rally.js): the office door entity is now `locked` (engine refuses move/delete, including replayed autosave ops); a guard (every 1.5 s in the office, at start-up and on pageshow) puts it back if found hidden/detached/moved (not when hidden in the outliner); pageshow with `persisted` (back-forward cache after Back from CQC/Interstate) clears busy/fade so the door works again. The cause of the reported missing door was not reproduced in Chromium (fresh and back navigations, VR emulator, Rally x3, Quest UA).
- Interstate round 3 (subagent): only the Lexus (default) and BMW e30 drivable; working ignition key, indicator stalk, hazards, light switch, wipers stalk, radio (radio/radio.json: 101.1 PHONK, 96.5 ROCK, 88.9 HARD ROCK / METAL, streamed), horn, BMW 5-speed H-pattern lever, Lexus P-R-N-D; live gauges; rebuilt mirrors (own glass + housing, wider, no black); cabin occlusion; louder deeper engine with the synth off; points banked at once, half lost on a clip; seated driver body; walkable garage. VR interactions untested.

## Interstate: highway network (build 1.30, Open road scenery only; headset check needed)
- §13.2b in interstate/index.html: buildNetwork(A) adds loops B (outside, 2 lanes), D (outside, 2 lanes), C (inside, 1 lane), each tangent to the ring at one site (~280 m apart, flattest site), clockwise so their ring-facing side is +u.
- Slip roads (RampRoad, one-way, 1 lane): two loop ramps per site (~115 m radius, 280 m tapers) plus at B two flyovers (A's far carriageway <-> B) bridged over the ring (spans from Network.bridges, earth fades over 20 m, piers in NetStatics). Params in RAMP (fly params tuned offline: minR ~300 m, grade <= 4.4%).
- Network: one ground (blend of every road's earthworks), verges stop at the midline (vergeLimit), rails/delineators/noise walls open where roads meet (railGap/wallOff), slip-road paint off on host paving.
- Cars: VehicleDynamics.net.follow switches sim.track (core = within edge; tapers not core). Traffic follows the loop you are on; the ring's outer carriageway uses trackRev (reversed ring) as its traffic view. Autopilot has a slip-road mode (<= ~95 km/h). Exit signs 1 km / 400 m before each slip road.
- Trees: ~1.6x density, all biomes keep trees; Lambert cards get sky + snow-bounce ambient (uTreeAmb) so shaded crowns are no longer black.

## CQC step 83: the map turns round at stairwells (CONFIG.stairs.uturn; headset check needed)
- roomFacing(i): +1/-1, flips after every stairwell. A turned room is rotated 180 deg (room.f = -1): world = origin + f * local in x and z. Use rWX/rWZ/rLX/rLZ/rYaw everywhere a room-local point becomes a world one (props, cover, enemies, decals, doors, nav, floors).
- U-turn stairwell (stairParams with S.u): both doors in the entry (+z) wall, one over the other; corridor along it at each floor (the upper one a slab), far flight from the ground pad, half landing, near flight back to the top pad. stairFloor(S, lx, lz, hint) uses the current floor as the hint where two floors overlap. Level-bound walk boxes: bot (ignored by anyone well below it) and lvTop (ignored above it). Builder: stairBuilderU; colliders in activateRoom (U branch).
- Wings stack: insideRoom / roomContaining / headRoom take y. The exit leaf of a U well hinges on its other edge and is drawn half a turn round (door.vis); the joint body rests square to the room (a revolute joint's zero is the relative turn).
- Safe rooms face the room they open into (roomFacing(slot + 1)).

- cars/*.glb + .json (scratch carprep/: real size, front -z, wheels removed and measured). GLB_CARS + buildGlbCar: own cabin (like the '97 saloon), own clear-coat paint sharing the garage colour (M.paint's object-space gaps are sedan-shaped). Garage has four slots.

## Interstate realism 1: water on the windscreen, working wipers (computer, cockpit camera; graphics.windscreen, ?wscreen=off)
- Audit (MASTER_PROMPT §5 A-K): lens rain, road spray, skid marks, wet road and body water, exposure, headlights, camera weight and road audio already exist; the cockpit had no water on the glass (lensRain cockpit 0) and the wiper stalk moved nothing. That gap was picked.
- DriveWorld #windscreen(dt, env, speed) (live and replay): water clock Wc (rain x (0.45 + v/14) + wet-road spray), glass wetness (dries ~4 min, faster in the airflow), runner drift along the rake (up at speed, creep down standing). Wipers from cab.wipers with the key on: intermittent (0.62 s strokes, 2.2-3.6 s pause), fast (0.42 s); off mid-sweep finishes and parks; 'wipe' cab event = a soft knock (audio #click 160 Hz). State in this.ws (one object), visualData().ws.
- #wsGlass(key): once per body, 350 rays from the driver's eye (CABIN_CEL / CABIN_E30) against the visible body triangles; first hits fitted to a symmetric plane y = a + cz (outliers dropped in 4 rounds, slack for the wrap); extent mirrored about the centreline; LHD tandem blades placed in it. Fallback: a 32 deg screen ahead of the inner mirror (Log.warn). Headless celsior (first, unsymmetric fit): 124 hits, rake ~25 deg, plane 1.5 cm in front of the inner mirror.
- finishShader (compiled at load, no new program): pixel ray meets the plane at g (m); only where the depth prepass is on or beyond the plane (dash, pillars, mirror, roof lining stay dry). Two static drop layers (cells 13 / 8.5 mm, merged beads grow, big ones blown off), runners with wakes, a bead ahead of a moving blade, film behind it, worn-blade arcs streaking lamps toward the pivot at night, drawn blades, arms and nuts. Last pass per point solved from the current + previous stroke (uWk0 / uWk1, water at their ends uWw). Drops refract: the bent ray is re-projected, so they show the world inverted.
- Needs the depth prepass (Medium and up); without it nothing is drawn. Never in the headset (VR renders without the finish pass; VR_GFX_OFF also sets windscreen false). No per-frame allocation.
- Checked: syntax; one headless SwiftShader run (open road, rain, cockpit, fast wipers): no page or shader errors, glass fitted, uWsOn 1. Not verified by eye (the fit was then made symmetric; not re-run) or on a real GPU.

## Interstate realism 2: slush spray and tyre tracks in snow (graphics.slush, ?slush=off; headset check needed)
- Road shader (asphaltMaterial, SLUSH_GLSL_MAP / _ROUGH, injected at load only when SLUSH.on): grey-brown ploughed slush on both shoulders, a ridge between the wheel tracks (lxl 1.85) and thin along lane lines; fresh snow on the deep shoulder; rougher, less wet sheen. Uniforms uSlush (0 = old road: tunnel or setting off) and uSlushQ (0 in the headset: one noise less). slushAt(track, u) is the same profile on the CPU at its mean patchiness; slushPaved limits tracks to the paving.
- DriveWorld #effects: where a wheel's u crosses slush, grey-brown PT.spray mist + PT.debris lumps (land on the road) scaled by speed; traffic does the same per side (rear wheels, trucks more), sharing one trafficSources list with the wet spray. emitAcc 16 -> 48 (slots 16-39).
- Tracks: SkidMarks(scene, { segs 3000, step .7, life 45 }) = this.tracks, rear wheels only, live only (not replays, not in VR); aux carries birth time, trackMat fades them (pressed dark road, tread ribs, light rim). SkidMarks.wheelAt now writes in place (no array per segment, same output).
- fixedUpdate: this.slushK (both wheel tracks) -> sim.slushN = slushK x 150 N x (1 + v/40) added to longitudinal drag only. Audio: slush adds to the wet hiss and a new low squelch (brown noise, lowpass, tread-rate AM), nodes made only when SLUSH.on.
- Headset: a third of the spray, no lumps, no tracks. ?slush=off: no shader code, no tracks mesh, no audio nodes, no drag (old behaviour).
- Checked: syntax; one headless run (open road): no page errors, uSlush 1. Not checked by eye, by ear or on Quest. P.off is "past the lanes" (the shoulder), so it does not gate slush.

## Interstate realism 3: exposure in and out of tunnels (graphics.tunnelAE, ?tunnelae=off; computer only)
- Extends the existing exposure (WorldEnvironment: open-road w.exposure from SKY_KEYS, TUNNEL_EXPOSURE 1.5 in the tunnel). #eye(dt, force) in WorldEnvironment keeps eyeEV (log2 adapted exposure) and sets world.eyeAE = 2^(eyeEV - log2 w.exposure); RenderSystem.render multiplies it in. TUNNEL_AE holds the knobs.
- Brightness from state only: tunnel flag, distance to the exit portal (track.tunnelSpans [s0, s1, ...]) and time of day (the open road's sky exposure; tunnel scenery: a snowy afternoon, .74 x snow .6). Opening up tau 1 s (2-3 s to settle), closing down tau 0.22 s (blown out, settles in ~0.7 s). Within 220 m of an exit the eye shifts 40 % toward outside, so the portal stays white.
- Found: neither scenery has a portal today (tunnel scenery is one endless bore, the open road has no tunnels; tunnelSpans is never set). So the visible effect is the run start in the tunnel (eye arrives from the snow: ~-1.75 EV, opens over ~2-3 s); the exit/portal path is ready for when spans exist. Open road without spans: eyeAE exactly 1.
- Headset: eyeAE 1, no animation. ?tunnelae=off never sets eyeAE (old exposure exactly). No per-frame allocation.
- Checked: syntax; one headless run (tunnel): no page errors, eyeAE 0.37 at start rising. Not checked by eye or on Quest.

## Interstate realism 4: road sound by surface (audio.roadSurface, ?roadsnd=off; headset check needed)
- Extends VehicleAudio #tyres (squeal, roll, roar, wet hiss incl. realism 2's slush, verge gravel, scrape, joints). ROADSND holds the knobs; ?roadsnd=off builds none of it and routes exactly as before; the setting off = new gains 0, old formulas, cabin filter open.
- World #roadSrc (from liveSrc): each wheel's u/s from the car's pose on sim.track (so drifts count) -> roadSurfWheel: rumble (edge lines, treated as profiled; no rumble strips are drawn), paint (lane dashes, 3 m of every 12), crunch (beyond tr.edge or in the median; off in the tunnel), snow (beyond roadOuter), bridge (1 - bridgeK: Network flyover spans). Folded per side into src.road.
- Audio #roadSurface (made once): per side a panner at the left / right wheels with a rib tone (v / 0.3 m) + paint zip, and gravel crunch (two shared square AMs) / snow churn; centre: concrete-deck whine, wet spray swish (pink, two slow LFOs; slush stays in the wet hiss). Asphalt roar = the old roar, lowered on decks and gravel. Old whole-car verge gravel muted when on.
- Deck joints: every 18 m along a span and at its ends, front axle then rear a wheelbase / v later, scheduled in audio time (#jointAt one-shots); the old 32 m thumps skip decks.
- Cabin: tyre bus (old tyre sounds too) and the side voices through rsCab, lowpass 20 kHz -> 1.1 kHz by this.cabin (the existing camera-mode cabin factor).
- Checked: syntax, one headless run (open road, no page errors, src.road present), Node check of roadSurfWheel. Not heard; replays have no src.road (crunch falls back to src.off).

## Interstate realism 5: headlamp beams (graphics.headBeam, ?headbeam=off; headset check needed)
- Audit: your lamps were two even SpotLight cones (no cut-off, no pattern); signs were plain diffuse; oncoming lamps were emissive + bloom only. Cockpit body motion, crosswind and traffic grime already existed.
- HEADBEAM / beamLow / beamHigh (JS) and HB_GLSL (same maths): dipped beam = sharp cut-off 0.57 deg down on the oncoming side, 15 deg kerb-side rise, hot spot ~1 deg down right, wide foreground spill; main beam = long hot centre + half the dipped beam. Baked once into 512 px DataTextures (beamTexture) used as SpotLight.map (angle 0.76, aimed level; the pattern carries the dip). Every car gets a map at construction (white), so NUM_SPOT_LIGHT_MAPS never changes: setting off = white map + the old angle/aim/intensity (no recompile). Peak lowK 1.6 / highK 2.55 x 2400 x world.headlights.
- Signs (signMaterial, key signhb): retroreflection = R_A (150) x the lamps' illuminance from the pattern, in a ~0.6 deg lobe about the lamp-to-eye line, capped (uHbMax 3). Uniforms set in setLights (lamp midpoint + body basis).
- Oncoming glare (computer only, finish pass): TrafficRenderer.dazzleAt keeps the 4 brightest facing cars (DAZ fixed arrays; each lamp's E at your eye from its own dipped pattern, 20000 cd peak); #feed projects them (uDz[8]: uv, k, view depth); shader adds 10 E / theta^2 veiling glare, 3-tap depth occlusion, x night^2, x(1 + 1.5 windscreen wetness). Gathered only when the finish pass asked last frame (DAZ.want), so never in the headset.
- ?headbeam=off: no map, no sign or finish shader text, no gathering (old code paths). No per-frame allocation; textures made at the first car build.
- Checked: syntax, Node check of the patterns (glare 0.15 lux at 50 m, 1 lux at 15 m), one headless night run (open road, dipped beam on): no page errors, 512 px map in use. Not checked by eye or on Quest.

## Interstate realism 6: snowfall, gusts, spray visibility (world.snowfall, ?snowx=off; headset check needed)
- Extends WorldEnvironment (no snow existed; rain stays as it was). SNOWX holds the knobs. #snowX: under cloud (sstep .3-.75, mostly replaced by rain) snow alternates calm flurries (0.06-0.2, 35-110 s) and bands (0.55-1, 18-60 s), eased tau 7 s; env.snow. Heavy snow: fogMult x (1 + 2.4 snow^1.5), fog greyed. Label "Snow / Heavy snow"; toast on a heavy band.
- Gusts: events (rise, hold 1.5-4 s, decay) -> env.sxGust m/s; #crosswind adds sxGust x 0.6 to the old wind speed (same force model, ~300 N at most at 30 m/s); trees and particle wind follow.
- Flakes: snowMesh (rainGeometry, own shader, layer 6): CPU-drifted box 30 x 18 x 30 m with flutter, speed smear, sky + sun + street lamp light, your headlamps' cone (dipped cut-off) lights them (HDR, so they bloom on a computer). 9000 / 3000 (Low, Quest preset) / 1400 in the headset, by snow level. Always visible with an empty draw range, so it compiles at load; kept out of the depth pre-pass (fxHidden).
- Spray veil: DriveWorld #sprayVeil finds the vehicle ahead in your path (2-75 m, going your way) throwing slush (slushAt), water or fresh snow; truck 1, car 0.45 -> this.sprayV.veil (eased). Fog x (1 + 2.2 veil) everywhere; on a computer the finish pass adds a depth-scaled rolling veil (4-tap glow, colour = fog through the exposure + your lamps' backscatter at night) and bloom widens / lowers its threshold (strength .2 -> .7, threshold 1.15 -> .7). Big slow puffs behind it (emitAcc slot 40; 5/s in the headset).
- Off (setting or ?snowx=off): no flakes, no gusts, fog / bloom / crosswind exactly as before; ?snowx=off also adds no shader text or mesh. No per-frame allocation.
- Checked: syntax; one headless run (open road, night, overcast, band forced): no page errors, snow 1, fog x4.9, lamps on the flakes. Not checked by eye or on Quest.

## Interstate realism 7: road grime and salt on your car (graphics.grime, ?grime=off; headset check needed)
- Reuses the traffic's road film: ROAD_GRIME_GLSL (colour + roadGrimeLow band) now feeds trafficAssets' paint (same maths) and the player's layer. grimeMat(mat) chains onBeforeCompile (like cabinOcclusion, key +grime7) on M.paint, the saloon's non-glass parts, the BMW pack body, GLB paint / lamps / tail and head glass / own opaque materials; compiled with the car at load.
- grimeBake(group, body, wheels, wagon) once per body: attribute grimeA (weight, salt band, z, height; body space): sills, behind the wheels (rears most), lower bumpers, the tail face on the m3 wagon; none above ~1 m, none inside the cabin (outward-facing flanks / ends only). One uniform per material, shared: GRIME.U uGrime (film, dried salt, film wetness).
- DriveWorld #grime (live only): film from wet road, slushK and the spray veil ahead, x speed; brine from slush / wet road dries into tide marks (shown x (1 - wetness)^2); clean at enter(), reset by leave(). Lenses: emissive x (1 - .5 film), headlamp SpotLights x lensK (1 - .22 film). Windscreen (realism 1 pass): uWsGr film outside the blades' arcs, never inside.
- Setting off = uGrime 0 (no recompile); ?grime=off = no shader text, attribute or uniform. No per-frame allocation. No garage wash.
- Checked: syntax; one headless run (open road, forced grime): no page errors, 23 saloon meshes baked (unbaked users of a grimed material, e.g. the cabin glasshouse, read weight 0). Not checked by eye or on Quest.

## CQC step 85: realism pass (BODYCAM brief in docs/MASTER_PROMPT.md; headset check needed)
- Room acoustics: ACOUSTICS small / room (the original IR, unchanged) / hall / stair / range, picked by roomAcoustic(stream.current). audio.verb is now a GainNode bus into two convolvers (ROOMAC.slots) crossfaded by updateAcoustics; the idle one is unplugged after its tail. Headset caps tails at 1.8 s. CONFIG.polish.audio.acoustics=false = one reverb. ACOUSTICS/ROOMAC are declared beside `audio` (audioUnlock runs early; its local `AC` is the AudioContext class, hence ROOMAC).
- Your own footsteps: ownStep(f) from bodyPre when a body foot lands (heel, grit, kit rattle, trousers when running; deeper on stairs, softer crouched, shuffles silent). Not positional, reverb carries them. CONFIG.polish.audio.ownSteps (gain, 0 = off).
- Air absorption: soundAt lowpasses positional sounds at 20 kHz x e^(-d/air), CONFIG.polish.audio.air 55 m (0 = off).
- Weapon light beam: FLASH_BEAM spot light map on the shadowed flashlight (hotspot / corona / spill, reflector rings), cone 0.38 -> 0.62 rad, intensity x1.6 (setFlashlight). Haze/motes keep the old 0.38 cone. ?beam=off restores.
- CONFIG.post.chroma 0.0025 -> 0.0012 (body cam mode keeps its own extra fringe).
- ?dev adds ROOMAC, ACOUSTICS, roomAcoustic, updateAcoustics, ownStep, audio, flashlight, setFlashlight, FLASH_BEAM.
- Verified in headless Chromium (SwiftShader, ~0.2 fps): loads with no errors, convolver crossfade between every acoustic kind, ownStep plays, beam renders. Not verified: how it sounds, Quest frame time.

## CQC step 86: body bumps (headset check needed)
- bodyBump(cx, cz, onDoor) from locomote: the push resolveBody gives the moving head is the contact (door loop unchanged: breach first, else nudge; bDoor / bBreach flags). Fresh contact into a wall, prop or leaf above min m/s: self thump (shoulder, sleeve, kit; a nudged leaf rings with kickSfx's modes, quieter), and in the headset a 30 ms buzz weighted to the hand on the wall's side. Sliding along: sleeve scuff (brush), no buzz. Skipped during BREACH / KICK / death. Not positional, no AI noise.
- CONFIG.polish.audio.bump { gain (0 = off), min m/s, free s, cool s, brush (0 = off), haptic }. ?bump=off. pulse() now stamps hand.hapEnd; pulseSoft(hand, s, ms) skips while a pulse is still running (the Quest replaces a running pulse).
- Footfall haptics rejected: they would mask the heartbeat buzz. ?dev adds BUMP, bodyBump, pulseSoft.
- Not verified: how it sounds and feels on a Quest.
## CQC step 86 (cloth foley): your clothes and kit, heard (headset check needed)
- clothFoley(dt) from tick right after bodyPre; state in CLOTH. Self sounds like ownStep: nBurst straight to audio.out (room reverb carries them), no soundAt voice, no AI noise, no haptics (the VR crouch toggle keeps its own pulse). CONFIG.polish.audio.cloth (gain 0.5, 0 = off).
- Crouch / kneel: eye drop below standing (BODY.standEye in the headset, CONFIG.player.eyeHeight on desktop) past 0.38 m x your scale; at the bottom of the move (smoothed drop speed under min(0.5, 0.05 + 0.35 x peak)) clothSettle plays: boots take the weight, grit, trousers fold, belt kit tick (louder for a faster drop). Back up under 0.22 m: clothRise (swish, two kit ticks). Seated: the crouch toggle only (leaning to the floor is not a crouch). Looking down past 0.8 rad at the bottom (looting) = silent, and its rise too. A jump landing into a crouch is silent (jumpLand is the sound). A one-frame jump over 0.25 m (re-base, calibration, respawn) resyncs silently.
- Hard pivot: a play-space torso yaw (camera.quaternion, so snap and smooth turns never count) following the head past CONFIG.body.turnDead like bodyPre; only the yaw beyond the dead zone adds up (decays 5/s); over 0.45 rad = one ripstop swish (at most every 0.5 s), louder for a faster turn. A headset recentre (over 1 rad in one frame) re-bases.
- Fast arm (headset): grip speed in rig space over 2.4 m/s = a faint sleeve swish, one per 0.3 s for both hands.
- ?dev adds CLOTH, clothFoley. State machine checked in Node with mocks (scratchpad cloth_test.js). Unverified by ear and on Quest.
## CQC step 86: door foley (not verified by ear or on Quest)
- doorSfx(door, kind, k): positional at the latch edge (2 x leaf centre - hinge, lever height) through soundAt's voice pool, event-based, no persistent nodes. lever = leverK reaches 1 (VR: pulse 0.3); spring = leverK back to 0 after a full turn (a knock into the strike when the leaf is shut); rattle = a locked door (next room not built) pressed on the computer or gripped in VR (released as before, at most every 0.4 s); shut = a leaf that passed 0.05 rad settling inside 0.012 rad under 0.06 rad/s; stop = the leaf crossing maxOpen/maxBack - 0.03 above 0.9 rad/s (a kick or shove). The breach and kick paths keep kickSfx. AI hearing unchanged.
- Shut gate is settling speed, not the 1.2 rad/s first planned: a headless Rapier run of the vendored build with this door (scratch doorsim/) showed a desktop shut press carries the leaf through 0 at ~0.42 rad/s to -0.24 rad, while the closer brings a leaf home at ~0.01 rad/s.
- Same run: with Rapier's default sleep (under 0.5 rad/s for 2 s) a leaf on the closer alone fell asleep ajar (from 1.54 rad it slept at ~0.96; after a kick at ~1.1). Not changed here; worth a check in the game (doorOpen uses 0.012).
- updateDoorsPost(dt) now takes the clamped physics dt. CONFIG.polish.audio.doors { on, gain }; ?doorsnd=off = silent doors. ?dev adds doorSfx, DOORSND.
## CQC step 86: props knock (CONFIG.polish.audio.knocks; headset check needed)
- Props and debris knock by material and mass when they land, bump or fall over: stepPhysics calls knockTick for loose props (speed lost in one frame >= knocks.minDv 0.6 m/s; gravity and friction never lose that much), knockSfx picks KNOCK_MAT[p.name] -> KNOCK_SND card / wood / pipe (beam modes 1 : 2.76 : 5.4) / can / drum (the jerry can's boom) / block / plaster / glass / chair. Heavier = lower and longer (pitch cbrt(2.5 / mass), 0.6-1.6). Through soundAt (8-voice pool), 50 ms between knocks, 100 ms per prop, within impactRange. Items (guns, mags, shells) stay silent; the men do not hear knocks (no aiNoise).
- Held props also sound on contact (beside the buzz in stepPhysics; once per contact, not while you lean it there). A knock that breaks a bottle is left to propsBreakTick / glassSfx (unchanged).
- Quiet for 1.5 s after a prop is built (room settling, addProp knFrom); debris spawned flying (spawnDebris with vel) clatters at once, boards from memory do not; resetProp clears the speed so a teleport is not a landing.
- CONFIG.polish.audio.knocks { on, gain, minDv } (on false = silent as before). ?dev adds knockSfx, KNOCK_SND, KNOCK_MAT.
- Not verified: how it sounds, Quest frame time.
## CQC step 86: the building is alive (sound only; CONFIG.polish.audio.amb, ?amb=off; headset check needed)
- ambTick(dt) from polishUpdate's audio block: every amb.gap (12-36 s) a building sound, but only between fights (no engaged man in an active room, SPAT.dose <= 0.15, no impactSound for amb.quiet 8 s, not dead); busy = retry in 3-6 s. AMB (next, ?amb=off) and _amP are declared beside ROOMAC.
- ambPipe: water hammer (2-4 decaying knocks) from the plant room's insulated line (layout.pipes, xi / yi 2.8) or a point inside the ceiling collider (occluded, so muffled). ambDuct: 1-4 ticks queued on P10.pending from the wall grille (layout.vent, y 2.55); falls back to ambPipe. ambDoor: a thump 12-25 m beyond a side wall. All positional through soundAt (one pool voice each) at room-local points (rWX/rWZ).
- ambSwell: traffic outside, not positional (straight to audio.out + a little audio.verb): lowpassed brown noise (SPAT.brown = the room tone's buffer) swelling over 4-7 s (car) or 8-11 s with a 42 Hz sine (truck). Level by roomAcoustic: hall/range 1.5, small 0.6, stair 0 (no traffic), else 1. Weights pipe 0.3, duct 0.3, door 0.1, traffic 0.3.
- Never calls enemiesHear. Room tone, ballast hum and updateBuzz unchanged. ?dev adds AMB, ambTick, ambPipe, ambDuct, ambDoor, ambSwell.
## CQC step 86: his body foley (headset check needed)
- enemyBodyFoley(e, now) from enemiesUpdate after enemyMove: edges on clamp(crouchT + stumble) with hysteresis (>= 0.75 down, <= 0.5 up; e.fcLow). Knee down (cover on a half wall, reload, leg-hit stumble): knee pad, carrier, mags via P10.pending 0.2 s later (ebfKneel). Coming up to peek: cloth and a boot scuff at once, before the pose lifts his head. Pivot on the spot (e.turning, speed < 0.2, once per 0.9 s; e.fcTurnAt): soles on grit. Synth nBurst through soundAt (voice pool, occlusion, air). CONFIG.polish.audio.bodyFoley (gain, 0 = off).
- ?dev adds enemyBodyFoley. Not verified: how it sounds, Quest frame time.
## CQC step 86: barrel heat smoke (GUN_WISP, gunWisp; CONFIG.polish.wisp, ?wisp=off; headset check needed)
- fireGun adds g.heat per shot (impulse x pellets / 2.2, so a 9 mm = 1; x1.8 with a can), decaying over GUN_WISP.tau 9 s. updateGun calls gunWisp while heat > 0.02: past GUN_WISP.from 5, 0.35 s after the last shot, one rising grey particle (impact pool via fxEmit, lit by P10U, grav < 0) every 1/5..1/16 s from the muzzle or the can end, the ejection port 30 % (60 % slide locked); a moving gun emits more often so the trail stays continuous. Skipped holstered (g.slot), hidden, or behind the desktop viewmodel. No new draw, material or allocation (wispL cloned once per gun). Tune S.from (onset), S.tau (duration), S.a (density).

## CQC step 86: merged sensory pass (headset check needed)
- Seven additive branches merged into cqc-realism: body bumps, cloth foley, door foley, prop knocks, building ambience, enemy body foley, barrel heat smoke (each section above has its flag). Smoke-tested in headless Chromium: loads with no errors, every new sound function runs. Not verified: how they sound, the wisp on screen, Quest frame time.
- Not built, waiting for the user's OK (they change what exists): gun foley + brass back on, VR mechanism haptics, desktop view weight, enemy gun handling sounds, muffled life behind doors, shoot-out ceiling tubes, exit-sign green spill.

## CQC step 87: VR mechanism haptics (headset check needed)
- mechPulse(hand, k, ms) (beside pulse/pulseSoft): k >= 0.4 is a knock that cuts only a weaker running pulse (pulse() now stamps hand.hapK beside hapEnd); smaller k is a tick through pulseSoft. Desktop hands (no ctrl) skip it.
- rackBackEvent: rack hand 0.55, gun hand 0.25 (rear stop). finishRack: gun hand 0.75 (0.45 caught by the slide stop, 0.4 a bolt pushed home), rack hand 0.35 (0.5 bolt) while it still rides the slide. slideRelease: 0.7 only when it goes home. Pump: back 0.6 forend hand, forward 0.8 forend + 0.4 gun hand. Selector 0.18, magazine catch (gunButtonA) 0.28.
- Back in battery: fireGun (self-loading, not full auto, VR hand) sets g.batAt = now + 0.055, g.batK 0.3 (0.12 locked open); updateGun ticks it after the 45 ms shot pulse. No setTimeout, no sound, no allocations.
- CONFIG.polish.haptics { mech, cycle } (mech 0 = all off), ?mechhap=off = exactly the old haptics. ?dev: MECH_HAP, mechPulse, pulse.

## CQC step 87: gun foley and brass back, placed in the room; drops make a sound (headset check needed)
- CONFIG.polish.audio.foley { brass: 1, mech: 1, drops: 1, kit: 0 }: gain per group, 0 = off; all 0 or ?foley=off = shots only, exactly as before. GUN_HANDLING_SOUNDS stays false; true is still the all-on override. foleyK(name) gives a sound's group gain (FOLEY_GRP: tink / tinkBounce / thud = brass, holster / pouch / handle = kit (synth bursts only, so off), magLand / gunLand = drops, the rest = mech).
- sfx gate: shots always; anything else x foleyK. Handling sounds now take sfx's k (the bench's 0.3-0.8 soft clicks were ignored before).
- brassTink (updateCasings, replaces the head-locked sfx call; still before e.speed = sp): within 2.5 m head-locked as before (0.045 s apart); further, his brass or yours, first landing only, within 9 m, 0.12 s apart, through soundAt (distance, occlusion, air, speed of sound). Over 12 cm above roomFloorAt (a table or crate top; stair treads count as floor) it is quieter, lower and darker. BRASS_SND holds the knobs.
- dropKnock (stepPhysics, loose items with no holder, before the root is posed): fall speed from the root's step (no linvel), peak kept in p.vyPk; a landing = losing 65% of a fall over 1.4 m/s. Mags magLand, guns and grenades gunLand (pistols 1.15x, grenades 1.25x pitch), live shells the hull thud: new FOLEY bakes magLand / gunLand (LVL 0.55 / 0.8). Lights, lasers, cans, kits, injectors knock like a small can (KNOCK_SND.can, mass -> pitch and gain as knockSfx). Gloves / uniforms silent. Off the floor (the collider box's lowest point over 12 cm above the floor: table, crate, a body) duller. 0.15 s per item, 0.05 s overall, 12 m (impactRange). Positional through soundAt; the men do not hear it. DROP_SND holds the knobs.
- ?dev adds brassTink, dropKnock, BRASS_SND, DROP_SND, foleyK, FOLEY_GRP. State machine checked in Node with mocks (scratchpad s87/test.js). Not verified: how it sounds, voice-pool churn in a long full-auto fight, Quest frame time.

## CQC step 87: shoot-out tubes (CONFIG.polish.fixtures, ?fixtures=off; headset check needed)
- A round into a ceiling troffer's diffuser (bulletHit -> fixtureShot, ceiling normal down) kills it for the run (tr.out, room memory tOut, fixturesRestore on reactivation, nightApplyRoom keeps it dark): metal hole, metal sparks + glass glitter, 6 bouncing diffuser shards, 6 blue-white sparks, phosphor powder, pop + dying ballast + arc crackle + tinkle (again on the floor 0.6 s later), a 35 ms blue-white arc on the muzzle-pool light (fixArc; never over a muzzle flash, reach unchanged). At night (no power): no arc, no ballast.
- Only troffers[0..1] carry pool light: assignLights zeroes a dead one's spot + area light, the portal spot takes the nearest live tube, the failing-tube flicker leaves a dead troffers[1]. Room fill (environmentIntensity, flat fill, body cam gain) eases to fixtures.amb (0.35) by the share still lit (fixShare); particles follow (FIXJ.amb). Probes / env bakes are not redone.
- Housing rim hit: metal hole + stutter. Near miss (nearR 0.45 m): stutter, plaster hole as before. Frag / flashbang in your room (fixturesBlast): tubes within 7 m stutter 0.18-0.45 s, fittings shed dust (dead ones too). fixJoltTick after updateFlicker; a stutter left mid-way is put back. Safe room tubes never die (fixtures.safe).
- No light, material, shader or allocation per frame; impact and haze pools. ?dev: FIX_ON, FIXJ, fixtureShot, fixtureKill, fixJolt, fixJoltTick, fixShare, fixturesBlast, fixturesRestore. Checked: syntax, stubbed logic test (kill, near miss, rim, dead diffuser, jolt, blast, leave mid-stutter, restore). Not verified in a browser or on Quest.

## CQC step 88: enemy torch beam in haze (headset check needed)
- The nearest man's weapon light shows in smoke, impact dust and the dust motes at night, like your flashlight. p10Light has a second beam (uL2On / uL2Cos / uL2P / uL2D in P10U), 0.9x the first; the motes shader (its own inline beam) gets the same term, and the motes show while his light is on.
- updateParticleLight reads NIGHT.torches[0] (nightUpdate builds it nearest-first and runs before polishUpdate): on = intensity > 0, position and target.position (pool spots and targets are scene children, so world), cone cos(T.angle). No allocation. Not occluded by walls, like your own beam.
- CONFIG.polish.torchHaze (true), ?torchhaze=off: TORCH_HAZE is fixed at load; off, the three particle shaders get exactly the old text (checked against the step 87 build). On, they compile at load as before.
- ?dev adds TORCH_HAZE, P10U, P10_LIGHT_GLSL, NIGHT, updateParticleLight. Checked: syntax, shader text on/off. Not verified in a browser or on Quest.
## CQC step 88: dust sifting from fresh holes (headset check needed)
- trickleAdd(room, kind, p, n, delay) from bulletHit's static branch (after the hole and impactFx; the step 87 fixtureShot return stays first): a plaster / concrete ceiling (normal y < -0.7) or a wall over trickle.wall 1.4 m above the floor below it (roomFloorAt) takes a slot in TRICKLE, a fixed ring of 12 made once (the oldest goes). Held room-local (shiftWorld safe); freed when its room deactivates. Floors, wood, metal, glass never trickle. His rounds and frag fragments count too.
- trickleTick(dt) in polishUpdate after updateBloodPools: for 1.2-3 s each hole sheds 2-5 mm grains (TRICKLE_GRAIN, impact pool via fxEmit, grav 2.5 / drag 1.5 = ~1.7 m/s; a wall's spill out a little), rate 6 trickles a second at first (1-2 grains each), thinning and sputtering to none; one thin sinking veil (TRICKLE_HAZE, haze pool) after 0.15 s. Grain life from the hole's height, so they fade before the floor. Token bucket: at most trickle.cap 20 grains a second in all.
- trickleBlast(P) in fragBlast after fixturesBlast(P, 1): 6-8 static rays up (within ~42 deg), cover skipped, each hit hole starting 0.1-0.8 s later.
- CONFIG.polish.trickle { on, slots, time, rate, cap, wall }; ?trickle=off = exactly as before (no slots, no rays, no random draws). No light, material, shader or per-frame allocation. ?dev adds TRICKLE, TRICKLE_GRAIN, TRICKLE_HAZE, trickleAdd, trickleTick, trickleBlast.
- Checked: syntax, top-level names, mocked Node test (scratchpad s88/test.js: floor / low wall / wood refused, ring wraps, cap holds, frag rays delayed, deactivated room frees its slot, off = nothing). Not verified in a browser or on Quest: how it looks, frame time.
## CQC step 88: muzzle blast off nearby surfaces (headset check needed)
- muzzleBlast(o, fd, fx, g, now) from fireGun right after muzzleSmoke (your shots only): four static rays (P10_STATIC_RAY, own MBLAST.ray mutated in place) from the muzzle / can end: the bore, world down (skipped when the gun points down or is rolled flat), both sides of the gun. A hit within range (0.55 m) takes k = (1 - d / range)^2 x clamp(impulse x pellets / 2.2, 0, 1.5), a can x0.3; kind from colliderInfo (unknown = concrete); a muzzle inside the collider (no normal) uses -ray.
- Dust: grit (MBLAST_GRIT plaster / concrete / wood / metal grime) streaks along the surface the way the gas runs (bore + 0.4 ray, projected onto the surface, lifted 0.3 off it; head-on it sprays back out, cone 0.9), 3 + 5k per hit, strongest first, rationed by a token bucket (48 per s, 28 at once) so the 384-point impact pool keeps room for blood. The strongest hit adds a haze puff (MBLAST_HAZE, alpha 0.12k, at most every 0.12 s; headset haze is off by default, the grit carries it there).
- Slap: strongest hit only, at most every 0.06 s (full auto: every other shot at 1200 rpm): soundAt(point, mbSlapPlay, { occlude: false }) = nBurst highpass (MBLAST_HZ by kind: plaster 1200, wood 1000, concrete 1500, metal 1800) 0.2 x k x GUN_MIX.shot x slap. No earsHit / enemiesHear (the shot does both).
- CONFIG.polish.muzzleBlast { on, range, dust, slap } (dust / slap gains, 0 = off); ?mblast=off = exactly as before (not called). No light, no material, presets and vectors made once; per shot only Rapier's hit objects and the audio nodes.

## CQC step 88: gun touch (headset check needed)
- gunTouch(g, e, dt, P, Q, need) from gunClipWorld (before the offset update) and handTouch(vh, P, dt, out) from handClipWorld (after CL_d): the clip already kept held guns and empty hands out of walls, cover and heavy props in silence; now the first touch is heard and felt. CONFIG.polish.audio.gunTouch { gain (0 = off), min m/s, cool s, scrape, haptic, hand }, ?guntouch=off = exactly as before.
- Edge: need appears after a frame without it (g.gtc.on). Speed = the touching point's velocity into the surface from last frame's pose (muzzle when the push is back along the bore, else that side of the clip box), at least need / dt. Silent: over 8 m/s (snap turn, re-base), need over 15 cm, the gun still blending in from a pick-up, not held last frame.
- Surface: one intersectionWithShape (clipFree's args) at the unpushed pose at contact start: colliderInfo kind, door -> metal, prop -> surfaceOfProp. Knock: KNOCK_SND plaster / block / wood / can / glass + the gun's tick (KNOCK_SND.can 1.7x), g = clamp((v - min) / 1.5) x 0.5 (x0.6 on the computer), pistols 1.15x pitch; soundAt from the drawn gun's point at the surface, never occluded, through a named fn (gtPlay reads GT, no closure).
- Haptics (headset): mechPulse(gun hand, 0.2 + 0.4k, 18 ms; so haptics.mech 0 / ?mechhap=off also drop it), pulseSoft(support, 0.15, 14 ms). Scrape: while pressed and sliding over 0.3 m/s, a bandpass burst (1.3-3.2 kHz by surface, q 1.4) every 0.14-0.18 s straight to audio.out + a 0.06 pulseSoft.
- Hand pat: CL_d over 2 mm after a free frame, into it over min: KNOCK_SND by surface at 0.12 x (0.35 + 0.65k), pitch 0.8, from the stop point; pulseSoft(vh, 0.18, 12 ms).
- No AI noise, no light, no material, no per-frame allocation (g.gtc / vh.gth made once). ?dev adds GT_ON, GT, GT_SND, gunTouch, handTouch, gtKind. Checked: syntax, top-level names. Not verified in a browser, by ear or on Quest.
## CQC step 88: hinge creak (headset check needed)
- CONFIG.polish.audio.doors gains creak (gain, 0 = off) and creakShare (0.45); ?creak=off (CREAK.on false) = no bake, no creak; ?doorsnd=off silences it too. Off = exactly as before.
- doorCreak(door) at activateDoor: fixed per room slot (mulberry32 of roomSeed), creakShare of the doors get creak 0.6-1, the rest 0; own pitch crP 0.88-1.12. The same door sounds the same when you come back.
- updateDoorsPost (dt > 0): while a creaky leaf moves (0.08-1.4 rad/s, open past 0.05 rad) crA adds up the swing; past crN (random 0.22-0.34 rad) and 0.2 s since the last, doorSfx(door, 'creak', ws). A still leaf preloads crA to 0.12, so it creaks soon after it breaks away. At most 8 per full swing; a fling (over 1.4 rad/s) stays silent.
- doorSfx 'creak': at the hinge (doorHinge) 0.35 m under the head of the leaf (closer arm), through soundAt with a static fn (creakPlay); never the same variant twice running (CREAK.one / last), rate crP x (0.85-1.15 by speed) x +-4 %, gain 0.14 x creak x a fade-in from 0.08 rad/s, down 60 % for a fast swing, x 0.75-1.25.
- Bake (bakeSounds, last, so earlier seeds are unchanged): 4 variants of 0.45 s (2 groans 85-140 Hz, 2 squeals 300-460 Hz) from a new bakeFoley layer 'slip' (stick-slip pulse train with jitter, wobble and an uneven grip, ringing two resonances 480-640 / 1150-1380 Hz), the plan's two scrape bands as friction hiss (g 3 / 1.3, ~13 dB under), kickSfx's leaf partials (300 / 517 Hz) knocked at the start; lowpass 4 kHz; squeals x 0.6. Existing bakes untouched (the slip branch only runs for 'slip').
- No per-frame allocation, no light; the men do not hear it. ?dev adds CREAK, doorCreak, creakPlay, doors. ## CQC step 90: extreme wounds (headset check needed)
- CONFIG.gore.extreme (default on; ?gore=mild = the step 67 look; ?gore=off turns it off too). Knobs in CONFIG.gore.x; x.bodyCam (1.35) scales holes, pour, spray and bits with Body cam realism on (g90K).
- Deep holes in his own blood shader (enemyBloodMat + G90_GLSL; key enemyBlood90, still one program per variant, warmed by enemyPrecompile; uGoreX 0 = the old shader path). Per wound uBloodA (the round's line into him, hole radius, < 0 = exit) and uBloodT (a tangent on him, freshness), from w.la / w.lt / w.hole in bone space (g90Hole). The hole is a tube along the round's line, so it lands on his skin wherever the hit capsule put the centre (capsules sit up to ~4 cm outside the mesh; with uGoreX the stain is measured from the line too). Parallax: the view ray carries on inside the tube to the wall (red muscle at the rim, darker deeper, black-red bottom), torn wet margin, ragged edge; all noise in the wound's own frame (nothing swims). Entry: cloth dips in, frayed threads. Exit: wider, shallower, torn flesh pushed out over the cloth. Rivulets run down his clothes from each wound; fresh blood stays red; rim and inner wall bend the normal; wet roughness.
- Holes: entry 10.5-16.5 mm radius (by round), exit 22-56 mm (head x1.4). Pellets / a burst on one bone within 5 cm and 0.25 s merge into one crater (g90Merge in addWound, up to 70 mm, head 85). Rifles (impulse >= 2.9), the AWP and every head shot always go through (g90Through -> addExitWound). addWound / addExitWound now return the wound.
- Pouring (G90.S, x.streams 6, oldest replaced): each fresh wound pours; steady while he lives (x.pourAlive), heavier once down (x.pourDead), weakening over blood.bleedTime; spurts on his heartbeat for x.spurtTorso / x.spurtNeck s after a torso / neck hit. Drops are streaks in one instanced draw (G90.mesh, fixed pool x.drops / x.dropsHeadset 300, tissue bits in its first x.chunks slots, P10U lighting, no per-frame allocation). A walking man's drops keep his speed. Landing: a 'drip' mark where it is new (the spatter material in its own layer, not remembered, drop budget); where it keeps landing it grows the spreading pool there or starts a small one (poolAt).
- Exit side (g90Hit): G90_MIST / G90_DROP cones (impact pool), tissue bits (g90Chunks: stick where they cross the wall they were thrown at, one ray at spawn + one to confirm, or on the floor; 'gib' decals, x.gibBudget), a big 'spray' / 'sprayH' decal (canvas: wet core, high-velocity streaks, tissue clumps; head: a few bone flecks; normal map from thickness) on what is behind him with runs under it on a wall, and a second on the floor (g90Spray, once per blast per man). Head shots: biggest spray, more bits, the head pours, a pool under it 1.4 s later if he is down. No sound added. A body shot again (ragdollHit -> g90RagHit): exit found on his own ragdoll collider.
- Load: streak program in g90Precompile, spray / gib decal dummies in WEAPON.precompileDummies. No light, no shadow caster.
- ?dev: GORE_X, G90, G90U, g90K, g90Hit, g90RagHit, g90StreamOn, g90Update, g90Chunks, g90Spray, g90Merge, enemyShot, enemyRayTest, addWound, poolAt, P10, g90Sim(n) (1/72 s steps: dying update, physics, impact particles, streams, pools).
- Headless check (no screenshots, at the user's request): torso, head and buckshot hits, no page errors, holes / streams / drops / decals made. Not verified: how it looks, Quest frame time with 8 deep wounds on a man filling the view.
