Direct renderer.render + readPixels with an own camera shows the men; the normal desktop post path screenshots did not show them in SwiftShader (not investigated).
## CQC step 89: computer always shows the viewmodel arms
- On a computer (not XR, not touch) a held gun is never held by the procedural/VR arms. vmGun() no longer drops out for ADS, SCOPE.desk, DNADE or attachments. VM.byType: m4a4 / m4a1s -> ak, k98 -> sniper, g17 -> pistol2 (life-size hands, VMH_FIT solved there); legacy types mapped to ak / pistol2. vm-pistol / vm-makarov are loaded by nothing now.
- The pack's own gun is never drawn (its meshes get layer mask 0 in vmLoad; vmhBake still samples them). Your real gun is drawn instead: a mirror of g.prop.root (shared geometry and materials, synced every frame by vmMirSync: slide, bolt, trigger, magazine, flash, fitted light / laser / can, scope lens) under VM.holder, riding on the pack gun's node (VM.rigs[].gunNode: ak 'ak', pistol2 'BPpistol', sniper 'Body_057'). Placement = vmGunFit: L = N0^-1 x U^-1 x M^-1 at the bake's idle frame (M = VMH_FIT[type].R, or VM_FIT for awp / k98, solved with vmhRegister + vmhSolve on the sniper take; else the same solve at run time). The AWP now shows as the AWP (scope, thumbhole stock), not the pack's Kar98-like rifle.
- Reload: your magazine follows the pack's magNode, a bolt gun's bolt + shroud the boltNode (vmDelta / vmFollow); the dropped magazine stays as a ghost in the hand until the new one is seated. vmHideGun is stamped: a part that leaves the gun gets its layers back (a reload-dropped magazine used to stay invisible on the floor).
- Aiming: vmGunPlace eases (g.ads) the whole viewmodel until your gun sits exactly where the real gun is in the camera's view (deskGunPose: eye on the sight line, heft, recoil); VM.fovNow lerps to camera.fov, so the sights are true. A viewmodel reload eases ADS out (deskGunPose busy). SCOPE.desk: nothing drawn (vmRender skips), as before no arms there.
- Grenade (chosen fallback): the viewmodel is lowered out of view (VM.nadeT) while your procedural left hand throws as before; the right procedural hand stays hidden.
- While a rig loads or a fit solves: empty hands (vmWait), never your own arms; a rig that fails to load or fit falls back to the old hands. vmhTake skips desk guns the VM owns. ?vm=off restores the old behaviour.
- The light beam and laser still come from the real (hidden) gun in the room.
- ?dev adds VMH, VM_FIT, DNADE, vmGun, vmLoad, vmhBake, vmhRegister, vmhSolve, vmGunFit, vmGunPlace, vmMirSync, vmHideGun, vmWait.
- Checked headless (SwiftShader): no page errors; for akmn, m4a4, m4a1s, g17, m1911, k98, awp the VM shows with your gun's mirror, no pack-gun mesh drawn, real gun hidden, procedural hands hidden, at hip and aimed (eye at the view origin, bore -z, VM fov = camera fov; AWP aimed = SCOPE.desk). Not checked by eye: grip look per gun, reload magazine follow, grenade lowering.
- Step 98: the m4a4 and k98 entries are removed; the sniper rig is the AWP's alone.
## CQC step 91: wounded men fall, crawl and clutch; headshots drop dead (headset check needed)
- CONFIG.enemy.downed (on); false or ?downed=off = the old instant deaths (both halves below). DOWNED holds the knobs.
- A lethal round not to the head or neck: enemyDown. He is out of the fight at once (e.dead, plan.dead, loopEnemyDied: AI, squads, score and room clear count him as down), his gun drops, e.downed holds the rest. enemyOut (stills, plan.dead) is shared with enemyDie.
- Fall (downedFallPose): knees buckle then he pivots over them onto his front, or sits (legs IK to the planted feet) and falls back. The way is picked from 12 headings by clear nav floor along where his body will lie, head away from the round (downedFallPick). The first 0.26 s blends from his last pose; bodyFallSound lands with him. At landing the root moves under the pelvis (downedLand).
- Floor loop (downedTargets / downedArm on the DRIVER, so the hit capsules and enemySkinSync follow): postures prone / side / back / crawl, writhing noise, pain spasms, breathing, all scaled by what he has left. The nearest hand pressed on the wound (arm hit: that arm held across the belly, the other hand on it); the other hand braced by his head, pressing too, by his side, or reaching toward you. Crawls of 2-4 pulls (hands planted while the body is pulled past, legs trailing, smear decals, a small pool after) along a clear nav path (downedPath, pivots up to ~1 rad), DOWNED.crawl m in all.
- Step 80 moans and breaths while he lasts (e.moan.until follows his world clock; voxDyingTick takes the neck bone when there is no ragdoll). Bleed-out: DOWNED.time x blood.bleedTime (11-23 s); drips per wound meanwhile.
- Any further hit (round, fragment, kick, blast: enemyRayTest and the blast loop include men down) or the bleed-out: enemyDie from the current pose, limp (no muscle phase, no fall sound, no voice, no second score), pool after 0.3 s.
- Head / neck kill: limpStart (hips and chest drop 1.4 m/s, knees fold), the round's shove x0.35, RAG_SETTLE_LIMP (asleep in ~1.5-3 s). No grunt (neck too), moan or breath. The body-fall thud stays (the floor, not him).
- Cost: posed only for men down in the active, visible room, offscreenPoseHz off screen; state made once at the drop; no per-frame allocation. Contact shadows include men down.
- ?dev: DOWNED, downedOn, enemyDown, downedUpdate, downedApply, downedNext, limpStart, RAG_SETTLE_LIMP; VOX.n counts sounds started (grunt, dying, moan, fall).

## CQC step 92: underfoot (brass, glass, fresh blood) (headset check needed)
- underfoot(f) from bodyPre after ownStep: the sole's middle (as goreStep) finds active casings / hulls within CONFIG.polish.audio.underfoot.r (read from the pools' instance matrices, no Rapier calls), at most 2: one tink (baked tinkBounce, a hull the thud) + scuff, x foleyK('tink'); a drywall chunk, board or flap under the sole (its own box frame) knocks quietly (KNOCK_SND by KNOCK_MAT). kick > 0 also sends casings off along the step (ufKick, e.tinks = 2 for one more landing); default kick 0 = sound only, casings never move. ?underfoot=off / gain 0 = as before.
- His steps (updateFootsteps): ufHis tests brass within 0.25 m and glass under him; ufHisPlay plays inside his footstep's existing soundAt closure (no extra voice). Kicks only with kick > 0.
- Glass (CONFIG.polish.glass { on, size, crunch }, ?glass=off): propBreak (glass) and fixtureKill call glassFloor: ray down (P10_STATIC_RAY, floors only, not cover, as poolAt), decal kind 'glass' (polishKit: 256 px shard canvas, bloodNormalTex 1.2, roughness 0.08, env 0.9; the pool's features, precompile dummy added) saved with the room's holes; roomMem(room).glass [lx, ly, lz, r] (24 max) for the crunch: 3-5 KNOCK_SND.glass grains + 5 kHz hiss (glassCrunch), you not positional, him in his voice.
- Fresh blood (CONFIG.gore.wetStep, ?wetstep=off): goreFreshPool stores lx / lz / s1 in GORE.dry; bloodWet(room, lx, lz) = 1 in a spreading P10.pools entry, 1 -> 0 while GORE.dry dries it, 0 for restored pools. goreStep's in-a-pool branch plays wetSlap (lowpass 600, bandpass 1400, tack clicks); the print branch wetPeel (bandpass 2800, 0.03 x n / prints). Smears are not pools (silent, no prints), as before.
- ?dev: UF_ON, GLASS_Q, WET_ON, UF, underfoot, ufBrass, ufKick, ufHis, ufHisPlay, ufGlassAt, ufGlassOn, glassCrunch, glassFloor, glassCanvas, bloodWet, wetSlap, wetPeel. ## CQC step 93: floor wear and water (CONFIG.floorWear, ?wear=off; headset check needed)
- One non-repeating layer on every room floor finish (they share the 'floor' macro program; its cache key is macro-floor93 while on). FLOOR_WEAR.tex = generateMacro(min(floorWear.size 256, texSize.macro), floorWearShade) at load: R water field (~2 m), G detail (~30 cm), B lane breakup, A dust mottle; tiled every floorWear.period 10 m, each room starting elsewhere in it.
- floorWearAttr(geo, room) on the room's own floor plane only (roomBuilder): w93a = room-local x, z, half width, half depth; w93b = entry x, exit x, leak code + wetness x 0.99, seed 2..3. The shader acts only when w93b.w > 1.5, so stair treads, the warm-up quads and anything without the attributes (generic 0 / 1) are the old floor. Per room slot from roomSeed (mulberry32 ^ 0x93F1A7), so a room looks the same when you come back; room.wear93 = { wet, leak }.
- floorWearInject (from applyMacro): lane between the doors (segment entry -> exit, strongest within ~1.8 m of each door) roughness x (1 - traffic 0.32), 5 % darker; dust within ~0.5 m of the walls + dust 0.14 roughness, greyed (tint 0.3); water: f = R + detail + (1 - concrete AO) x 0.1 (pores and saw-cut joints fill first) against level 1.06 - 0.18 x wet; standing water = roughness 0.035, normal map flattened 92 %, 8 % darker; damp halo (13 % of the field) darkens by damp 0.38 and halves roughness; dried tide lines (2-3 cm, lighter, a touch rougher) where water stood (dry rooms keep old rings). Leak: under macroCeiling's stain (-0.34 w, +0.28 d): code 2 puddle (~0.6 m), 1 damp patch, 0 dried ring. Edges anti-aliased with fwidth.
- wetShare per room type (mechanical 0.8 ... records 0.2, safe 0); wet rooms 0.2-1. Coverage checked offline (scratchpad s93/cover.js, 9 x 10 m room): dry 0 % water, ~1 % rings; wet 0.6 about 4 % standing, wet 1 about 8-9 %; a leak puddle 1.2-1.8 %.
- Reflections in the water are the troffers' area lights and spots (analytic, correct parallax) plus the load-time environment. No light, no new program mid-game (compiled at load with the floor finishes), no per-frame work. Quest: same shader, two extra texture reads on floor pixels.
- ?dev adds FLOOR_WEAR, floorWearShade, floorWearAttr, floorWearInject. Headless Chromium: loads with no page errors, both room floors use macro-floor93. Not verified by eye on a real GPU or on Quest; painted floor lines stay dry over a puddle.

## CQC step 94: puddles react (CONFIG.splash, ?splash=off; headset check needed)
- CPU wetness: at load FLOOR_WEAR.cpu = the wear texture's bytes (FLOOR_WEAR.n px); floorWearAttr also stores hw, hd and the texture offset (s1, s2) in room.wear93. wearWetAt(room, lx, lz) repeats the shader's water maths (bilinear, clamp-to-edge like the texture, AO term as its mean): 0 dry, damp up to 0.4, standing water 0.45..1; SPLASH.pool / SPLASH.damp. splashWetAt(P) for a world floor point. No GPU reads.
- Steps: splashStep(f, k) from bodyPre after goreStep (the sole's middle); splashHis(e, k) in updateFootsteps (the boot beside his path, alternating; not in stairwells), its sound inside his footstep's soundAt voice. splashSnd by depth: standing water = slap, slosh, spray, drops falling back; damp = tacky peel (CONFIG.splash.gain). Standing water also throws droplets from the impact pool (fxEmit, SPLASH.drop, drops x depth).
- Wet prints: after standing water a boot prints on dry floor for splash.prints steps (damp patch over splash.damp: dampPrints), fainter each step. Decal kind 'wprint' (printCanvas(..., wet) dark grey, roughness 0.12; onBeforeCompile: the instance colour's green is its alpha, cache key wprint94, precompile dummy, layers start at 0 via splashInitLayer). Not saved with the room. splashTick (4 Hz, polishUpdate) dries each over splash.dry s x (0.4 + 0.6 x strength); a fixed 64-entry ring (SPLASH.dry).
- Bullets: bulletHit on a room floor ('concrete', normal up) into standing water -> splashShot: impactFx('water') (FX_KIND.water droplets, IMPACT_SND.water, no haze) plus a crown (SPLASH.crown); no hole, no trickle.
- No light, no new program mid-game, no per-frame allocation. ?dev adds SPLASH, wearWetAt, splashWetAt, splashSnd, splashPrint, splashFoot, splashStep, splashHis, splashShot, splashTick. Headless Chromium: no page errors, wprint compiled at load (no new program after walking), forced wet room reads pools, prints placed, a shot splashes. Note: the wear texture is clamp-to-edge (not tiled), so pool cover per room varies more than step 93's offline estimate. Not verified: how it sounds or looks, Quest frame time.

## CQC step 95: worn walls (CONFIG.wallWear, ?wallwear=off; headset check needed)
- The wing paints (upper and lower, applyMacro key 'wall') share one program, cache key macro-wall95 while on. WALL_WEAR.tex = generateMacro(min(wallWear.size 256, texSize.macro), wallWearShade) at load, set to repeat: R rub mottle (~1 x 0.5 m), G long scuff strokes, B chip grain (~4 cm), A vertical streak lines; tiled every wallWear.period 3 m, each room starting elsewhere in it.
- wallWearAttr(geo, room, side, yOff, doorU) on roomBuilder's four wall planes and four lower paint bands only: w95a = wall-local x, height, half length, doorway x (999 none); w95b = leak streak x, streak strength, room height x 10 + wear level, seed 2..3. The shader acts only when w95b.w > 1.5, so stairwells, columns, pilasters and the warm-up quads stay the old walls. wallWearRoom: per room slot from roomSeed (mulberry32 ^ 0x95A11E), level = wear[type] (hall 1 ... records 0.4, safe 0.15) x 0.75..1.1, so a room looks the same each visit; room.wear95.
- wallWearInject: rubs at hip (0.8-1.05 m) and shoulder (1.25-1.6 m), strongest within ~1.8 m of a doorway and in the room corners: darker (dark 0.16), roughness x (1 - gloss 0.55), orange peel flattened; the rest of the paint keeps its matte roughness. Low: kick-plate band to ~34 cm, scuff strokes, black boot marks (rubber sheen). Chips only beside the door reveals (outside the casing, below the head, more at cart height): bare matte concrete (roughness 0.95) with a dark edge. Water: faint vertical streaks on the left and front walls under macroCeiling's leak stain, strength by the stain's distance and step 93's leak code (puddle freshest).

## CQC step 96: eye adaptation (computer only; CONFIG.eyeAdapt, ?adapt=off)
- EYE / eyeLight / eyeAdaptPre / eyeAdaptPost (beside nightUpdate). Light estimate, no readback: L = NIGHT.env (the eased fill, already carries night mode and shot-out tubes via fixShare) x room kind (eyeAdapt.kind: safe 1.1, stair 0.85, hall/range 0.95). No per-shot input, so muzzle flashes and grenades never pump it.
- Adapted level chases L in log space: into the dark tau 1.1 s (murk, then opens up over ~3 s), into the light tau 0.25 s (short glare, settles in ~0.75 s). Multiplier exp(-s x lnA), s 0.55, held to 0.6..1.8.
- Applied as renderer.toneMappingExposure x m around the frame's draw only (eyeAdaptPre after bodyCamLivePre, eyeAdaptPost after vmRender), so the [ / ] setting, the terminal and saves see the user's own value. Off and reset in the headset and in body cam mode (BCX has its own auto exposure).
- Headless Chromium: loads with no page errors (safe room by day: m 0.96). Not verified: how it looks on a real screen.

## CQC step 97: furniture and prop wear (CONFIG.propWear, ?propwear=off; headset check needed)
- PROP_WEAR / propWearApply(mat, kind) chains onBeforeCompile and appends '|pw97-<kind>' to the cache key. Kinds (PW97_KINDS, #define PW97_KIND): paint = M.locker, M.cabinet, M.steelGrey, M.safetyYellow, the ammo can's olive, the jerry can's red; steel = M.steel; wood = M.wood, M.laminate; fabric = M.fabric; plastic = M.blackPlastic. Not M.plastic / M.redPaint / M.dark / M.vinyl (guns use them). Same materials, same count.
- PROP_WEAR.tex = generateMacro(min(propWear.size 256, texSize.macro), propWearShade) at load, repeat: R blotch (~25 cm), G hairline scratches (90 tiled segments), B fine grain (~1 cm), A vertical streak lines; period 0.8 m. One fetch per pixel (fabric two), projected on the object-space dominant axis in metres (modelMatrix scale), so the pattern stays on a moved prop.
- Seed per object: hash of the mesh's world origin + the shift sum (uPW97F.xyz, propWearSync from shiftWorld) in 1 m cells: stable per room, varies per cabinet; a carried prop takes a new seed when it crosses a cell. Height above your floor: uPW97F.w = level.floor (synced where it changes).
- Edges: curvature (fwidth(world normal) / fwidth(world position): rounded bevels, x0.75) and a box proxy: mesh() tags each geometry used with a wear material with its bounds (pw97c centre + flag 2, pw97h half size); untagged geometry reads the default (w 1) and uses curvature only.
- paint: edge chips to bare metal (metalness 1, roughness 0.34) with a rust rim, scratches to primer, rust streaks from the top on ~45 % of objects. steel: smudges, water spots, tea-stain streaks, polished edges, no dust. wood/laminate: top edges darkened and polished, light scratches, water rings. fabric: pilling (5x grain), stains, sheen on top. plastic: scratches, whitened edges. All: roughness x0.8..1.2, grime 0.65-1.45 m, dust on tops (more above 1.6 m).
- No light, no per-frame work, programs compiled at load with the kit. ?dev adds PROP_WEAR, PW97_KINDS, propWearShade, propWearTag, propWearSync, propWearApply, propWearInject.

## Studio: zoom to the computer screen (desktop only)
- addons/zoom.js (loaded after ui.js, `window.__studioZoom`): wraps `S.desktop.update`. While `desk.mode === "computer"` the camera eases (0.4 s, cubic in-out) to a pose square to `targets.screen` (the glass's +z side you came from), fov min(40, yours), distance so the monitor head's bezel (its meshes' extents in the screen plane) fits the window aspect with 4 % margin: letterboxed, never cropped. Your own pose is put back before the engine's update each frame, so yaw/pitch/walking are unchanged; leaving eases back. Something else moving the camera (Rally) wins; desk disabled or VR = no override, `sessionstart` snaps back.
- Clicks: on the computer the mouse drives the OS cursor (pointer lock, movementX/Y), not a ray, so the camera pose does not affect where clicks land.
- Leaving: Esc / losing the mouse (engine's exitComputer), or a walking key (WASD / arrows) the focused app does not use (`computer.physicalKey` wrapped: exits and starts walking).
- UI Settings, Display: "Zoom to the computer screen" (zoomScreen, default on, not in presets).

## Interstate: grabbable, adjustable mirrors (graphics.mirrorAdjust, ?mirradj=off; headset check needed)
- CarMirrors (§14.10, MIRADJ): once per body the mirror's housing and the model's own glass (triangles wholly inside a box about the glass) are moved out of the model meshes into their own meshes (same material and local frame; extracted copies follow their source's visibility) and hung on pivots: inner = ball joint behind/above the head (whole head swivels, ±0.45 yaw / ±0.3 pitch rad); door = glass pivot (±0.26 both) inside a housing that folds back 60° about a vertical hinge at its inboard end. The live reflection uses the turned frame (frames[id].M); a folded glass is not rendered.
- VR (CabinControls.vrHands -> mirrors.vrHand): squeeze on a glass: it follows the hand's twist and turns toward where the hand pulls; a door mirror's outer end or back folds it (detent click 'mirFold' and a tick at both ends; let go mid-way = swings to the nearer stop). Haptic tick at the aim limits.
- Computer (cockpit camera): centre dot; on a mirror the label "Adjust mirror"; drag with the left button (mouse captured) or hold E and move (E does not shift up while on a mirror; InputSystem.keyHook); F folds a door mirror. The view holds still while dragging.
- Saved per body in localStorage interstate.mirrors.v1 ({ body: { id: [yaw, pitch, fold] } }), restored when the body's mirrors are built. Setting off = rest pose (saved aim kept); ?mirradj=off = no split, no pivots, no hooks (the old fixed mirrors). mirrors.setAim(id, y, p, f) for tests. No per-frame allocation.
- Checked: syntax; one headless run (Lexus): housings split (6 parts), setAim turns the glass normal and world quaternion, VR grab + move turns the inner mirror and ticks at the limit, desktop hover "aim" -> drag changes it, values survive a reload and the next drive. The housing box was sized offline from the model data; not checked by eye, on the BMW or on Quest.

## CQC step 98: body position (VR + computer) and field of view (computer) (headset check needed)
- The headset cannot change its FOV (Quest shows ~48 deg below the view axis, real eyes ~70), so the body comes to the view instead. Measured (scratchpad s98/measure.js: headset branch of bodyPre, eyes 1.70 m, neck model nod, 48 deg down): the old body put the sternum 1.8 cm in front of / 35 cm below the eyes (upper chest 1 cm behind); it entered view at a 60 deg nod (upper chest 82.5, belly 55, belt frag pouch 45, thigh holster 50).
- Fix: bodyPre moves atlas and C7 by BODY.fwd along the hips' heading and BODY.up vertically, so the whole body and everything on its bones (hip holster, drop-leg pouch, caddy, RIG pouches, leg holster, med pouch, sling anchor, torso / thigh capsules) follow; the camera never moves. Default forward +8 cm: sternum 9.8 cm in front, in view at 37.5 deg (upper chest 45, belt pouch 37.5, thigh holster 45). A changed value resets BODY.init so the feet go back under you.
- COMFORT page (rows 50 px apart now): "Your body" row: forward -5..+15 cm, height -10..+10 cm (1 cm steps; +height past ~4 cm stretches the spine, legs are straight), Show body ON/OFF (off hides clothes, belt and boots; the bones still run, so holsters, pouches and injectors stay visible and grabbable; the step 13 cuffs return). Computer only, on the Brightness row: Field of view 60-100 deg (5 deg steps, default 72) -> camera.fov in updateDeskGunInput, never in XR; aiming keeps the old 72:58 ratio and the viewmodel's hip fov the 72:54 ratio (fovFor, tan space), so VM.fovNow follows. The scope view stays 72 / magnification.
- Saved in cqcvr.comfort.v1 (bodyFwd, bodyUp, bodyShow, fov; VIEW_FIT holds range / step / default). Near fade unchanged (0.045): at +8 the collar comes within 1.3 cm of the eyes only at a 90 deg nod and is dissolved there; it never enters the view otherwise.
- ?dev adds bodyPre, bodyArms, rigTick, comfort, VIEW_FIT, viewFitStep, fovFor, TERM, termDraw, updateDeskGunInput.
- Checked: syntax; headless (s98/smoke98b.js, menu frames then driven directly): no page errors, COMFORT draws all new buttons, presses save to storage and clamp (fov 60..100), comfort.fov 90 -> camera.fov 90 (aim 74.7, viewmodel hip 70.1; at 72 exactly 58 / 54), XR leaves camera.fov alone, Show body off hides BODY.root while all 6 RIG slots stay visible. - Removed: WEAPON_DEFS / X64 / X64BYMAG / MAG_DEFS / inv / LH_MAG_DOWN / cover energy / HAND_FIT / VM, VMH and VMH_FIT / VM_FIT entries, the M4A4 60 kit, the dust cover code (M4A4 only), the bench spawns, cqc/models/m4a4-glb.glb and k98-glb.glb. - Gun locker (GL, gunLockerSpawn / gunLockerRestock): open powder-coated steel cabinet (KIT.mat.cabinet) on the right wall's back corner (room-local x 3.975 back face, z -2.2, 1.3 x 0.45 x 1.85 m). Left bay: AKM, M4A1-S, AWP stand muzzle-up on a padded butt shelf, barrels in a barrel rest whose notches are cut to each gun's measured section at the rest height (10 cm under the shortest muzzle), pad-lined. Right bay: M1911 and G17 on a padded shelf, hung by the muzzle on pegs; magazine shelf below (two of each, standing). Number plates under each gun, ARMORY on the header. Geometry built once (GL.kit) and cloned per room; plate materials made once.
- Fit: glBounds measures the drawn vertices (index + draw range, visible meshes) in the item's frame; guns once per type (GL.fit), magazines per placement. Everything is kinematic at rest; grip / E / click take it as before. Colliders on room.body (back, sides, top, plinth, divider, butt shelf, both shelves; not the barrel rest) and one walk box, like the terminal.
- Restock: a slot whose gun type you carry (hand, holster, sling, vise) is skipped (never a second one); else the locker's own gun returns from wherever it lies, or a new one. Magazines: on the shelf stay, loose come back full, in use / gone are replaced. Guns and magazines are no longer on the benches (layout items keep the grenades).
- Checked headless: no page errors; drawn gaps 0.00 mm for every gun and magazine; soldier drop = m4a1s; restock keeps a carried AKM single, returns or replaces the rest; bench parts 7/7 after being taken. Note: the safe-room bench code (and wearSpawn) place items with origin + local (not rWX), so a turned safe room (f = -1) would misplace them; the locker uses room.group / room.body and is unaffected.

## CQC step 99: real gun handling recordings (CONFIG.polish.audio.handlingRec, ?hrec=off; headset check needed)
- Numbered 99: two other step 98 sections landed first. Sources (user uploads, raw in cqc/sounds/src/): freesound 675008 AK cocked 3x, 677160 Uzi empty reload, 855656 suppressed handgun (serutonin-deprivd), 142892 AK chambering (ceremonialchapstick), 184438 cash register (capslok), 762911 game pick-up (ienba), 369604 heavy coat sprinting foley (evanboyerman). Licences NOT verified: freesound.org does not resolve from the agent sandbox; check each page and credit any CC-BY (the files' own tags name the authors).
- tools/cut_user_sfx.py (ffmpeg + numpy, re-runnable): channels averaged, cut, tail trimmed 55 dB (pick-ups 45 dB) under the peak, fades 2 ms (coat 12 ms) / up to 40 ms, mono 44.1 kHz 128k mp3. Normalised per group (loudest peak -3.5 dBFS, relative levels kept); pick-ups and coat strides each to a 50 ms RMS (-17 / -19 dBFS). Labels from onsets / envelope / spectrum, not by ear: AK magazine out = the rattly rock-outs with a low thump onset, in = tick then snap 140 ms later.
- Files: akMagOut1-3, akMagIn1, akRackBack1-4, akRackFwd1-4, smgMagOut, smgMagIn, smgBoltBack, smgBoltFwd, pstMagOut / pstMagIn (the SMG's, short), supPistol1, buy, pickup1-9, cloth1-7.
- HREC (loaded with the shot recordings in bakeSounds; a missing file plays the synth): gunSfx(g, ev, synth) from seatMag, ejectMag, reloadMagTask, rackBackEvent, finishRack, slideRelease (vmReloadTask goes through these; a rack that runs back and home in one frame plays home 0.16 s later). HREC_SET per family: ak (akm, akmn), m4 (m4a1s, rifle: SMG magazine / knob, AK slam x1.12), smg (MPX), pistol (every cls pistol: magazines only, x1.1-1.2). Shotgun pump, AWP bolt, pistol slides stay synth. Variants are (file, rate), never the last one (hrecPick); each file brought to the synth bakes' level at load (hrecNorm, -12 dBFS loudest 50 ms; HREC_LVL keeps stop < slam). Gate: foleyK (mech, on by default) x handlingRec.gain.
- Suppressed: supShot from fireGun: pistols with g.sup, the FN 509 and the PPK play supPistol1 (SUP_VAR: rate 1 / 0.96 / 1.04 with shelves, never the same twice); rifles with g.sup keep sfx(d.sound, 0.28) and layer it at 0.78 rate, +6 dB low shelf, a small highpass crack. enemyShotSfx takes sup (W.sup) the same way from his muzzle; no enemy loadout carries a can today.
- His reloads (enemyReloadTick / enemyReloadDone on the reload countdown): magazine out at the start, in past 55 %, handle back + home at the end when he ran dry, from e.gunP via soundAt (handlingRec.his). Not the shotgun.
- pickSfx() on itemGrab for every item but guns and wear, and lootDesk (the pick-up takes are noisy hand grabs, flatness ~0.25, not a tonal blip). clothRec() replaces the synth cloth bursts (kept as the fallback): ownStep's running trousers layer, clothSettle's fold, clothRise, the pivot swish, the fast-arm sleeve, bodyBump's sleeve and the brush; taking gloves / a uniform at the locker. Boot, grit and kit-tick bursts stay synth.
- playBuy() (__cqcDev): 'buy' straight to audio.out (a two-note chime without the file); called by termRestock. The workshop store should call it.
- ?dev adds HREC, HREC_SET, HREC_FAM, HREC_LVL, SUP_VAR, baked, gunSfx, hrecPlay, hrecPick, hrecAny, clothRec, pickSfx, PICK_REC, CLOTH_REC, supPlay, supShot, enemyReloadSfx, enemyReloadTick, playBuy. Checked: module parse; headless Chromium (SwiftShader) ?dev: no page errors (favicon 404 only), all 36 files decode, every family / event picks a recording or the synth as planned, no repeats in 12 racks, playBuy / supPlay / supShot / clothRec / pickSfx / his reload play. Yaw from the chest, gravity hang. Long guns (AWP, Kar98k) tip up just enough to stay 10 cm off the floor (slcHangFor: visible-vertex box, once per gun). Sway: damped spring (anchor acceleration, turn lag; sub-stepped 4 ms, clamped, back stop = you), animation only. Body stays kinematic, COLL.item.
- Move (stowStart / stowAnim): from the gun's pose at let-go to the carry, held relative to the carry so it rides with you; smootherstep, sin arc out from you (holsters: out to the side); chest 0.42 s, holsters 0.34 s (x0.75-1 by distance). Draw: blend 0.15 s VR (grip settle), 0.22 s computer (g.blendDur). VR grip within 13 cm of a gun on the chest takes it (slungNear, after nearestProp).

## CQC step 98: no dark dip entering a room; laser beam in smoke (headset check needed)
- Cause of the dip: the failing-tube flicker (updateFlicker, combat rooms' troffers[1]) kept its clock only while a lit combat room was current. Leaving the safe room (or a dark / shot-out room) found flicker.next long past, so on the first frame in the new room its spot + area light dropped to 0.12-0.37 for 0.15-0.5 s (the headless log: main[1] 22 -> 3, rect 38 -> 5 one frame after entry, every time). Fix: assignLights arms the flicker when its tube changes room; updateFlicker then waits flicker.grace (6 s) + up to 6 s. The pool hand-over itself never darkens (the room you enter gains both spots + both area lights in the same frame).
- Also no brightness change on entry: body cam auto exposure ae.hunt 0 (was 0.3, dipped 40 % of entries), step 96 eye adaptation off by default (CONFIG.eyeAdapt.on false; it changed exposure by room kind).
- Laser (CONFIG.weapons.laserAir, ?laserbeam=off = the step 16 tube): one camera-facing ribbon per lit laser (LAIR, own ShaderMaterial copy per slot, one program, compiled at load), at least minPx wide (dimmer when thinner than a pixel), base 0.06 in clean air; every hazeEmit also logs a cloud in a ring of 16 (before the haze-off return, so the headset with haze off has it; polishShift moves them), laserAirPick sends the 6 densest along the beam (vec4 centre + radius, density) and the shader adds their gaussians (smoke 0.75, max 0.85). One pistol shot: ~0.36 in its cloud for ~2 s; a burst saturates. No light, no per-frame allocation.
- Red dot: CONFIG.nade.dotGain 1.4, dotCover 0.4 (premultiplied blend: its core hides 40 % of a bright wall, adds 1.4x the old red).
- ?dev adds pool, shadowSched, flicker, BCX, BCV, MENU, LASER, LAIR, laserAirAdd, laserAirPick. Headless note: drawing room 1 in SwiftShader gets the page OOM-killed in the shared memory cgroup; the probe skips the main draw (lights and exposure are set before it).

## CQC step 100: Glock 17 attachments (?g17att=off; headset check needed)
- Models: cqc/models/att/g17-att.glb (339 KB) by tools/pack_glock_att.py from the user's packs: dot = Hex Dragonfly class red dot (attachments_light_sup_scope "Scope.001", x0.76: 45.5 x 28.7 x 23 mm, turned to -z; lens split off as dotGlass, its flat rear face only), canMD3 (34 x 148 mm) and canSB100 (34 x 114 mm) from silencers_pack_1 (decals dropped), comp = weapon_accessories "Compensator 1" (22 x 48.5 mm, ports turned up). Baked vertices / normals / tangents, metres, origin on the mating face; base colour pressed to dark cerakote, roughness 0.45-0.60, metal 0.8 (0.7 the dot), normals kept, WebP (UV-cropped). The pack's flashlight (Olight light + laser combo, 102 mm, logo) was not used: the procedural X300 / stock PL-2 work with the switch and beam code. The pistol can on the bench is now the MD3 for every pistol.
- Built (G17A.kit, once): optic plate in a slide cut (2.1 mm in, 0.3 mm proud, 18.2 x 47 mm, ends 1.5 mm ahead of the rear sight), threaded barrel (1/2x28 lathe threads, 13 mm out of the slide; a tube inside the slide shows with the slide back), knurled thread protector (shown when no muzzle device), suppressor-height night sights over the factory ones (tops 21.5 mm over the bore = lower third of the dot window; tritium + rings), rubber optic cover from the dot's own bounds.
- Measured Glock (g17Measure, GLB design space): slide top 9.73 mm, front face z -130.41, top half width 10.29, rear sight z 44.2..52.0 / top 13.83, front sight z -120.2..-108.2 / top 13.85, rail underside -27.56 (over the clamp's length). WEAPON_DEFS.g17.eye y 0.01385 (was the M&P9's 0.0262). Dot window centre 23.94 mm over the bore (g.sightY: fireGun's zero and wr.eye follow it).
- Parents: plate, sights, dot, cover ride parts.slide; barrel, protector, cans, comp, rail devices on g.model (the barrel stays forward when the slide runs). Gaps (g17Gaps, drawn vertices / rays): dot on plate 0.00, can / comp rear face on the barrel shoulder 0.00 (13 mm of thread inside), light / laser mount 0.00 (0.53 at the rail grooves), sights 0.4 into the slide (dovetail), cover 1.2 mm wall clearance, dot rear 4.1 mm ahead of the rear sight.
- One build for every Glock: the store's fitted set. g17SyncGun (createGun, every store change, kit load) attaches / detaches att props (frozen like bench parts, kept in WB.atts); a vise RELEASE writes the gun's parts back (g17FromGun) and syncs the other Glocks. Bench: let go within 9 cm (rail parts 10 cm) of a part's place on the clamped Glock, or click it (computer): g17BenchFit snaps it there. g17Refuse: dot needs the plate, cans / comp the threaded barrel, one muzzle device, one rail device, cover needs a dot, a rifle can = wrong thread; dot / cover / comp refused on other guns. Owned Glock parts lie on the bench (WB_G17 spots via wbTable; a bought one appears at once). The stock PL-2 hides while a rail device is on. Bench 33-round card <-> store row g17.mag33.
- Store: new rows g17.sup2 (compact can), g17.comp; every Glock row has a model (no MODEL COMING left for the Glock); add-ons need their base fitted; footer "Effects apply when fitted: these are your Glock's numbers".
- GLOCK_FX (one table) -> g.afx: weight (deskHeft mass), recoil kick / climb / desk kick, flash scale + muzzle light (comp 1.35 / 1.25), report (sfx k, supPlay k: SB100 1.2), aim time (dot 0.85, MD3 1.12), ready / aimed sway (dot aimed 0.6), reload time (33-round 1.15: runTask k, viewmodel take timeScale), VR heft lag (1.45 s x sum m r^2, at most 20 ms; MD3 + light + dot ~14 ms). Store bars = g17Stats (wsBaseStats on the gun with the parts' weight and recoil, then the same multipliers); WS_ITEMS fx derived from it (g17DeriveFx). sfx now applies k to recorded / baked shots too (the documented x0.28 for rifle cans was being ignored).
- Red dot: ShaderMaterial on the lens (G17A.glass, premultiplied): per-eye view-space angle to the slide axis, 3 MOA (never under ~1.5 px), a small flare, coating tint; brightness G17A.lvl from eyeLight, eased (g17Tick). Covered = hidden. Desktop ADS puts the eye on the window centre; the viewmodel mirror draws it (same material).
- AI: nightCanSee also sees you when your held gun's laser is on within CONFIG.night.laserSee (16 m default). Holds (it.hold via holdPose): cans, comp, light, laser in the fist round the body (axis up), dot / cover pinched by the sides.
- Sounds (g17Sfx, synth, positional): thread ratchet 0.4 s + seat, rail clack + screw ticks, dot seat + screws, cover snap.
- Checked: module parse; headless (before the no-browser rule): no page errors, kit loads, store fits / removes on a live Glock, bench vise fits and refusals, RELEASE writes the build back, gaps above, a close-up render (dot centred in the window, sights co-witness). PLAY ran once with a dot + comp Glock in hand and aimed: no errors. Not tested: firing / recoil / reload timing in play, VR holds and bench snaps in a headset, the dot on Quest, sounds by ear, Quest frame time.
## CQC step 101: second batch of recordings (handlingRec / ?hrec=off; CONFIG.polish.audio.ambBed, ?ambbed=off; headset check needed)
- Sources (user uploads, raw in cqc/sounds/src/): freesound 121766 + 121765 Glock 17 reloads (kno-sfx), 486226 pistol dry fire (ruben_uitenweerde), 174058 M4 magazine table drop (pjkasinski3), 395541 button click (alayan), 712564 server room / data centre machine and vent noise (newlocknew). Licences NOT verified (freesound.org does not resolve from the sandbox); check each page and credit any CC-BY.
- tools/cut_user_sfx.py re-runs everything (step 99 files come out byte-identical). Labels from onsets / envelope / spectrum, not by ear. Glock: magazine out = release press + slide out (bright rattle, 6-6.5 kHz), in = last of the push + the catch (40-55 ms lead), glkMagTap = palm tap (low onset), glkSlideRel = single slam with a 1.3 kHz ring (reload 1, 3.49 s), rack = back lump then the forward slam 0.2 s later. Dry fire: 8 clicks across the take (several pistols), 4 reset ticks (25 dB under a click, 0.3-0.9 s after it). Mag drop: first hit + bounces / first hit + one bounce / the double hit at 0.43 s. Button: press, release, and each main click alone (the hum after 0.4 s left out). LOOPS: ambServer = 0.3-17.9 s, last 1.5 s crossfaded (equal power) into the first, padded 0.3 s each side with its own wrap, -20 dBFS RMS; period 16.099977 s, played from 0.3 to 16.399977 s (an MP3 start delay only shifts it inside periodic material).
- Files: glkMagOut1-2, glkMagIn1-2, glkMagTap, glkSlideRel, glkRackBack1-2, glkRackFwd1-2, dry1-8, dryReset1-4, magDrop1-3, btnClick1-4, ambServer. pstMagOut / pstMagIn are still cut but no longer loaded.
- Pistols (every cls 'pistol', and his): HREC_SET.pistol = the Glock: magOut, magIn (catch or tap), rackBack (rackBackEvent), rackFwd (finishRack), lockBack (finishRack onto the slide stop: the rear stop pitched up, lighter), slideRel (slideRelease: the slam, or a rack forward). HREC_ALT maps slideRel / lockBack to rackFwd for other families. PIST_RATE g17 1, M&P 0.96, FN 509 1.05, PPK 1.09, 1911 0.91; PIST_LS +1 / +2.5 dB low shelf for the PPK / 1911. vmReloadTask goes through seatMag / ejectMag / rackBackEvent / finishRack, so the desktop viewmodel reload plays them too.
- Dry fire: dryFireSfx(g) where the empty trigger broke (updateGun trigger block, was sfx('dry')): pistols x PIST_RATE, rifles 0.82 / AWP 0.76 (+3 dB shelf, lowpass 5.5 kHz), the pump 0.7 (3.8 kHz). dryResetSfx: a pistol's trigger released after a semi shot (g.rstArm), 0.07 level.
- Mag drop: dropKnock, kind 'mag' with hrecOn: magDrop1-3 (hrecAny), as recorded off the floor (table, crate: DROP_SND's off flag), on the floor x0.7, lowpass 3.6 kHz, +2 dB low shelf; pistol magazines (MAG_DEFS hold 'pistol') 1.15x, x0.8, cut to 0.3 s. Guns, grenades, shells, parts unchanged (synth bakes).
- Buttons (btnSfx(kind, pos), BTN_KIND): term (terminal + workshop glass, through soundAt from the screen; workshop refusals keep the low beep), vise (wbButtonPress and the empty-vise refusal, from the button), light (toggleGunLight: tail cap), stock (the Glock's PL-2), laser (pad: x1.32, softer, 7 kHz), sel (selector: x0.72, +4 dB). selSfx: AKM / AKM-N selector = a dry-fire click at 0.6 (steel clack), others the button. sfx('pMagIn' / 'pMagOut') called by name (rail light mount / unmount, bench parts sticking and hooking, the vise clamp / release) -> hrecSfx: the Glock magazine takes at 1.06. Gate: foleyK (mech) x handlingRec.gain; missing file = the synth.
- Room bed (AMB_BED, ambBedLoad from bakeSounds, ambBedTick from polishUpdate's audio block): one shared buffer, two looping slots crossfaded on a room change (tau 0.7 s). Each slot: lowpass -> gain -> 60 % through its own panner at the room's wall grille (layout.vent, y 2.55, room-local, refDistance 2.5, rolloff 0.6) + 40 % plain, + 0.25 to the room reverb; no vent / no spatial audio = all plain. Levels (x ambBed 0.1): mechanical 1 (16 kHz), records 0.55 (9 kHz), hall 0.3 (1.5 kHz), range 0.26, storage / office / lockers 0.22 (1.1 kHz), safe 0.12 (900 Hz), stair 0; x0.15 with the power out (nightDark). Room tone (SPAT.toneGain) ducks by 45 % x the room's k. Nodes made at the first tick with the buffer and ambBed > 0; ?ambbed=off = nothing loaded or made.
- ?dev adds PIST_RATE, PIST_LS, HREC_ALT, DRY_REC, DRY_RESET_REC, MDROP_REC, BTN_REC, BTN_KIND, HREC_SFX, dryFireSfx, dryResetSfx, btnSfx, selSfx, hrecSfx, AMB_BED, ambBedTick, ambBedTarget, ambBedVent, SPAT.
- Still synth: rifle / SMG / AK handling stays step 99 (AK and Uzi takes); shotgun pump and shell loading, AWP / bolt; gun, grenade, shell and part landings; holster / pouch / handle (kit group, off); selector on non-AK guns is a button pitched down (no selector recording); workshop refusal beep; pipe / duct / door / traffic ambience (step 86) and the room tone underneath the bed.
- Checked: module parse (node --check); all 30 new files decode (ffmpeg); every name in HREC_FILES exists. One headless Chromium run (?dev, SwiftShader): no page errors, all 29 handling files and the bed (16.70 s) decode, gunSfx every event x 9 guns, dryFireSfx (all classes), dryResetSfx, btnSfx (6 kinds, plain and positional), selSfx, sfx('pMagIn' / 'pMagOut') play (75 voices), no repeats in 12 picks; bed slots built (menu: no room, so level 0). Untested: the bed in a real room (vent panner, crossfade, duck, loop seam), mag drops through dropKnock, levels by ear, VR / Quest CPU.

## CQC step 102: AKM attachments (?akmatt=off; syntax parse only, not run; headset check needed)
- Models: cqc/models/att/akm-att.glb (1.2 MB) by tools/pack_akm_att.py (imports pack_glock_att.py's helpers): ats5 + ats5Sleeve (silencers_pack_1 ATS5, 42 x 197 mm), fh (flash_hiders_pack_1 mesh 0, the 4652-tri component, 22 x 56, prongs found by ring fill), comp ("Flashhider 2", 24 x 76), dot ("Sight 1", rail scale: channel 0.2549 units = 21.2 mm), holo ("Sight 3", 97 mm; channel cut back to the rail, jaws 5.6 mm deep), x2 ("Scope 1", rail scale; glass mesh dropped). Origins: devices' rear face; optics' clamp ceiling (rail top). Node extras: dot / holo window (hood opening by rays round the reticle decal: dot 20 x 17 mm, holo 29 x 20), x2 eyepiece (z 36.3, r 12.0) / objective (z -45.3, r 8.7) / axis 19.8 mm over the rail.
- Measured at load (akmMeasure, design space): wood handguard z -211.5..-376.2 (upper from -265.1), front sight block face -587.2, side rail flat x -30.0 (y -17.2..-3.0, z -90.0..11.1), dust cover top 38.0, receiver rear face 38.8 (outline +-17.15, y -38.1..3.1), band loop, wood stock side. akmPrepare splits the slant brake (triangles ahead of the block face) off the barrel mesh before any AKM is built and tags wood / stock / brake geometries.
- Built (AKMA.kit, once): kit = lower handguard with side + bottom MIL-STD-1913 rails and a smooth upper (top 45.0: a top rail would sit in the iron line), on the wood's span; RS Regulate style side mount (clamp face on the side rail's flat, lips over its chamfers, lever; bridge 2.5 mm over the dust cover; rail top 53.9). Collar (16 mm, 14x1 LH -> 5/8x24) on the block face: devices seat at -603.2 (MUZ base). Stock adapter on the receiver's rear outline, tube, castle nut, adjustable stock (butt 282), pad. Vertical grip (vfgProfile lathe), angled grip, hand stop: jaws cut to the rail section. Rubber covers behind the parts. Sling: 4 straps (band loop / handguard QD x wood / new stock), cosmetic.
- Places (akmLocal): light right rail z -300, laser left -330, VG -335, AFG -330, hand stop -362, dot -30, holo -70, 2x -41.3 (eyepiece 65 mm from the eye at z 60). Sight lines (g.sightY): dot 73.7, holo 92.6, 2x 73.7; irons 47.
- Progression: Standard AKM until akmn.kit is owned and FITTED (no longer hardware); kit, stock, covers, sling are built on the gun from the store (akmSyncGun, every AKM); optics, grips, light, laser also at the vise (akmBenchFit: snap within 10 cm / click, refusals: no kit, slot taken, Glock parts). Muzzle: step 103 chain kinds ats5 / akcomp (vents) / akfh (flash x0.3) in MUZ_DEV; first device on the AKM: owned muzzle interface, AKM or rifle devices only (akmMuzRefuse); AKM devices refused as first on other guns. Store muzzle row = the chain's first device (AKM_MUZ). RELEASE writes the parts back (akmFromGun). Owned parts lie on the bench (WB_AKM, left of the vise and x 3.0-3.12).
- Effects: AKM_FX -> g.afx (afxFrom, generic; VR lag k 0.12, max 45 ms); store bars akmStats (afxStats, generic) through the WS_REAL registry (wsStats, preview, footer, buy / fit messages); fx derived per row (akmDeriveFx). The collar counts only with a device on.
- Optics: dot (3 MOA) / holo (65 MOA ring + 1 MOA) reticle shaders on the window (akmReticleMat, Glock's brightness G17A.lvl); 2x picture in picture (akmPipTick: own camera at the objective, 13 deg, 256 px headset / 384 computer, only with an eye 1.5-25 cm behind the eyepiece; ring-and-dot reticle, exit pupil). Desktop ADS: g.wr.eye on each optic's line.
- Holds: VG = the step 23 foregrip (g.def = Object.create(akmn) with vfg); AFG / hand stop pin the support slide (g.supFix: supportZone and the desk hand). Loose parts: g17Hold fist / pinch. The viewmodel mirror shows it all (children of g.model).
- Shared edits (one line each): wbMakeAtt / muzMake / muzKind / muzIsDev (it.muz) / muzBase / muzRefresh / muzSync / muzBenchFit refusals / muzFx (counted, D.vent, D.flash) / wbAttRelease / wbDeskAtt / wbFreeze / wbClose / createGun / extToggle / boot / precompile / tick; wbSpotFits (restock + stow, ATS5 spot). ?akmatt=off: the gun as step 101 (store rows keep their new text).
- Gaps: every contact is placed on a measured face (0 mm by construction: clamp face, collar, adapter, rail jaws, optic ceilings, devices on the seat); handguard ends as the wood's (0.6 mm to the receiver). akmGaps(g) (?dev) measures them with rays: not run. Not verified: anything on screen, the 2x view, holds, Quest frame time.

- DOOR_REC / doorRecLoad (bakeSounds, beside ambBedLoad) / doorRecPick (never the same twice) / doorRecPlay. updateDoorsPost: a leaf leaving the frame (past 0.05 rad, shutArm not yet set, > 0.05 rad/s) = doorSfx 'open' at the hinge, gain 0.45-0.85 by speed, pitch door.crP; the step 88 synth creak waits while it plays (door.recUntil). doorSfx 'shut' plays a slam at the latch (0.3-0.8 by settling speed) instead of the synth when loaded. ## CQC: M16A1 at the vise
- Cause: WEAPON_DEFS.rifle (M16A1) had no def.phys, so the vise (wbJawPoint), the bench stick test (wbAttRelease) and createGun used M1911_PHYS: jaws at a pistol frame's z (by the M16's grip) and a 22 cm pick box near the grip, so no light / laser / can stuck to it.
- Fix: rifle phys (root space): receiver-to-flash-hider box, A1 stock, grip / magazine well; pick box over the whole gun (jaws under the front of the A1 handguard). M4A1-S / AKM-N / AWP keep their own phys. supTip finds the flash hider's prong tips (design z -0.765), so the rifle can seats there; light and laser go on with wbSnap like the AKM.
- Store: WS_GUNS.rifle (Muzzle, Tactical (multi), Magazine; factory 40-round magazine, so no extension kit), rows rifle.sup / rifle.light / rifle.laser (owned). Not in the gun locker: its three long-gun bays have barrel-rest notches cut to the AKM / M4A1-S / AWP. Only the syntax parse was run (no in-game test).

## CQC step 104: gloved hands all the time (GLOVE104, ?glove104=off; headset check needed)
- Cause: the step 98 glove was applied (gloves on, handsBuild + wearApply), but it is a shader on the procedural hand mesh (own shape, no texture); the real pack glove (textured skinned mesh) only showed via vmhTake on a gun, so empty hands, mags, items and doors looked different.
- Fix: glove104Start (end of handsBuild) loads vm-ak through vmLoad; glove104Hand cuts its arm SkinnedMesh at each wrist (vertices >= half weight on the wrist subtree, forearm share -> wrist), bakes it into the procedural mesh space (wrist frame as vmhRootM: wrist, forearm, index / pinky knuckles; cm x HANDLOOK.vmhScale) and adds it as a child of h.mesh (detached bind). The procedural mesh gets GLOVE104.hide (visible false) and only drives: per pack bone, rotation = procedural bone's mesh-space rotation (rest identity) x a fixed swing of the pack bind segment onto the procedural rest segment (thumb1-3 -> 1-3, point -> 4-6, middle -> 7-9, ring -> 10-12, pink -> 13-15; palm and tips follow their parent); pack lengths kept (FK). Written in the overridden skeleton.update (once per rendered frame), no allocation, one draw per hand. vmhTake hides h.mesh, so its child glove hides with it on a gun (the fitted hand shows, same material).
- Save: the gloves choice moved to gloves104 in cqc.wear.v1 (old gloves98: false saves start gloved); the locker still toggles (off = skin material, glove hidden, bare hands on guns too).
- Checked: module parse only (no browser run, at the user's request). Not verified: how it looks, thumb twist / finger alignment against the procedural poses, the glove program compiling on first show (not in handsPrecompile), Quest frame time.
## CQC: bench parts in turned safe rooms + bug sweep
- Cause of the floating bench parts / AKM 40 kit: world positions were made as origin + local, wrong in a turned safe room (f = -1, after a U-turn stairwell). New helpers rWP(room, lx, ly, lz, out) and rWQ(room, q) beside rWX. Fixed with rWP / rYaw: wbHome + wbRestock home check (table parts), wbButtonPos, vise pose (position and yaw), wbViseNear / wbTryVise / wbCandidate / away check, wear locker body + items, ext kit cards, respawn spot, safe-room layout items (grenades, m1911 / ext / x64 spawns). Things parented to room.group (vise, sign, button meshes, workshop computer, gun locker) were already right.
- Rack: no rack is spawned since step 88 (WB.rack null); its kit mesh has the back board and wbHookPose uses the rack's world matrix, so nothing to fix there.
- Sweep (partial, stopped for usage): no duplicate top-level declarations; door recording code read, no bug found; stale m4a4 / k98 only in dead data (VMH_FIT98.m4a4, WS_SKIP). Listener / timer and scope review not finished.
- Checked: syntax parse only.

## CQC step 103: screw-on muzzle chains (?muzchain=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Any muzzle device (MD3 / SB100 / rifle can, comp, new barrel extension) threads onto the front of the last one at the vise (VR: let go within 9 cm of the chain end; desktop: click), max 6 (MUZ.max). Chain = muzzle devices on the gun (g.atts + stuck in the vise) sorted along the bore (muzChain); no extra bookkeeping.
- Fit: rear face (per model, frontmost of each sector's rearmost vertex) on the previous front face (rearmost of each sector's frontmost, as supTip), 0 mm, coaxial (muzFace / muzPlace). Base: Glock barrel shoulder (G17A.M.front), else supTip. Extension (muzMakeExt, procedural, 100 mm, dark steel): knurled female collar, tube, shoulder at -88 mm (seat), 12 mm male 1/2x28. Table spot ['ext', 2.52, 2.9].
- Taking one off (grab / click) sends everything in front back to the table (muzOff / muzStow). Bench is a dispenser: a part threaded on frees its spot and a fresh one appears; a spare taken off is removed. Rifle can still refused directly on the Glock barrel; any device fits on a device front (adapters). The comp now fits every gun.
- Effects (muzFx on g.afx; non-Glock guns now get g.afx when a chain is on; Glock's first device stays GLOCK_FX): mass adds; inertia m lever^2 relative to the gun's (mass x (muzzle/2)^2) -> ads x(1+.07r), sway x(1+.09r), aim x(1+.05r), VR heft lag (cap 60 ms); kick by momentum, climb /(1+.1r). Comp vents (climb x0.7) only with no can/comp behind it, else a fifth. Extra cans: 0.75, 0.375, 0.19... of the first can's -11 dB (converges, never silent; rifle path now applies afx.snd too). Flash hidden by any can unless a comp is last (then x0.5).
- Muzzle point (g.wr.muzzle, flash position, muzzle light, smoke, wisp, gun touch) = chain tip (g.muzEnd); the round still starts in the chamber. Clip: gunFreeAt also tests g.muzClip, a box as long and wide as the chain.
- Saved per gun type in cqc.muzzle.v1 at RELEASE (muzSaveGun); createGun / G17A ready / g17SyncGun run muzSync. Glock store muzzle rows set the chain to that device (removing cuts the chain there); old saves migrate from the fitted row.
- Checked: syntax parse only (no browser, no Node maths run, per the user's time limit).
## CQC: glove hands 8 % smaller (CONFIG.handLook.extra 0.92)
- HANDLOOK.extra: vmhFit scales each fitted gun hand by it about its grip contact centre (vmhContacts cc) after the stored 0.9 fit, no new solve; the step 104 glove (glove104Hand sg) by the same factor about the wrist. 1 = as before. Not checked in the headset (fingertip reach on grips).

## CQC: VR buttons by fingertip and thumb tip (CONFIG.vrInput, ?tiponly=off; headset check needed)
- VRTIP (beside glove104Pose): terminal and workshop glass (glassFinger), watch face and its menu (watchTips, glassFinger), VISE button (viseTips), every gun light / laser switch incl. the Glock stock light (switchTips) are pressed only by the drawn hand's index tip or thumb tip. handTip: the step 104 glove's end joints (G.tipK, G.P from glove104Pose, now flagged G.posed) while the glove is drawn, else the procedural distal bone x its rest tip (also on a gun, under the fitted pack hand). Door levers are gripped, not pressed (unchanged).
- A tip counts on an empty hand while straight (fingerBend: index < idxExt 1.35 rad, so the Quest point with the grip squeezed counts, a finger on the trigger does not; thumb's outer joints < thumbExt 0.55: up off the controller). A hand on a gun: that gun's switches only, its thumb (no straightness test) or, as support hand, a straight index (tipMask). A grab disarms (a fitted hand landing on a switch never presses it).
- tipCross: skin = tip joint - pad 6 mm; fires once when it crosses the surface from in front over its face, re-arms only in front by rearm; never from behind, off the face or deeper than deep 3 cm. Glass: cursor follows the nearest tip within 3 cm, touch at the glass, re-arm 12 mm. Switches: 10 mm spheres. VISE cap: 3 cm radius, pushed 3 mm.
- The trigger presses nothing (the auto-injector keeps its own trigger: medTrigger). Haptic ticks and btnSfx unchanged; desktop E / click unchanged. tipOnly false = the old controller-tip points and trigger presses (glassCtrl, old branches kept).
- ?dev adds VRTIP, handTip, fingerBend, vrTips, tipMask, tipCross, glassFinger, switchNear, switchTips, viseTips, watchTips. Checked: module parse only. Not verified on Quest: tip accuracy against the drawn glove, thumb reach on a held gun (the fitted hand is fixed to the gun, so in practice the free hand presses).
## CQC: stair door phantom wall + respawn fix (headset check needed)
- Phantom wall: a U well rolls both doorways anywhere along its door wall, so they overlap in x about 4 times in 10. activateRoom split that wall into columns but gave a column only the first doorway (ops.find = the entry): going down the overlap was a solid collider + an lvTop walk box across the lower doorway (going up, across the upper one). Now every doorway in a column counts: solid only between and above the openings, no walk box where both floors are open.
- Same root, other checks: headPenetration (VR wall fade, desktop lean) and the men's move ignored bot / lvTop, so the box over a U well's low doorway, the box under its raised one and the walls of the wing above blacked out the doorframe (and pushed men in the room below). One rule, colOnFloor(col, fy), for resolveBody, headPenetration and enemyMove. A stair's entry plug walk box has bot = its sill (a raised entry plug walled the doorway under it).
- Respawn: respawnPlayer moved only x and z. After a U-turn the live safe room is often on the other floor (the one a stairwell back, while the new one waits to swap in), so you woke at your old height in the room above or below it. Now: the plan's safe room (frontier - safeBehind) if live, else the newest; level.floor = its floor + applyHeight, crouch cleared (desktop and VR), facing into it (rYaw: local -z; VR turns the rig about the head), then the head onto CONFIG.player.start via rWX / rWZ (right for turned rooms). The next frame's headRoom / updateFloor / vertical re-base take it from there.
- Checked: module parse; Node check of the column split (overlapping, apart, same x). Not run in a browser.

## Studio cleanup: unused props, files and dead code
- Found by reading and a scope analysis of the bundle (acorn + eslint-scope: module-level bindings with no reads, repeated until nothing new). Every cut is a whole top-level unit; the bundle re-parses as a module; `__vite__mapDeps` unchanged (handPreview, three.core, handModel: nothing added or removed).

## CQC step 107: suppressor glow and steam (CONFIG.polish.canHeat, ?canheat=off; headset check needed)
- HEAT (beside GUN_WISP, after gunWisp): heatShot from fireGun gives each round's gas heat (HEAT.E J: 9 mm 460, 5.56 2200, 7.62x39 2500, .338 5200) down the chain (heatChain: muzChain cached per parts change), nearest the barrel first; a can keeps take.sup 0.62 of what reaches it, a comp 0.25, an extension 0.12, so the first can heats most. Can temperature on the can item (it.hT, deg C over 20): C J/K + tau s (rifle 260 / 140, MD3 / pistol 150 / 110, SB100 120 / 95). Barrel g.hB (bore 0.4 x E; 80 J/K pistols, 420 long guns, AWP 900; tau 260 s), handguard g.hH follows 0.55 x the barrel (tau 45 s). Newton cooling. Rifle can: ~6 K a round, glows after ~90 rounds of full auto; pistol can ~2 K a round (very long strings only).
- canHeatTick (weaponsUpdate after g17Tick) runs only HEAT.list (hot guns and cans; dropped below 1.5 K). Past matAt 380 a can's tube gets its own material (heatGlowMat: clone of its shared one, onBeforeCompile chained, key +|can107; the desktop viewmodel mirror node is switched too); one uniform uCanH (deg C, 1 / length, patch seed, gain). Shader: blackbody from 450 (dull red 480, cherry 650, orange 800, exp2 rise), profile peaking at the blast baffles (t 0.2 of the length), cooler mount and front, value-noise patches. Programs warmed in weaponsPrecompile (canHeatPrecompile: rifle can, MD3 / SB100 when the Glock kit is loaded).
- Light: past 600 the hottest can drives pool.muzzle (the muzzle-flash point light) between flashes (pool.muzzleT <= 0), 0.35 cd at 850; off again when it cools. No new light.
- Particles (fxEmit, impact pool, own token bucket 24/s): shimmer (faint, quick, rising) from cans past 130, barrels past 90, long-gun handguards past 70; steam past 100, x(0.35 + 2 after a string of 30, fading over 8 s) x(1 + 2.5 wet). Wet = the room you stand in (wear93.wet x 0.5) or the floor under the muzzle within 0.6 m (wearWetAt), 4 Hz. Skipped 0.3 s after a shot, hidden, or behind the desktop viewmodel (glow still shows there).
- Water: a hot can (> 100) on a gun nobody holds, or loose, lying in standing water (SPLASH.pool, within R + 3.5 cm of the floor): quench (x0.7 at once, then tau 6 s), steam burst + haze puff, positional hiss + crackle.
- Sound: metal ticks while it cools (soundAt, within 4.5 m, interval by cooling rate), a soft hiss sometimes when wet.
- Hands: a hand on a can past touch 160 (grab test in itemGrab: a loose can or the gun where the hand closes; held: VR hands on the can each tick) is refused / let go (release) with a 110 ms full haptic sting; computer: toast "Hot!". Past 450 it burns: playerHit('body') for 5 -> 20 damage (450 -> 800), heatOuch = a VOX grunt take x1.07-1.15, not positional, at most every 1.5 s.
- No jam mechanic exists in the game; none added. ?dev adds HEAT, heatShot, canHeatTick, heatChain, heatGlowSet, heatGlowMat, heatWet, heatWater, heatQuench, heatBurn, heatBurnGrab, heatOuch, heatLight, canHeatPrecompile. Checked: module syntax parse only (no browser run).
## CQC: entry blackout + invisible enemy fix (headset check needed)
- Blackout cause 1: headPenetration (VR wall fade, desktop lean) skipped walk boxes only by top, not by floor like resolveBody. A U well's box over its low doorway (bot), the column under its upper doorway (lvTop) and a room stacked above (bot = its floor) counted as walls at head height, so walking through a U well's doors (or under a stacked room) faded the view fully black and back. Shared colOffLevel(col, fy) now filters resolveBody, headPenetration (level.floor) and enemyMove (his room's floor).
- Blackout cause 2: three.js drops a light whose parent chain is hidden or out of the scene; the flashlight rides g.model, so a lit gun in a room that hid (door shut behind you) or the desktop scope hiding the gun changed the light count = every program recompiled (long stall, dark on Quest). updateLightMount parks the light on the camera while the gun is hidden (lit only if in your hand, LMNT.hid); lightMountCheck() before flushShadows re-checks every frame.
- Blackout cause 3: tickSafe caught a frame error but drew nothing; the XR framebuffer is cleared each frame, so that frame showed black. Now it renders the scene anyway (FRAME_ERR.drawn) and runs the exposure posts.
- Checked, not changed: pool spots/rects/fill/muzzle are fixed scene children (light count constant), assignLights / flicker grace / fixJolt / nightUpdate / BCX hunt 0 / eye adaptation (computer only) as step 98 left them.
- Invisible enemy: enemyMove pushed men out of every walk box in x/z, including another floor's (stacked wings after a U well), which could shove him out through his own wall: drawn where nobody can see, still fighting. Now level-filtered, and e.pos is clamped inside his room (nav grid bounds). A man on another floor no longer pushes you. enemiesUpdate also freezes a man whose room group is not drawn or whose root left the scene. Skinned parts not in the first mesh's space no longer share its pelvis culling sphere (frustumCulled false). spawnEnemy returns null with no model loaded (no throw mid activateRoom).
- Checked: module parse; Node check of the head filter (U well doors and a room above no longer fade, own walls still do). Not run in a browser or on Quest.
## CQC step 106: gun finishes and camos (?paint=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Workshop store: FINISH ▸ (top right of a gun's page; ◂ PARTS back) opens the FINISH page: group tabs (each with its current finish chip), SOLID FINISHES / CAMO switch, a swatch grid, a big preview (roughness / metal, price), APPLY / RESET. Factory finish free; a finish costs the group's price ($50-150, walletSpend + playBuy). Hook: wsDraw calls paintGunPage instead of wsDrawGun, plus paintLive.
- GUN_FINISHES (one table: colour, roughness, metalness): Original, Graphite Black, Armor Black, FDE, OD Green, Sniper Grey, Tungsten, Burnt / Midnight Bronze, Magpul Stealth Grey, Coyote Tan, Olive Drab, Robin's Egg Blue, Crimson, Bright White, Stainless (bare); stains Natural oil / Walnut / Dark only on wood groups (paint too).
- GUN_PAINT_GROUPS (measured from the GLBs / specs): g17 frame (controls are in the frame mesh), slide; m1911 frame (+ the walnut grips: one texture), slide, controls (hammer, trigger, slide stop, catch), barrel; M16A1 upper + lower (one material, anodized), handguard / grip / stock (furniture, stipple), barrel (blued); AKM receiver + dust cover, handguard (wood), stock (wood), barrel + gas block; AWP stock and action (sniper atlas), bolt (part), scope; M4A1-S one texture. Every gun with groups also has ATTACHMENTS. A mesh takes the first group naming its model part (geometry -> M1911 / EXTG / X64 part key), else its material (GLB name or WM key).
- Camos (same table, camo rows, $200-600 per group): Holographic (thin film n 1.5 by view angle, rainbow glitter flakes), 24k Gold / Chrome / Brushed stainless (polished metal, brushing along the bore fading below a pixel, micro-scratches, edges rubbed bright), Candy pink and Midnight candy blue (tint deeper at grazing angles, metal flake glints), Pink tiger and Tiger stripe (tapered strokes along the bore), Blackout (satin worn edges), Arctic white (grime in the recesses), Rusty (paint flaking to steel, rust through edges / recesses / noise, pits; rough orange-brown), Woodland, Desert digital (3.6 mm pixels), Carbon fibre (2x2 twill, 3 mm tows, resin), Damascus (folded etched layers), Splinter (shards + rain). One injection for all (paintCamoInject: PAINT_CAMO_VERT / _PARS / _APPLY after metalnessmap_fragment, before step 17's edge wear) on every clone; uniforms per gun type and group (uCamoK kind, 0 = solid; 4 colours, size, bore stretch, knobs; seed per gun type). Edges from curvature (fwidth of the object normal), recesses from the baked map. Flat patterns triplanar in object space (side / top planes keep u along the bore), others solid 3D noise. Part detail kept: for a camo the clone's colour / roughness are 1 / the copies' means, so the shader multiplies the part's own wear, AO and roughness under the pattern.
- Swatches: each camo rendered once with the same injection on a curved strip of gun side (paintPreview: key light + the room environment, 192 x 96 sRGB target, read back to a canvas; one preview program).
- Apply: one clone per gun type, group and factory material (paintFor), shared by every gun of the type; onBeforeCompile / default attributes copied plus the camo injection: one program variant per source program (key + '|camo106'), compiled when a finish first shows (a hitch then), never per finish or camo. Textured: a grey copy of the colour map (luminance / mean, wear and grain kept, its linear mean divides the colour) and an ORM copy (AO, roughness detail around 0.5, metal 1) take the finish's numbers; copies cached per source texture (1024 px, 512 in the headset). Unreadable map = the finish tints it.
- Guns: createGun -> paintGunInit (meshes by group, before the magazine goes in); hand, holster, locker, vise and the viewmodel mirror (vmMirSync now follows the source's material). His drops keep the factory finish (PAINT.drops false). Attachments: wbFreeze paints the fitted part's opaque, non-emissive, non-chrome, non-rubber materials with the gun's ATTACHMENTS finish, wbUnfreeze puts its own back (loose or in the open vise: factory).
- Live: a touched finish shows on every gun of that type (the one in the vise too) until APPLY, another gun, ◂ PARTS, the screen sleeps or the room unloads. Saved in cqc.paint.v1 { fin: { type: { group: finish or camo id } }, grp }, restored at load.
- Checked: JS syntax parse only (the GLSL is not compiled anywhere yet). Not checked: the camo shader compiling, the patterns' scale and look, colours and the grey-map look on the GLB textures, the M16A1 / M4A1-S tabs next to the other jobs' new rows, Quest cost of the first map copies (~1 M pixels each, once per texture).

## Studio: office props removed
- Goal: an empty office for the user's own guns (target / bird shooting later). About 9,076 KB less on disk (models 8,727 KB, sfx 228 KB, armory.js 46 KB, chair mesh textures 38 KB, bundle 33 KB, registry 3 KB).
- Removed from the room (`assets/index-B2A-NN9Y.js`, office builder `ss` and start-up `Mm`): task chair (+ 4 colliders), sideboard / credenza (+ collider), art print, wall socket, all cables, mug, notebook, pen (their physics props and contact shadows), desk lamp, potted plant, wall clock (GLTF models and the clock-hands tick), the desk pistol (P38 model, prop, controller, ballistics / sound / flash / casing / bullet-hole system `Vs`, `__pistolSpec`, `__cartridges`). Whole top-level units cut: builders `Jo $o es ts rs Qo is`, prop descriptors `Ns Ps Fs Is`, `tp np rp`, `Vs` and its texture helpers `Us Ws Gs Ks qs Hs`, `Rs zs Bs ep`, contact shadows `Co`, the embedded-GLTF loader `as os`, cable / profile helpers `zt Vt Ht`, chair size `qo`, four unused three imports; then a scope scan (acorn + eslint-scope) until no unreferenced module binding was left. Entity names for them removed from `ac` (Outliner).

- Armory chain: Props app group "Armory" gone; commands attach / detach / strip_pistol / pistol_accessory, the "attachment" enum and the 5 attachment prop words removed from `tools/make_registry.py`, `commands.json` regenerated (126 commands, 112 ready). The planned shooting-target commands stay. project.js no longer waits for the armory and deletes `studio.pistol.mods` once.
- Old autosaves: ops naming removed things (trs on "mug", spawns of "suppressor", copies of the pistol...) are refused one by one by the engine's load (unknown entity / "unknown prop") and counted as skipped; nothing throws.
- Stays: the room shell, window glazing, terrace, door (Rally / Interstate / CQC destinations), lights (Sun, window skylight, downlights), and the computer workstation: desk (with its colliders and AO), monitor (Game Editor, apps, door menu on its screen), felt desk pad, keyboard, mouse, PC tower. Training enemies, the prop library, wrist tablet, UI all unchanged.
