Script `tone()` keeps working: the gameplay runtime gets its own AudioListener (it used the pistol effects' one).
- Checked: `node --check` on the bundle and every add-on, the bundle parses as a module with no unreferenced top-level bindings or imports, `__vite__mapDeps` unchanged, every path index.html, the bundle (textures, env, chunks) and the add-ons load exists. Not run in a browser (the user tests).
## CQC step 109: viewmodel sleeves in VR (CONFIG.body.vmSleeves, ?vmsleeves=off; headset check needed)
- Your arms wear vm-ak's own sleeve (the computer view's tac top), one SkinnedMesh per arm on BODY.skel; the shirt draws its torso only (bodyBuildShirt returns the torso's index count, setDrawRange). ?vmsleeves=off or a failed load = the procedural sleeves.
- vmSleeveGeo (once, after bodyBuild, the glove's cached vmLoad): vertices under half on the wrist subtree (the step 104 cut's complement) + the glove side of the triangles across the cut (in neither half before; the old sleeve hid the gap), those exactly where the glove has them. Fit = vmPackWrist (glove104's wrist frame, now shared). Pose: LBS on the pack's arm / elbow / wrist weights: the bind's bend plane (31 deg off) turned onto the body's hinge (front = -x right / +x left in arm space), spread over the forearm from 0 at the cuff; upper arm swung straight about the elbow; segments stretched to BARM (forearm x0.96, upper x1.14), so the elbow is the IK elbow. Skinned by sleeveKnots (the old sleeve's knots, now shared): the body bones are the pack bones with the swing baked in rest.
- Girth: the pack's (mean radius over the old sleeve: wrist 1.07, mid forearm 1.20, elbow / upper arm 1.27) x vmSleeveGirth 1.05 (clamped 1..1.08), faded in over 6 cm above the cuff. ARMFIT.foreR and ARM_STOCK.foreR / upR are scaled by those ratios at load.
- vmSleeveTop: the pack sleeve ends ~8 cm under the shoulder joint; its open top ring (24 edges) is carried on (6 rings) and closed in the old sleeve's flattened cap (5 rings, 0.62 R) inside the torso. Cap UVs: per column the fabric's gradient walked down the sleeve through index neighbours (never across a UV seam), smoothed along the ring, held 3 cm past the shoulder line.
- Material: clone of the pack arm material (normal map; normalScale.y flipped as GLTFLoader does without vertex tangents), DoubleSide, inside shaded as cloth, dithered near fade at max(body.near 0.045, armNearDesk on a computer), program key vmsleeve109, compileAsync at build. Colour = vmArmColor (vmTint's tint, now shared) in wearApply. Bare hands: same sleeve.
- Checked: module parse; Node run of the game's own vmSleeveGeo / vmSleeveTop on vm-ak.glb (three r170, scratchpad s109/test.mjs): finite, weights sum 1, no directed edge twice, faces outward, normals agree, 29 / 31 cut vertices shared with the glove, cap UV edges within the pack's, cuff on f2, cap on shH, cap top y 1.499 (old 1.491). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, the watch strap (it sits on the old cuff's radius; the pack cuff is ~3 mm wider underneath), clipping when aiming.
- Found, not changed: glove104 and the vmhBake gun hands drop the pack's tangents but keep normalScale.y as loaded (GLTFLoader flips it only when tangents are missing), so their normal maps' green may read inverted.

## CQC: restart coat rack + bug sweep 2
- Coat rack (uniforms on hangers + gloves, and the bench kit cards): one global set each (WEAR.items, EXT.items). A new safe room is built while the old one is still live, so wearSpawn / extKitSpawn found the old set "in the world" and skipped: every other safe room had an empty locker and no kit cards, and a set stayed where another room had put it (before the rWP fix, mirrored in a turned room: in front of the terminal). Now safeSetHere / safeSetClear: the set belongs to WEAR.room / EXT.room; at activation, on entering, on respawn / restart and new run (safeRestock) it is removed and made again in that safe room (kept only while you stand in the old one), its own geometry / materials / canvases freed (wearFree, extFree; the locker's cabinet, rail and PERSONAL plate now go with the room's ownGeo / ownMat).
- Sweep: desktop B at the vise measured from origin + local (wrong in a turned safe room) -> rWX / rWZ. heatChain kept a stale chain when a device was swapped for one of the same count (heat went into a can on the table) -> heatChainOn checks each is still on the gun. lightMountCheck re-ran updateLightMount every frame while the light was parked (two arrays + a closure a frame) -> only on a change (lmInHand). watchUpdate made a Date every frame -> once a second.
- Checked, no change: muzSync runs twice at createGun for the Glock / AKM (idempotent); m4a4 / k98 only in dead data (VMH_FIT98, WS_SKIP); colOffLevel is colOnFloor negated (one rule); no handlers or timers added twice in steps 98-109. Known, left: a can that got hot keeps its heat material, so a later finish change does not show on it.
- Checked: syntax parse; Node checks of safeSetHere / safeSetClear and heatChainOn (scratchpad sw2/). Not run in a browser.
## CQC step 111: downed men crawl farther and live longer (headset check needed)
- DOWNED knobs: life [60, 120] s x lifeK (chest 0.7, spine / pelvis 1.15, legs 1.3, arms 1.2; was 0.5-1.05 x bleedTime = 11-23 s), start [1, 2] s to the first pull, crawl [6, 15] m in all, speed [0.22, 0.38] m/s weak .. strong (downedPeriod sets the arm cycle from it; stroke 0.24, reach 0.38), pulls [3, 7] a bout, rest [5, 1.2] s between bouts (short rests on his front), crawlVig 0.25, turn 0.9 rad/s, smearGap [0.16, 0.3] m, prints 30, away (true). DOWNED.time / period removed. ?downed=off unchanged.
- Way (downedRoute / downedGoal): an A* path (findPath, room-local) to a cover spot hidden from you (coverPoints normal facing away from you, +2) or a free point away from you, within what he has left; else the old straight-on downedPath. downedStep (shared): heads for the furthest waypoint in a clear line (navLine), slower in sharp turns, pivots while his head (0.6 m, or the waypoint) is blocked (2.5 s, then the bout ends), slides along a grazed edge; the last waypoint is where his head stops. Failures (D.stuck) fade while he moves; 4 = no more crawling. Inside his room only (the nav grid closes doorways).
- Trail: downedSmear (room-local, downedMark: putDecal + room memory, no world conversion) every smearGap (fresh .. bled out, never under gore.smearStep), smear1 while wet > 0.6 (left x wound freshness) else smear2, smaller as he weakens; up to gore.smearMax with the body's later smears. Hand prints: new decal kinds hprintL / hprintR (handPrintCanvas, the boot prints' material features, precompiled), at each planted hand (downedArm) or each pull offscreen (downedPlants), the wound hand 90 %, the other 40 %, x (0.3 + 0.7 left), DOWNED.prints a man. Drips (downedBleed) x (0.15 + 0.85 left^2). Pools: a long rest or his last stop (downedPoolSoon).
- Away (room not visible / not current, floor phase): downedAway: same clock, decisions, path, trail and prints, root at his average pull speed; no pose, skin, drips or voice (his moan clock follows). Back in view: downedSnap poses him at once (targets taken, hands put down, no reach from stale points). Bled out away: posed, then enemyDie (limp, pool).
- Unloaded: enemiesRoomUnloaded -> downedSave (roomMem(room).down[idx]: room-local place, heading, clock, bout, path, wounds by bone name incl. step 90 hole frames, smears / prints counts, time). enemiesRoomActivated -> downedRestore for a dead plan slot with a record: spawnEnemy (same look), gun removed, no score / drop / voice start; caught up in 0.2 s steps for the time away (decals laid live into the rebuilt room and its memory), then posed; bled out meanwhile = limp at the end of his trail, the pools finished at once. The record is used once (a body after that goes with the room, as any body).
- ?dev adds downedAway, downedSnap, downedSave, downedRestore, downedGoal, downedStep, downedMark, downedSmear, downedPrint, downedLife.
- Checked: module parse; Node test with the real decision / path / trail code and stubs (scratchpad s111/test.js: 8 x 10 m room with a crate, you behind him): first pull 1.3 s after landing, ~12 m in 9-12 bouts at 0.30-0.33 m/s while crawling, never on a blocked cell, ends ~7 m farther from you, ~57 smears (heavy then thin), ~25 hand prints, all in room memory, dies at his time; the save record survives JSON. Not run in a browser or on Quest: the posed crawl at the new speed, prints / smears by eye, restore after a rebuild.
## CQC step 108: M1911 attachments (?m1911att=off; headset check needed)
- Same shape as step 100 (the Glock): M1911A kit, M1911_FX -> g.afx, bench fit / refuse, store rows with models, one build for every 1911 (m19SyncGun; vise RELEASE -> m19FromGun), holds (g17Hold), viewmodel mirror (generic), synth fit sounds (m19Sfx: the Glock's + grip screws, sight drift, magazine). Needs the Glock kit (G17A): the red dot, its reticle glass (per eye, auto brightness) and the cover are reused.
- Models: cqc/models/att/m1911-att.glb (109 KB) by tools/pack_1911_att.py (imports pack_glock_att.py helpers): can45 = weapon_accessories "Silencer 1" at 36 x 155 mm, front bore opened to 12.8 mm, FDE cerakote, collar black (+ a dark bore disc in the game); comp45 = "Compensator 4" at 24 mm, length x0.55 -> 50.5 mm, 11.9 mm bore, black nitride. Origins on the rear face.
- Measured (m19Measure, GLB design space): slide crown 11.53 mm over the bore (round top: 9.49 at 6.5 mm out, 6.55 at 9.4), GI rear blade z -19.3..-17.8 top 15.21, front blade z -184.1..-177.3 top 14.44, slide nose -190.65, barrel crown -191.39 (OD r 7.78), dust cover z -145.5 forward of the guard, bottom -26.43, half width 9.45, trigger guard front edge sliced into M19_GUARD.
- Built: optic plate 17 x 47 mm in a pocket (flat walls from 0.3 mm over the crown down 4.6 mm into it, 1.5 mm ahead of the rear sight; dot rear 3.6 mm clear of the rear sight block); .578x28 threaded barrel on parts.barrel (2 mm band, shoulder z -193.39, 12.7 mm of thread, .45 bore) + knurled protector; clamp-on rail (the user's Beretta 92X idea): cheeks on the dust cover with cap screws, body 3 mm under it, 5-slot Picatinny z -159..-105.5 (mount face y -29.43; 0.15 mm inside the X300 / DBAL's own rail section), strap round the guard's front 0.4 mm off with its screw; suppressor-height tritium sights (tops on the lower third of the dot window) or standard-height night sights (9 mm rear dovetail block over the 1.5 mm GI blade, front post).
- Devices on the rail: X300 origin z -100 (switch block 1.8 mm clear of the guard), DBAL z -127 (battery cap 1.7 mm clear). No rail = refused (no more sticking a light anywhere on a GI frame).
- Muzzle chain (step 103): base = the shoulder read through the barrel's pose (m19Base); 1911 devices are parented to parts.barrel (m19FreezeParent), so they ride the barrel like a real 1911's. Kinds can45 / comp45 (MUZ_DEV, muzKind via it.k45, muzMake). Only .45 devices on the 1911 (9 mm / 5.56 / extension: bore too small); a .45 device is never the first on another gun. MUZ.saved.m1911 keeps .45 kinds only. Bench spots variant-matched (wbSpotFits / wbVar).
- Grip panels: the factory walnut is 152 frame triangles (three UV rects, all corners inside one: exact match with the texture's brown). A custom look is an overlay mesh on those triangles (polygon offset -1/-2, shares the frame's position/normal, own uv1 in mm along the screw line), child of the frame mesh. Looks (M19_GRIPS): walnut double-diamond, cocobolo, black G10, OD G10, ivory polymer. Maps generated per look on first fit (256 x 1024 DataTextures, channel 1): height -> normal (v slope x kV for three's derivative TBN), checkering cut to the surface, border line 2.25 mm in, double diamonds round the screws (t +-40.2 mm), bevelled edges (flat last 0.4 mm), G10 laminate bands on the slopes. One program for all looks, precompiled with a 2 x 2 proto.

No MODEL COMING left on the 1911. wsStats / preview / footer / buy and fit messages know the 1911.

One wbSpotFits (wbVar: AKM key, .45, sb100); muzKind knows it.k45 and it.muz; the chain refusal is akmMuzRefuse || m19MuzRefuse; saved chains are re-read once the AKM's kinds exist (muzLoad).

## CQC step 105: M4A1-S attachments (?m4att=off; headset check needed)
- Asked as "M16"; the user meant the M4A1-S. No M16A1 code was kept (its store rows rifle.sup / light / laser are as before). Shared pieces made for this and kept: afxOf / afxStats / afxDeriveFx (one effects engine for every gun's table; GLOCK_FX runs through it), wsStoreIds, WS_BUILD (store guns whose rows are real parts: g17, m4a1s), RETICLE + reticleTick (one room brightness for every reticle glass; was g17Tick), SCOPE generalised (parts.scope per gun: AWP = SCOPE.awp, M4 = M4A.acog; SCOPE_RETICLE mil / chevron, scopeMat), wbSpotFits / wbHomePose (restQ, tableC) / wbTableFill, attRefresh, triPick, muzSeat / muzApply.
- Measured (offline plane slices of models/m4-glb.glb, design space): one mesh. Fixed A2-style carry handle (top bar y 0.051-0.0595, 14 mm wide, rear sight 0.066 at z -0.022, run z +0.0147..-0.1624) over a narrow receiver top (0.0203; side walls ~0.008); round vented handguard r 0.0265 -> 0.0240, z -0.1861..-0.3459; front sight post top 0.0596 (z -0.385); barrel r 0.0089, thread to a shoulder at z -0.4925, stub to -0.5199; the S can r 0.0166 (collar 0.018) to -0.655, separate triangles; collapsible stock z 0.1205..0.2626 on a buffer tube (top 0.0127, bottom -0.020, half width 0.0129); front sight base bottom -0.038; charging handle T-bar front z 0.0.
- m4Cut (extLoad; M4_GEO, m4CutOf), by triangle: can (centre ahead of the shoulder, not the barrel stub), handle (any vertex over 0.0215 in the run, |x| < 16 mm: rear sight and windage knob too), handguard (r >= 18 mm: barrel and gas tube stay), stock (z > 0.1215). Counts 20515 body / 3372 / 3094 / 9238 / 2967. Handle, handguard, stock: parts.handle / hg / stock, hidden when replaced. A failed cut = m4Fallback (the step 60 gun, store "attachments coming").
- The can is a part: kind m4can, the muzzle chain's first device on the shoulder (MUZ_DEV base: m4can, a2, rcomp). WEAPON_DEFS.m4a1s.muzzle = the bare stub; sound 'akm' (the recorded rifle shot). Default build = its can, so it now sounds through supShot's suppressed rifle path (was the MPX 'smg' report at full level); bare, the rifle report. Saved chains migrate once (cqc.m4att.v1): the old chain goes in front of m4can. wsBaseStats' built-in-can special case is off (the can's effect is M4_FX).
- Hardware (owned = on every M4, like the Glock plate): flat-top rail on a riser over the cut upper (rail top 0.0287, z -0.0014..-0.1594, stops ahead of the charging handle) with a flip-up rear sight (aperture on the post line, up with no optic, folded forward under one); quad rail (body r 0.0235, rails r 0.0295 at 12 / 3 / 6 / 9).
- Parts (M4_SPEC: spec parts in the armory materials; templates once, clones share): red dot CompM4 class 2 MOA (axis 0.0596: absolute co-witness), holo EXPS class (68 MOA ring + 1 MOA dot, axis 4 mm higher), 4x32 ACOG class (lit chevron + drop stadia; SCOPE picture 256 px headset / 384, only with the eye behind it; computer: full-screen with its own SVG); vertical grip (the AKM foregrip's offsets from the rail face, so VFG_SUP holds it; per-gun def.vfg), angled grip (the support zone); light / laser on the 3 / 9 o'clock rails (no quad rail: the old barrel-clamp snap); A2 birdcage, rifle comp, its own can; SOPMOD and slim CTR stocks. Store only: 20-round (the 30 body shortened x0.64 from the lips; capacity via extApplyCaps), 60-round (= the bench card, both ways), sling (black strap, front sight swivel to the stock), rail covers. Mount gaps: 0 by construction (seats from the measurements); mounts clear the folded sight by 5 mm.
- Bench: parts lie on the step 64 bench left of the gunsmith's (WB_M4); m4BenchFit (seat within 9 cm, or a click), m4Refuse (flat-top / quad rail needed, one optic, one grip, one stock, side rails full), base devices only first on the bare barrel; RELEASE: m4FromGun. Store: slots Optic / Muzzle / Tactical / Underbarrel / Magazine / Stock / Accessories + Hardware; m4StoreChanged -> m4SyncGun on every M4.
- M4_FX -> g.afx (further chain devices: muzFx); store bars m4Stats = afxStats on the same table; row fx derived at load.
- Gun locker: the M4's barrel-rest notch padded to the largest muzzle device radius (m4NotchPad).

M4 paint group takes its cut pieces (handle, hg, stock). Locker notch: the pad uses the largest MUZ_DEV radius, now the AKM's ATS5 (21 mm).
