## CQC step 110: hand shadows and flashlight dazzle (CONFIG.polish.handShadows / dazzle, ?handshadow=off / ?dazzle=off; headset check needed)
- HS (beside EYE): in the headset the step 104 gloves, the fitted gun hands (VMH.meshes), the procedural hand when it shows, the step 109 sleeves (VMSLV) and the gun in your hand cast shadows. three cannot pick lights per mesh (the shadow pass tests the main camera's layers), so hsHook (onBeforeShadow) collapses the mesh to a point (modelViewMatrix scale 0) in every map whose shadow camera lacks userData.hsOn: the weapon light and his torches take them (maps already redrawn while they move), plus the lit room spot nearest your hands (HS.room 1), redrawn every max(HS.every 2, shadowSched.every) frames while it takes them and once more to clear them (handShadowFlush after flushShadows; u.hsMap). No stale hand in an on-demand room map. Computer: no hands in the main scene, nothing takes them.
- Held guns: setKinematic turns castShadow off; while in a VR hand hsGuns tags them (castShadow on for opaque meshes) and on letting go puts setCast(root, !kinematic) back. The gun whose model carries the flashlight (HS.lg, any state) never draws into the flashlight's map. Light origin was already 4 mm past LIGHT_LENS (updateLightMount), unchanged.
- Flashlight shadow near 0.05 -> HS.near 0.02, bias x0.4 (same metres: perspective depth ~ near / d^2), normalBias kept. Nearer than that the lens: hsBeam, 73 rays over the cone (rings, weights = solid angle x the FLASH_BEAM pattern, dzProf) against 27 spheres a hand (palm, knuckle line, finger joints and midpoints from the posed bones / handTip), soft edge = lens radius 12 mm. HS.A (spheres within ap 2-6 cm of the lens) x the light's intensity; HS.T (all) x the haze beam (P10U.uLOn). A fitted grip hand within 6 cm of the lens is left out (its mesh casts; the bones only approximate it). Node: palm over the lens at 0.5-3 cm A 0, at 5 cm 0.004; at 10 cm A 1 (map's job), T 0.25 (0.26 with finger gaps); 3 cm aside at 1.5 cm A 0.26.
- DZ: lux at the eye per source = cd (game intensity x cd 160: yours 11.5k, his 6.7k) x dzProf(angle) / d^2 x hsSeg (hands on the line, lens end 12 mm, pupil end 3 mm) x one P10_OCCLUDE_RAY wall test. Sources: NIGHT.torches, your light turned on you, your light splashing back off what the beam hits within splashR 3 m (lambertian hot spot: splash 0.5 x rho x I x T x tan^2(hot) x cos / d^2, an extended source of its angular size; glass / metal / lockers also as the lens's mirror image).
- Glare per pixel g = B / (max(theta0, size)^2 + max(theta - size, 0)^2), B = gain x E / L deg^2, L = la 5.5 x NIGHT.env^0.45 + adapt 0.00087 x Ea; display sum(g c) / (1 + sum g). Ea chases total lux (rise 0.3 s, relax 3 s): view x (1 - dim), dim = 1 - la / L (max 0.7). Afterimages: 3 head-locked slots, strength 1 - e^(-0.005 B), fade tau 1-3 s by exposure. Node (yours, B and glare at 1 / 10 / 30 deg after adapting): lit 2 m on axis 360: 0.99 / 0.78 / 0.29, dim 0.31; night same 791: 1.0 / 0.89 / 0.47, dim 0.69 (0.74 at 30 deg for the first ~0.3 s); 10 m 20 deg off lit 6: 0.75 / 0.06 / 0.01, night 28: 0.93 / 0.22 / 0.03; his 10 m on axis at night 56: 0.97 / 0.36 / 0.06.
- Draw: one full-view triangle, ShaderMaterial (view ray from inverse projection, sources in world space per eye), premultiplied (rgb glare + dst x (1 - dim)). Headset: DZ.vr in the scene, layers mask 6 (only the eye cameras draw it), renderOrder 1e5, compiled with the scene at boot. Computer: DZ.desk in its own scene, compiled at load, drawn after vmRender (dazzleRender). Visible only while something shows. Off in body cam mode on a computer.
- No light count change; per frame only Rapier's ray hits. ?dev adds HS, DZ, dzProf, hsRays, hsSpheres, hsSeg, hsBeam, hsHook, hsScan, hsGuns, hsLights, handShadowTick, handShadowFlush, dazzleTick, dazzleRender, dzOwn, dzLux, dzAft.
- Checked: module parse; Node run of the page's own dzProf / hsRays / hsSeg / hsBeam (scratchpad s110/test.mjs). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, the room spot redraw cost (one 1024 map every 2nd frame), a support hand on a handguard next to a rail light (real, but may shadow part of the beam all the time), a magazine dropped from a held gun keeps its tag (collapsed in room maps until the gun leaves the hand).

## CQC step 115: grenades: recorded sound, smoke, scorch, gore (CONFIG.polish.nade; headset check needed)
- Sources (raw in cqc/sounds/src/): freesound 161622 ryanconway "grenade throw" (pin clicks 28-74 ms, striker snap 148 ms + lever 226 ms, the arm's wind thump 0.47-0.9 s (all under 1 kHz), an explosion spliced in at 1.791 s, clipped), 172334 knova "grenade" (one bassy explosion). Licences NOT verified; check both pages and credit if CC-BY.
- tools/cut_user_sfx.py (earlier files byte-identical): nadePin1/2, nadeSpoon, nadeTick (the snap alone), nadeBoom1/2 (group 'boom'), nadeThrow (DERIVED: the wind take highpassed at 28 Hz + seeded band noise on its own envelope, bright then darker; RMS -18).
- NREC (nadeRecLoad from bakeSounds; hrecNorm levels; nrecPick never repeats). nadeArmSfx: pin (nadePullPin, desktop pin phase), spoon (nadeSpoon). nadeSwingSfx: VR release / desktop throw over 3.5 m/s. nadeLand (dropKnock, kind nade): ray down -> gtKind -> NREC_SURF (KNOCK_SND block / plaster / wood / can / glass + nadeTick pitched 0.48-0.72), off the floor duller, a roll when landing moving.
- nadeBoom (from nadeSound; synth = fallback): NREC_BOOM variants (frag 4, bang 3: pitch / shelves) through soundAt; + sub tone 58 -> 27 Hz (x1.6 occluded), crack (close, clear line), slaps (NB_DIRS: 6 walls + ceiling, delay = extra path / 343, a 0.22 s darker slice of the take), verb 1.7 / echo 1.6 sends, debris patter 0.3-2.5 s (grit, plaster chips, glass if bottles / floor glass near). earsBlast(k, ring): SPAT.blast muffles the ears filter to earsFloor 320 Hz (sets in over 0.12 s, earsTau 2.2 s), SPAT.ring drives a second oscillator ringHz 4300 (ringTau 6 s); updateEars handles both. Flashbang ring x1.5.
- Smoke (nadeSmoke, replaces the step 23 haze): S.n 44 puffs in four waves (NSMOKE_WAVES, P10.pending), grey-brown, life 8-15 s; LAIR sees each wave (hazeEmit). hazeEmit o.force: in the headset (haze off) smoke.headset 0.4 of it still shows.
- Scorch (nadeScorchX, decal kinds scorch / scorchW, nadeScorchKit from polishKit, precompile dummies, renderOrder 0.95 under the blood): floor 1.2-2 m (smaller in the air), walls within 1.7 m (8 rays, one per wall plane), saved in room memory holes. Old mesh scorch when off. Flash: fragLight x1.5, time x1.4, shake x1.25.
- Gore (frag, g90K > 0): nadeGoreScan before the fragments: men within 4.5 m with LOS get up to 7 fragment wounds (enemyRayTest from the blast, g90Hole 6-12 mm, 2 pour); < 1.5 m nadeTear: two nearest limbs cratered (crater 0.045 m), pouring, chunks. nadeGoreAfter: mist + red haze, g90Spray behind each man; severe: second spray, 12 chunks, pools 1.3-1.7 / 1.0-1.3, death impulse and rag shove x2.2. nadeDownHit: a man the overpressure kills beyond 1.5 m goes down alive (step 91) with chance 0.55. NADE.blastT: a man its own fragments or overpressure just put down is not finished by the same blast. G90 budgets: streams 8, chunks 60, gibBudget 20.
- ?naderec=off = step 23 sounds (no arming / swing sounds, no earsBlast, no ring oscillator). Checked: module parse only, offline analysis of the takes. Not heard, not seen, not on Quest.
## CQC: PC fixes (viewmodel arms, hold, sway)
- "VR arms on the PC game": on a computer handsUpdate still put both procedural hands on the real (hidden) gun every frame, bodyArms then reached the body's arms to them, and only vmTick hid the hands afterwards. The step 109 pack sleeves (VMSLV, grey: the pack fabric lerped to white) were therefore drawn in the main scene reaching across the view to the hidden gun's hold under the viewmodel (headless: 26-121 of 576 sleeve vertices on screen at FOV 72-100, pitch 0 to -0.6). Their IK chasing the real gun's heft / breathing sway / ready pivot was the "lots of movement" and the "bad hand positions" (cuffs nowhere near the viewmodel hands).
- Fix: handsUpdate skips the desktop gun holds while vmGun() owns the gun (drawn or still loading), so the body's arms hang; vmTick hides the VMSLV sleeves while vmGun() is set and shows them again otherwise (headset: vmGun() is null, unchanged). ?vm=off / a failed rig or fit = the old procedural hold, as before. Checked headless (?dev, AKM / Glock / M4 from the locker): vmGun true, VM visible, real gun hidden, procedural hands hidden, 0 sleeve vertices drawn; full attachment builds on all three (AKM kit/holo/VG/stock/light/can/40, M4 everything, Glock dot/comp/light): vmTick runs with no error, afx sway 0.88-1.2.
- Other bugs found: ?dev threw at load (window.__cqcDev assigned an undefined g17Tick, a step 105 rename left in a duplicated line), which stopped the module and left AKMA / MUZ / M4A / M1911A in their TDZ (dev only; removed from the line). M1911 parts never fitted on any build: step 108's hardware handle g.m19 collided with the M1911 hammer state item.m19 set in createGun, so m19Refresh threw "Cannot set properties of undefined" on every sync; the hardware handle is now g.m19hw (m19Hw, m19Gaps).
- Not done (stopped for time): the rest of the PC sweep (E / click / Space ADS / CapsLock / B at the vise / R / workshop by mouse, optic sight lines on desktop ADS, FOV slider, store zoom). Not checked by eye.
## CQC step 114: live gun build view (?buildview=off; syntax parse only, not run; headset check needed)
- Workshop gun page tabs BUILD (default) / PARTS (wsDrawGun) / FINISH (paintDrawPage, PAINT.page set by the tab) in the store header (BV.tab, bvGunPage). Hooks: wsDraw (subtitle only on SUPPLIES or with ?buildview=off; bvGunPage instead of paintGunPage), wsUpdate VR loop (bvFinger after glassFinger), wsUpdate end (bvTick). Everything else self-contained (BVIEW knobs, BV state, bv* functions after wsUpdate); reads WS_GUNS / WS_ITEMS / wsReal / wsRealOn / wsStats only.
- Gun: a mirror of a real gun of that type (bvPick every 0.5 s: in hand / holster / sling 8, vise 6, not his drop 2, current +1 hysteresis) via vmMirSync (shared geometry and materials, layers as the main camera sees them, lights become empties), so the store's sync functions' parts and finishes show as on your guns. None in the room = "No <gun> in this room". Own scene: PMREM studio environment (bvStudioEnv: grey room, warm softbox, two cool strips, top panel; made once), key + rim DirectionalLight, backdrop card on the camera; turntable pivot. Target HalfFloat MSAA 4, 1024 x 576 computer / 512 x 288 headset and phone, shown by a MeshBasicMaterial quad over the view rectangle on the glass (no canvas copy, no readback). Render as the PiPs do (xr off, target saved / restored) and shadowMap.needsUpdate kept for the room.
- Build change = the mirror's visible mesh + material-id signature (bvSync): re-anchor (bvAnchors) and renderer.compileAsync on the target (programs for this target and these lights) before the next picture; a new gun shows "Building the view…" until compiled.
- Slots (BV_SLOTS): Optic, Stock, Grip / Panels, Magazine (left), Rail, Muzzle, Barrel, Laser / Light, Underbarrel (right). A row goes to a box by category (handguard -> Barrel, sights -> Optic, sling / accessories sling -> Stock, accessories -> Rail, bipod -> Underbarrel), hardware and unknown categories by name regex, else Grip / Panels (auto sear). Box: name, your fitted parts (else installed hardware, else WS_GUNS factory text), dot, nub. No rows = "No parts" (dim, no line).
- Anchors (gun root space): fitted att props (g.atts, classified by sub / type / part / akKey / label) and the magazine by their mirrored mesh boxes; else g.parts stand-ins (scope / handle, stock / wStock, hg, stockLight); else from WR (muzzle, chamber, eye, light, vfg / support, magSeat, palm = grip). Frame = phys pick box + atts + magazine + chain tip; camera distance fits the swept circle.
- Lines: three LineSegments on the glass (dim: empty or round the back, lit: yours in front, hot: hovered / open), elbow stub + diamond per slot, written in place (draw range), redrawn on render or hover change.
- Open box: the view moves left, the list (5 rows, ▲▼ past 5, ✕) takes the right; rows use row: / act: ids so the bottom bars preview (bvPreview = wsDrawStats' rule); BUY / FIT / REMOVE via wsBuyItem / wsFit (bvAct forces a re-check); the gun eases to the part's side (left side for -x anchors). Bars: 12 in three columns, EQUIPPED n / max (boxes with yours / boxes with rows).
- Drag: VR touch on the gun (r 2 in the view) then the tip sliding turns (0.0105 rad / unit) and tilts (pitch -0.45..0.8), lifted past the glass re-arm = let go, 15 deg haptic detents; computer E or click on the gun holds it, mouse turns it (window capture mousemove stops the look), mouseup / E up lets go; phones turntable only. 2.5 s hold, then turning again (0.22 rad/s).
- Cost: only while awake, the last canvas draw was BUILD, the glass within ~70 deg of your view (bvLooking); turning 15 fps headset / 30 computer, dragging 30 / 60, still = a 4 Hz signature check and no render. No per-frame allocation in bvTick / bvRender / bvLines. Known: shared materials switch programs between the room and this scene each render (three's per-material lookup, as for the viewmodel mirror).
- ?dev adds BV, BVIEW, BV_SLOTS, BV_UI, bvSlotOf, bvRows, bvGunPage, bvPick, bvSync, bvAnchors, bvRender, bvLines, bvTick, bvFinger, bvTurn. Checked: module parse (node --check). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, light levels, anchors per gun, drag feel, frame time.
## Studio: CQC guns and the range (first part; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- addons/range.js (`window.__studioRange`, loaded after enemies.js): Glock 17, M1911, AKM, M4A1-S, AWM from cqc/models/*.glb through the bundle's GLTFLoader chunk, baked once per type (CQC bakes: EXTG g17 / m4 with compactGeo, X64 akmn / awp with quantized -> float, m1911 node offset) into the bundle's held-gun frame (grip point (0, -0.012, 0.02) = CQC palm, bore -z). Each gun is a physics prop (one box from the drawn bounds) and a controller in `S.guns` implementing the bundle interface (owns, pullTrigger, setTrigger, primary, secondary, reload, cycle, accessory, status, eyePoint, forendPoint, recoilMatrix, update), so the engine's grab, VR support hand (forendPoint), ADS and recoil drive it. Not saved; spawned on the bench (x 1.2, static collider + walk blocker).
- Handling: desktop click fire (held = auto), RMB aim, R / B reload (magazine out, a fresh one from the bag, slide / bolt home), F rack or bolt, L / V selector; HUD shows the gun's own keys (desk.updateHud wrapped). VR: trigger, A / X slide release / selector / bolt, B / Y reload, other hand on the handguard. Mechanics: chamber + magazine count, pistols and M4 lock back empty, AKM closes, AWM leaves the case until the bolt is worked; AKM / M4 semi / auto / safe at 600 / 800 rpm.
- Ballistics: per cartridge mass, muzzle velocity, G1 BC (9 mm 355 m/s .15, .45 255 .19, 7.62x39 715 .29, 5.56 880 .30, .338 LM 905 .67); drag = rho Cd_G1(Mach) pi v² / (8 BC 703.07) + gravity, 4 sub-steps a frame, time of flight; zero angle solved numerically (pistols 25 m, rifles 100 m; AWM sight line from its scope glass). Rays: Rapier castRayAndGetNormal (room, bench, props: impulse 0.6 m v) + THREE (targets, enemy capsules via onBulletHit). Desktop hip fire: from the muzzle toward the crosshair point with scatter.
- Recoil: per gun back / rise impulses into a critically damped spring (two hands halve the climb); desktop camera kick, 55 % recovered. Sound: cqc/sounds recordings (pistol1 at 1 / 0.91, ak1-5, M4A1-S = suppressed ak at 0.87 rate, 0.32 gain, lowpass; awp1; handling takes glk* / ak* / smg*, dry, magDrop, pickup), equal-power panners, compressor, indoor 0.8 s room IR, outdoor 0.45 s field echo past the window line. Effects: additive flash sprite, 64 pooled hole decals, 16 dust puffs.
- Targets (`setup()`, "set up the range"): down the lane from the bench through the open middle pane: paper bullseyes at 5 / 10 m (holes punched into the canvas, ring score), AR500 plates on a crossbar at 10, 25, 50, 100, 200, 300 m (pendulum swing + twist from the round's momentum, synth plate ring delayed d / 343 s, air lowpass). Ground collider beyond the terrace (top -0.07, the sky's ground); camera far raised to 420 m for the 300 m plate.
- Wiring: index.html script tag; Props app group "Range" (cards from `__studioRange.cards()`); palette group "Range"; commands spawn_gun ("spawn the AKM", gun enum), range_setup (+ "spawn targets"), reset_targets, score, clear_guns ready; start_clays planned. commands.json regenerated (129, 117 ready).
- Not built yet: magazines as separate props (VR change by hand, mags on the bench), VR racking by grabbing the slide, casings and tinks, the AWM scope picture / desktop scope view, wall gun rack, scoreboard board, poppers, bottles / cans, clays and birds, walking out onto the terrace. Checked: node --check only.
## CQC step 113: AWM attachments (?awmatt=off; syntax parse + Node maths only, not run; headset check needed)
- Measured (offline plane slices of models/awp-glb.glb, design space): 'Scope' mesh = the 3-25x with its rings and a top housing (axis y 0.0599, objective front z -0.4794 r 0.0316, glass at -0.46, eyepiece rear 0.0166 r 0.0366; awmMeasure re-reads it at load); receiver Picatinny top 0.0236 z -0.050..-0.2525; fore-end bottom -0.0493 z -0.26..-0.46 (sling stud -0.412..-0.420), half 0.0175; comb top 0.0053 z 0.115..0.235; barrel r 0.0113, brake collar from -0.843 (r 0.0189), brake r 0.0155 to the muzzle -0.958.
- Parts (AWM_SPEC, procedural, armory materials, templates once): sunshade 75 mm and honeycomb killflash 28 mm (151 cells, 4.2 mm, 68 % open) on the 3-25x objective (killflash in front of the shade; a shade under a fitted killflash is refused; the killflash slides back when the shade comes off), flip-up covers front + rear (store, cosmetic, open), fixed 10x42 on its own rings (store optic slot: one optic; SCOPE desc AWMA.s10, mil-dot, 256 / 384 px, axis 0.0455), bipod (Harris class, 6.7-9.5 in legs, 10 deg splay) on the bipod interface rail under the fore-end nose (z -0.424..-0.458), cheek riser on the stock module, .338 can 52 x 275 mm over the brake (rear face on the QD ring z -0.829), QD sling on left cups, 10-round magazine (body +45 mm via magStretch; factory 5: MAG_DEFS.awp / WEAPON_DEFS.awp.capacity follow, pouches restocked on a change).
- Hardware (owned = on every AWM): rail + rings (the rifle's own), bipod rail, stock module (plate + knobs), QD ring, sling cups. Factory scope hidden when the store optic is none / the 10x.
- Bipod (awmBipodTick, one ray a held AWM): held near level (the computer: aimed), a horizontal surface (normal y > 0.75) under the leg hinge within the legs' reach (+18 cm on the computer: it cannot lower the rifle) deploys it (0.28 s, latch sound), legs reach for it while deploying, then lock; folds 1.2 s after the surface is gone, or when let go / stowed. Rested (feet on it) eases k to 1: g.afx sway x0.25, aimed sway x0.3, kick x0.5, climb x0.38, desk kick x0.45 (AWM_REST over afx0). VR (awmRestPose in updateGun): the hinge is held at surface + legs, the gun turns about the grip, the feet drag (tau 0.15 s); lifted 3 cm = free.
- Cheek riser: the scope's eye box x1.35 + AWM_FX sway / aim. Glint (awmGlint): the scope in your hand without a killflash, pointed within 6 deg (shade or 10x: 3.5) of a man: lit room, he sees you out to x1.35 (1.2) his sight range (enemySees); dark, his torch coming back off the glass (nightCanSee).
- Effects: AWM_FX -> g.afx (afxOf, VR heft k 0.1 / 50 ms), store bars afxStats on it through WS_REAL.awp, row fx derived (afxDeriveFx). Chain kind k338 (MUZ_DEV, r 26 mm): first and only device on the AWM, needs the muzzle interface, refused on other guns, other devices refused on the AWM (awmMuzRefuse); muzBase = the QD ring (awmBase); AWM_MUZ row = the chain's first device. Heat: HEAT.can.k338 C 380 J/K, tau 170 s (8.5 K a round). Old saves (once, cqc.awmatt.v1): a bench rifle can on the AWP becomes the .338 can; the 10-round row owned and fitted (the old capacity).
- Bench: parts on the front row of the bench left of the gunsmith's (WB_AWM, z 2.44); awmBenchFit (9 cm or a click) / awmRefuse; RELEASE: awmFromGun; store changes: awmSyncGun on every AWM. Holds: g17Hold fist (attKey 'att:awm:<part>'). Viewmodel mirror: generic (legs animate in it). Gun locker notch padded for the folded legs / a can (awmNotchPad).
- Hooks (one line each, step 113 comments): boot (awmInit), createGun, createMag, attKey, tick (awmTick), updateGun VR (awmRestPose), wbMakeAtt, wbTable, wbVar, wbClose, wbAttRelease, wbDeskAtt, wbFreeze, attRefresh, muzBase, muzBenchFit refusal, muzFx counted, muzRefresh, muzMake, precompile, gun locker notch, WS_REAL, enemySees, nightCanSee. WS_GUNS.awp gains the Magazine slot.
- Checked: module parse (node --check); Node run of the page's own constants and awmHoneycomb (cells inside the ring, leg drops 168-237 mm, rest multipliers, glint cones). Not run in a browser (user's instruction): the look of every part, bipod deploy / rest in VR and on the computer, the 10x picture, holds and bench snaps, the glint in play, Quest frame time.
## CQC step 112: stacking, wrap rails, Glock stock light (?rails=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Rail spots (RAIL, end of the module): railFaces lists each gun's rail faces in design space: Glock frame rail (two key positions, -92.5 / -102.5 mm; X300 / DBAL hung under the underside as in step 100, the PL-2 on the base plane), AKM kit side + bottom rails, M4 quad rail (12 / 3 / 6 / 9), the clamp-on wrap rail when owned. A spot = a 10 mm recoil slot (railSlotsOf: the gaps of akmRailGeo's teeth); the device's cross-bolt sits in it.
- Free spot (railSpots): the device's boxes (RAIL_DEV mount + body, from the wbKit / G17L geometry) miss every other part (rail devices by their boxes, other parts by mesh bounds), the gun's drawn body (railCloud: points every 3 mm over the visible triangles, 1 cm hash; bodies only, 0.6 mm tolerance), the firing hand (round def.palm), the support hand on long guns (def.support, M4 at -0.25), the sight line (6 mm tube at g.sightY), and 60 % of the clamp is on the rail. Order: light bottom / right / left / top, laser right / left / top / bottom, front-most slot first; VR: the free spot nearest the release (12 cm). None = refused with a message (+ the wrap rail's store name if not owned).
- Bench: railBenchFit runs first for lights / lasers / the PL-2 on a gun with faces (wbAttRelease, wbDeskAtt); guns without faces keep the old fits, and the old barrel-clamp snap is refused if it would sit inside a part (railClash). Frozen parts have no body; stuck ones are kinematic.
- Several lights: g.lights, each switched by its own buttons (nearLightSwitch / switchNear return which; desktop F and the stick click switch all). The brightest lit (it.lum: X300 1, PL-2 0.55) is g.light and drives the one shadowed flashlight (updateLightMount unchanged); the others add their lens glow and a faint additive cone (RAIL.glow, pool of 4, precompiled). No light count change.
- Several lasers: g.lasers entries { it, emit, dir, sw } (gun-root space), each its own pad; g.wr.laser = the first; updateLasers casts every lit one (railLasersCast; LASER slots 2 -> 6). laserCast takes the entry.
- PL-2: a real att item (railMakePl2, G17L geometry; extBuild no longer builds the hidden stock light when rails are on). Store row g17.pl2 "PL-2 (factory)" (owned, fitted on first run, GLOCK_FX entry). Fitting a device on its spot sends it to the bench table (railBump, RAIL_PL2_SPOTS: listed in wbTable only while a PL-2 off a gun claims one); a store-fitted device that only fits there bumps it too (railSync).
- Wrap rails (hardware rows, $110-150): g17.wrap (U sleeve round the dust cover, measured from the frame by railPistolKit: front -128.2, underside -27.6, half width 12.2, rear -77.2 where the frame widens, cheeks to the slide's outer skirt -12.4; bottom rail 12 mm out front, side rails on risers), m1911.frame (step 108's rail + side risers), akmn.wrap / m4a1s.wrap / rifle.wrap / awp.wrap (clamp rings 1.2 mm off the drawn section over RAIL_WRAP.z, railSection; bottom + side rails, split screws on top; the M4's hides with the quad rail). Side rails at least 21.2 mm out (RAIL.sideX) so a device underneath and one on each side clear each other.
- Layout per gun type in cqc.rails.v1 ([{ d, f, k }]): railSaveGun at RELEASE, railSync on every gun (createGun, each gun's store sync, wrap purchase); rows light / laser / pl2 = at least one on (off removes all of the kind, on adds one at the next free spot; a row whose device finds no spot is unfitted). Extra devices of a kind add their FX entry again (railFx); wraps count as hardware (g17 / akmn / m4a1s GunIds, FX entries). WS_GUNS g17 / m1911 tactical is multi.
- Hooks (one line each, step 112 comments): toggleGunLight / toggleGunLaser (param), nearLightSwitch / nearLaserSwitch / switchNear / switchTry / switchTips, updateLasers, laserCast, buildLaserKit, weapons tick (railTick), weaponsPrecompile, wbMakeAtt, wbVar, wbTable, wbClose (save + sync), wbAttRelease, wbDeskAtt, wbGrabbed, wbFreeze, wbUnfreeze, g17Detach, extBuild, g17Refresh, G17_PARTS / g17PartId, g17 / akm / m19 / m4 SyncGun (rows skipped, railSync), the four Refresh (railFx), createGun, wsBuyItem. Fixed: the ?dev list named g17Tick (gone since step 105: a ReferenceError with ?dev) -> reticleTick.
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef; Node run of the page's own railSlotsOf / railCutGeo / railDevBoxes / railCloudHit on the Glock GLB (/tmp/s112/test.mjs): factory rail PL-2 and X300 clear at slot 0, DBAL only at slot 1, light + laser cannot share it; wrap: bottom light, right laser and left light stack with no overlaps, clear of the gun and the firing hand. Not run in a browser or on Quest: the ring sections and looks, VR placement and switches, the glow cones.

## CQC: recentre out of map, shelf light, bug check 3 (headset check needed)
- Recentre guard (HG, headGuard in tick after xrSysFrame, before input moves you; ?headguard=off). Cause: holding the Quest system button recentres the play space ('reset' on the reference space, the head pose jumps); nothing moved the rig back, and physical walking was never resolved against walls (only the fade), so you could end up through a wall or outside the map. Now: every frame the last head spot that is in an active room (walls included) with its centre clear of every walk box on your floor is kept room-local (shiftWorld safe) with its floor. A 'reset' (then a move over 5 cm / 0.15 rad within 0.6 s) or a tracking jump (xrsys.jump, or 0.5 rad of yaw in one frame): the rig turns back by the yaw jump (you keep your heading) and moves the head to where it was a frame ago (the last good spot if that was bad), xrSysReset re-seats body, feet and hand histories, held loose props come along. Safety net (headset and computer): out of every room 0.35 s, centre over a body radius inside a box 0.35 s, or clear again on the far side of a box (hgCrosses from the last good spot: walked through a wall, door leaf or crate) = head back to the last good spot (its floor; resolved if something moved onto it). Leaning into a wall and back is untouched (headPenetration fade stays); cover you peek over only counts below its top.
- Shelf light (SHELF_LIGHT, glShelfLight in glKit; ?shelflight=off): the gun locker (the safe room's shelves: pistol shelf, magazine shelf, long-gun bay) gets warm-white LED strips in aluminium channels under its top (one per bay, behind the header) and under the pistol shelf. No light object (the light count stays fixed); no pool light reused (the pool spots / area lights all follow the troffers). Emissive opal diffusers (2.4) plus additive light cards baked from a Lambertian line source per strip (cos emit x cos incidence / r, ends dimmer) x reflectance x gain 0.2: on the pistol and magazine shelf pads and the back panel of each bay. Built once with the kit, cloned per safe room under room.group (turned rooms right). The guns themselves are not relit (no real light).
- Bug check 3 fixes: (1) a man standing into you pushed the rig without any wall check (enemyMove "never stand inside you"): now resolveBody, so he cannot shove you into or through a wall; (2) grenade smoke waves 2-4 (step 115, P10.pending) used the world position of the blast, so a world shift in the next 2.2 s (a vertical re-base on a stairwell landing) put them metres away: now room-local, none if the room unloaded; (3) the headset 'reset' listener's second addEventListener sat outside its null check.
- Checked, no change: respawn (floor, facing, feet re-seat, slung rifle snaps back past 1.2 m), holster / sling after a recentre (bone-anchored; slingCarry resets its spring past 0.6 m, stowAnim rides relative to the carry, bodyPre runs before weapons), desktop hop (eye at most 2.07 m under 3 m ceilings), the rest of step 115 (blastT / downAt ordering, earsBlast gain reaching 0, swing sound on a removed grenade has 0 speed).
- Checked: module parse; Node runs of the page's own functions (scratchpad /tmp/hg: test.mjs wall depth / crossing / shelf light maths; guard.mjs headGuard with three r170: recentre puts the head back to 0 mm with yaw kept and one body reset, walking out through a side wall never gets past it, lean in and back untouched, through an interior wall put back, through a doorway into the next room untouched, computer outside the map put back). Not run in a browser or on Quest: a real Quest recentre (event timing), how the shelf light looks.

## CQC step 117: surrender (CONFIG.enemy.surrender, ?surrender=off; headset check needed)
- When (surrCheck, after enemyThink): you within near 3.5 m, a bore line of a gun in your hand (chamber, -z; desktop gun or either VR hand) within headR 0.25 m of his head for hold 0.6-1.2 s while he sees you (e.visible): one roll, then cool 2.5 s. surrChance: base 0.04, each reason an independent chance (morale < 0.5: 0.5, health < 80 %: 0.4, no teammate still fighting: 0.35, reloading 0.45, dry 0.6), x1.15 timid .. x0.7 pushy, max 0.92. Node: calm 3 % per 1.5 s hold, low morale + reloading 72 %, never at 5 m / 40 cm off / unseen. Rarely on his own (rand): a check a second, his squad all down, he engaged and seeing you, hurt or surprised (saw you < 2 s ago or flashed < 3 s ago), within 10 m: 0.02 (Node: 17 % in 10 s).
- Then (surrStart): enemyDropGun (rounds left in it; e.unarmed stops a second drop at death), hands leave the gun hold, then up and open (palms to you, elbows out, a slight shake), steps back up to 1.1 m while you are within 2.6 m (anchor walked away on his nav grid, enemyMove follows it, faces you), kneels (right knee first, toes tucked, ebfKneel on landing) after 1.6 s covered (bore within 0.6 m of head / chest). Calls: the step 69 grunt takes pitched 1.16-1.32 and cut to 0.42 s, two in a row, again every 3-6 s while covered (max 4); no takes = two band-passed breaths. Toast "Surrendering", pulseSoft 0.16 on VR gun hands. enemyOut: plan.dead, so leaving him clears the room (loopRoomCleared if he was the last); teammates lose 0.15 morale.
- He no longer fights (enemiesUpdate: e.surr -> surrTick: own sight check, move, pose; no think, no fire). surrPose on the DRIVER (skin and hit capsules follow): legs IK standing / kneeling, head on you (down once tied), arms by solveIK + frameQ, finger curl by state. Node with a test rig: hands 0.29 m over the shoulders, palm normal 0.97 toward you; kneeling knees 5 cm off the floor, pelvis 0.51 m; tied wrists 7 cm apart behind the pelvis.
- Shot: enemyShot -> surrShot (hp 0) -> the normal death path (enemyDown / enemyDie), a scored kill; blast and kick use their own damage as before.
- Back to the fight (surrRearmCheck): not detained, his dropped gun within 1.4 m and nobody holding it, you not covering him and looking over 1 rad off him (or out of his sight) for 10 s, then 0.015 a second (x0.6-1.4 by aggr, x0.6 kneeling; Node: 22 / 40 within 60 s turned away, 0 / 20 covered). He gets up, walks to it facing it, stoops, takes it (removeProp, his own gun model back, rounds from its magazine), state react; plan.dead false. You picking it up meanwhile = he gives up the try.
- Detain: E with the dot on his upper body within 1.7 m (deskToggleGrab hook) or an empty VR hand squeezing within 0.22 m of either wrist (squeezeStart hook, before props): hands zip-tied behind his back over 1.2 s (ratchet synth), kneeling, head down, a breath take; + detain.pts 150 x streak x scoreK at risk, walletEarn 50, toast. A tied man never rearms.
- Hooks (one line each): CONFIG.enemy.surrender, enemiesUpdate (surrFrame, surrTick, surrCheck), enemyShot (surrShot), enemyDropGun (e.unarmed), deskToggleGrab, VR squeezeStart, ?dev line. ?dev adds SURR (stat counts), surrFrame, surrBoreDist, surrAim, surrWhy, surrChance, surrCheck, surrStart, surrShot, surrTick, surrBack, surrRearmCheck, surrRearmTick, surrRearm, surrDetain, surrDetainGrab, surrDetainDesk, surrCall, surrZip, surrPose.
- Known: a surrendered man still counts as present for his teammates' squad code (cover claims, check fire through him); a room unloaded with him alive does not bring him back; respawn resets his spot to home but he stays surrendered.
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef (browser globals only); Node run of the page's own block with solveIK / frameQ on a test driver rig and stubs (scratchpad /tmp/s117/test.mjs, 21 checks). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look of the poses on the skinned models, voice pitch, VR wrist reach.
## CQC step 119: bullet penetration (CONFIG.ballistics, ?pen=off; headset check needed)
- traceBullet is now a wrapper (PEN, penStart) round the old trace (traceBullet0): the round's state lives only while it flies (PEN.live): its cartridge (CONFIG.ballistics.rounds: m, v, d, form, defl, tum; gun -> round in .gun), J per energy unit (muzzle J / its start E), things gone through (PEN.n, max 3), tumble 0..1, its own copy of the path (PEN.d, it may turn). Guns missing from CONFIG.cover.energy (g17, m1911, m4a1s, akmn, awp: they were 1) now start at their round's e (1, 1.1, 1.9, 2.3, 4.5).
- penHit (after coverHit says no; cover keeps its step 6 rules): walls, doors, loose props. Thickness along the path = the collider's own (obbExit on its cuboid, else a ray cast back from 0.8 m beyond). What is inside by surface: an end-wall face (the slab between rooms) or plaster <= 14 cm = stud wall (two 12.5 mm boards and air, 10 % a steel stud); thicker plaster (outer walls, clad columns) = a board over block; concrete solid; floors / ceilings / treads stop; room metal <= 7 cm (the door stand-in) and door leaves = two steel skins (2.4 mm) + paper core; machines stop; props by KNOCK_MAT (cardboard, crates, tins and jerry cans with liquid, blocks, bottles, pipes; ammo cans, chairs and items stop). Cost J = sum r (J/cm for a 9 mm FMJ) x cm / cos x (area / 9 mm's)^a / form x (1 + 1.2 tumble); gypsum 14, wood 22, steel 1100 (a 0.5), glass 60, concrete 330 (a 0.5), water 4.5. Under graze |cos| 0.2 it skids off. Not enough J = the old hole, it stops.
- Through (Node, the page's functions): every round through a stud wall (9 mm keeps E 0.93; four walls 0.93 / 0.85 / 0.74 / 0.60 as it tumbles); outer walls, ceilings, 20 cm concrete stop all but the .338 (E 0.57 left of 4.5); a door: 9 mm yes (0.48), .45 yes, 5.56 / 7.62 / .338 yes, .380 / a buckshot pellet / a fragment no.
- penOn after anything it went through (cover, bodies, penHit): count (the 4th thing stops it), tumble += tum x (0.35 + share of energy lost), deflect by defl deg x (0.35 + share) x (1 + tumble / 2) x a triangular draw (stud wall: 9 mm median 0.75 deg / 95 % 2.1, 5.56 0.5, .338 0.2, pellet 1.2). Your rounds: men within 2.5 m of where it came out learn (penWarn). His rounds traced at you (PEN.aim) are re-aimed from the exit (playerRayHit): PEN.part, or PEN.miss and it flies on to the wall behind you (a 9 mm through a door 3 m in front of you: 14 % turn off you).
- Visuals: entry holes (bulletHit, door holes, partition sheets) sized to calibre (PEN.holeS = d / 9, 0.7..1.35; addRoomHole / addDoorHole / propHole take sc). Exit: decal kinds plasterX (0.11 m: torn paper flaps bent out, crumbled gypsum ring, loose dust), woodX (0.085: splinters along the grain), metalX (0.045: petals bent out of a bare-steel bulge), concreteX (0.13: terraced spall cone, aggregate, cracks); canvas + height canvas -> bloodNormalTex; map + normalMap + transparent like the holes (no new program, warmed with them; penKit after the hole materials) x penXS (calibre, tumble). Wall exits saved in the room they show in (penExitRoom: the far side of a wall between rooms); door exits in doorHoles with h[8] = kind. Cover's own exits (lockers, crates, tables, the far partition sheet) use the torn kinds too (penX). Dust + bits + a haze puff out along the path (PEN_FX, metal: sparks), a token bucket (24/s, 10 at once) so a frag's 70 fragments share it; plaster exits trickle (step 88).
- Sound: a through-and-through mutes the usual impact (P10.muteImpact round bulletHit) and plays penPunchPlay at the entry (punch + crumble / splinters / torn skin / grit) and penCrackPlay at the exit (a flat crack, bits landing), through soundAt, within impactRange, 40 ms apart. Cover exits add the crack.
- Gameplay: men behind walls are hit on the next pass (enemyRayTest along the turned path, reduced E); a tumbling round does dmg x (1 + 0.3 tumble) and step 90 holes x (1 + 0.6 tumble) (penDmgK, penWoundK in g90Hit); his rounds through walls hit you with the energy left x penYouK. AI: room.penK (1 when a man is hit through something, +0.5 when your round comes out near one; forgotten over ai.memory 120 s); chooseCover / aiDecide take penCoverMalus off soft points (partitions 1, lockers / tables 0.8, crates 0.6, columns 0) x your gun (rifles 1, pistols 0.55, buckshot 0.35) x ai.malus 2.2.
- Hooks (one line each, step 119 comments): traceBullet0 (E, penHit after coverHit, penOn), bulletHit (hole sizes), addRoomHole / addDoorHole / restoreRoomHoles / propHole, softHit / crateHit / tableHit / partHit / partSheet exits, buildWeaponKit (penKit), enemyShot (damage, learn), enemyShoot (re-aimed part / miss), g90Hit (K), chooseCover / aiDecide, ?dev list.
- ?dev adds PEN_ON, PEN, PEN_FX, penRound, penStart, penHit, penThick, penRoomLayers, penDoorLayers, penPropLayers, penCost, penOn, penExitRoom, penLearn, penMem, penCoverMalus, penExitFx, penSound, penKit, penExitCanvas, traceBullet0. ?pen=off: traceBullet calls the old trace directly (energy 1 for unlisted guns, no penetration, old hole sizes and kinds).
- Checked: module parse (node --check), eslint no-undef (only browser globals); Node runs of the page's own penRound / penCost / penRoomLayers / penDoorLayers / penPropLayers (scratchpad /tmp/s119/test.mjs: table above, tumble cost) and penOn with three r170 (/tmp/s119/deflect.mjs: deflection spread, the 4th thing refused, re-aim). Not run in a browser or on Quest: how the exit decals look, sounds, AI behaviour, frame time with a frag through drywall.
## CQC step 116: brass collection and selling (CONFIG.brass, ?brass=off; headset check needed)
- Spent cases stay: a case from the step 5 pools (yours or his) that is asleep, or slower than 5 cm/s after CONFIG.brass.settle s, leaves the pool (brassSettle from updateCasings; also forced when its pool slot is reused or its room unloads) and goes into roomMem(room).brass as [lx, ly, lz, quaternion (room-local), calibre, count, time]. Calibre from the gun (BRASS_TYPE: 9 mm, .45, 5.56, 7.62x39, .338, 12 ga hull; his gun by its def). The 9 mm pool's body takes the calibre's real length, radius and mass at spawn (brassSpawned), drawn scaled in flight.
- Drawing: per room and calibre one InstancedMesh under room.group (turned rooms right; rebuilt on a change and on reactivation: brassRoomOn / brassRoomOff). 10-segment lathe cases with neck and shoulder (rifle), hulls use the hull pool's geometry. Over CONFIG.brass.cap (200) entries the oldest merge into the nearest case of their calibre (brassMerge): piles, drawn as up to 6 cases round the spot (5th+ on top).
- Collect: VR grip (brassGrab, in squeezeStart before lootGrab) within 9 cm of a case takes the nearest one; keep the grip and sweep (over 0.15 m/s) to scoop up to CONFIG.brass.scoop (5). Computer: E (brassDesk, first in deskToggleGrab) with the dot on a floor or a top (ray normal up, not a prop) takes every case within CONFIG.brass.reach 0.6 m; a box in your hand still drops. Cases go into the bag by loose volume (bulk per calibre; bag 2000 cm3 = ~800 9 mm); a full bag refuses.
- Hot: a case fresher than CONFIG.brass.hot 6 s is not taken: VR 40 ms strong haptic sting, computer toast. No damage.
- Bag: canvas dump bag (coyote duck canvas texture, gathered drawstring, cord lock, belt loop) on the left hip behind the drop leg (rigLocal on the pelvis); rounder as it fills. VR: grip it to take it in your hand (hangs from the fist); held over the bin's opening for 0.35 s it tips and pours; let go = back on the hip.
- Bin: steel ammo-can style (olive ammo-can paint, rolled rim, pressed ribs, skids, end handles, cam latch, lid with gasket open against the wall, BRASS / SPENT CASES ONLY stencil), safe room back right corner (x 3.1, z -2.74, between the exit door and the gun locker), room.group child; colliders (bottom, four walls) on room.body + a walk box. Inside: a heap mesh (shared grid, a cone at 28 deg repose solved from the bin's loose volume, flat at the brim; brass case canvas texture) and up to 40 real cases lying on it. Computer: E with the dot on the bin pours the bag (about 2.2 s for any bag). Holds ~47.7 L (~19000 9 mm).
- Sounds (foley brass group): picks = baked tinks pitched by calibre, then a muffled bag rattle (duller as it fills) + canvas burst; bag taken / hung back = cloth recording; pour = tink stream through the bin's soundAt voice every 0.07 s, steel knocks while the bin is nearly empty, a sliding hiss.
- Shown: watch face ("BRASS n" instead of the depth line), terminal GEAR (bag per calibre, % full; bin count and value), store SUPPLIES (brassSupplies: the three restock rows shorter + SELL BRASS: per calibre, total at $0.50 a case; SELL empties the bin, walletEarn, playBuy; part cents carry to the next sale). Underfoot (step 92) also tinks on brass at rest (brassUf in ufBrass); no kicks for it.
- Saved: cqc.brass.v1 { bag[6], bin[6], cents, sold }. Room brass is session memory like holes. ?brass=off: no settling, collider changes, bag, bin or row (old behaviour). ?dev adds BRASS, BRASS_CAL, BRASS_BIN, brass* functions.
- Hooks (one line each, step 116 comments): CONFIG.brass, spawnCasing (2), updateCasings (2), tick, weaponsRoomActivated, weaponsRoomUnloaded, ufBrass, deskToggleGrab, squeezeStart / squeezeEnd, wsDrawSupplies, termLoadout, watchDrawFace, ?dev.
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef, Node run of the page's own code with stubs (scratchpad /tmp/s116/test.mjs: calibres, merge to the cap with counts kept, heap volume solve, sell cents, collect in a turned room with hot / far / pile / bag full, VR pinch, underfoot, pile layout, pour timing and a full bin). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look of the bag, bin and heap, grab reach near the floor, the tip-and-pour feel, sounds, frame time with many cases.
## CQC step 118: night vision and IR (CONFIG.nvg, ?nvg=off; headset check needed)
- Item: dual-tube PVS-31 class goggle. GUNSMITH computer SUPPLIES gets a 4th row (rows packed tighter when there are more than 3; a supply may carry its own draw / buy): BUY $1500 plus chips TUBE green / white phosphor, LASER visible / IR, LIGHT white / IR. Bought = on the personal locker shelf beside the gloves (wear item sub 'nvg', shared kit geometry: wearFree skips userData.shared); take it (grip / E) = worn on your head, again = back on the shelf. Saved in cqc.nvg.v1 (owned, worn, phos, irLaser, irLight).
- Mount (nvgKit / nvgModel, KIT materials, no new program): helmet shroud + receiver fixed on the camera, a swinging part on a hinge at the brow (NVG_H, head space): arm, bridge, power knob, two tubes 64 mm apart, objective bells and lenses, eyecups. Flip 0.38 s (cubic), up = 1.62 rad (objectives pointing up in front of the brow, eyecups ~19 deg over your line of sight); hidden when down (inside the 3 cm near plane). Headset: an empty hand squeezing the grip within 13 cm of the hinge or the tubes; computer: N (Shift+N kicks while worn; nvgKey in the main key handler). Latch / detent clicks, a faint power-up whine.
- Picture: three's ACES function is patched at load (nvgToneChunk, right after the renderer): exposure < 0 = nvgTone (luminance weighted 0.36 / 0.48 / 0.16, 1 - exp(-1.35 L) over a 1.2 % background glow, phosphor colour, burnt highlights toward white; white phosphor = exposure + 100). nvgPre sets -(exposure x gain x power-up) for the frame (nvgPost restores, also in tickSafe's error path): every lit material in the headset, the desktop OutputPass and the viewmodel. Nothing recompiles.
- Gain: target 0.9 / (eyeLight + lux at your eyes from the dazzle model x 1/800 + muzzle / blast cd/m^2 x 0.02 + flashbang white x 40), held to 0.5..30; attack 30 ms, release 0.4 s, the frame draws with last frame's gain, so a flash washes the screen once (wash = flash x 0.0005 x gain) then gates (own pistol shot: 30 -> ~6, back to 90 % in ~1.1 s). Dark room = gain 30, lit room 0.9, safe room at night 1.8.
- Overlay core (NVG_FS_CORE, shared): field stops (40 deg tubes; computer two circles with axes +-6 deg, headset one per eye), rim darkening, fibre-bundle hexagons (~1.2 deg, fwidth-antialiased edges), scintillation grain + bright speckle scaled by gain, wash, halos (core + ring) round the dazzle model's point sources and the muzzle flash. Headset: NVG.vr, an eye-locked triangle (DZ_VS, layers 1-2, renderOrder 100001, premultiplied, early out outside the tube). Computer: nvgRender after dazzleRender copies the framebuffer (FramebufferTexture, remade on resize only) and redraws it through the core with a slight rim barrel (uProj), 8-tap bloom and a re-tint to the phosphor (sprites that skip tone mapping turn green too). The dazzle overlay hides while the tubes are down (its sources feed the gate). Both programs compiled at load.
- IR: one mode for every laser, one for every weapon light. Long press (CONFIG.nvg.hold 0.6 s): fingertip held on the switch (switchTips: nvgSwitchDown / nvgSwitchHold, tip not lifted past re-arm), computer hold F / T (F no longer key-repeats); the device is left on in the new mode. IR laser: laserCast returns early (nothing drawn, no ray) unless the tubes are down; down, every laser slot's dot (one cloned SpriteMaterial, same program) and beam (step 98 ribbon uniforms; the old tube's shared colour) take the phosphor colour, an IR one brighter with beam base 0.35 in clean air. IR light: after handShadowTick, nvgBeam sets the same flashlight to 0 (HS.base 0: no glare; haze beam off; rail glow cones and the viewmodel lamp off; lit lenses WM.lensOff) or x0.25 flood with the tubes down. No light added or removed.
- AI: nightCanSee ignores your light and your lasers when they are IR (the men have no night vision); a white light, a visible laser and your muzzle flash still give you away.
- Hooks (one line each, "step 118"): CONFIG.nvg, nvgToneChunk after the renderer, tick (nvgTick after handShadowTick, nvgPre after eyeAdaptPre, nvgRender + nvgPost after dazzleRender), tickSafe, laserCast, nightCanSee (2), switchTips (2), keydown F / N, wsDrawSupplies, wsBuySupply, wearApply, wearTake, wearSpawn, wearFree, ?dev. Code: the STEP 118 section after nightCanSee.
- ?dev adds NVG, NVG_C, nvgTick, nvgGain, nvgGainTarget, nvgNoiseK, nvgPre, nvgPost, nvgRender, nvgFlip, nvgTake, nvgBuy, nvgModeHold, nvgSetIr, nvgSetPhos, nvgBeam, nvgLaserLook, nvgLaserHide, nvgIr, nvgKit, nvgModel.
- Checked: module parse (node --check), eslint no-undef (only the usual browser globals), Node run of the page's own CONFIG.nvg / nvgGainTarget / nvgNoiseK / nvgEase and nvgToneChunk on three r170's chunk (scratchpad /tmp/s118/test.mjs: gains above, gate recovery, tone curve stays in 0..1, flipped-up pose). GLSL not compiled anywhere; not run in a browser or on Quest. Known: things drawn without tone mapping (muzzle flash sprite, his lens glow) keep their colour in the headset; scope pictures in picture are not intensified; tracked hands (no gamepad) cannot flip the mount; enemies never wear night vision.
## CQC step 120: Beretta 92X (?b92=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Model: the user's cqc/models/src/beretta92x.glb (Sketchfab "Beretta 92X - 9mm Pistol" by KaL-ABIZZARE, **CC-BY-4.0: credit needed**; the packed GLB keeps it in asset.extras). tools/pack_beretta.py -> cqc/models/beretta92x.glb (1.16 MB from 5.5 MB): already metres at real size (nose to tang 215 mm), baked into design space (source minus (0, 0.12382, 0.04472): bore = the barrel's muzzle ring, the grip's web on the Glock's), so EXTG.b92 loads with no transform (extLoad's new `baked` flag). Nodes: frame (+ magwell, grip screws, mag release, trigger bar, guide rod, takedown lever), grips, slide (+ sights, slide-mounted decocker), threaded barrel, hammer, trigger, catch (slide stop), mag (body, base plate, follower), rounds (one 9x19 in the lips), wrap, dot, glass. WebP 1024 (gun emissive sight inserts lossless), opaque. The model's compensator and weapon light are not packed (the game's comp / cans / X300 / DBAL carry the code).
- Gun (WEAPON_DEFS.b92, from the pistol def): 17 rounds, 0.95 kg, softer recoil than the Glock (kick 0.8, climb 9.5), muzzle on the thread's front (-0.1531), sight line 16.6 mm (front sight top), phys boxes / pick box for the vise. b92Anim (createGun anim): DA/SA hammer (down on a new gun, rises with a double-action pull, falls on the shot or a dry break and stays down until the trigger resets, the slide cocks it), cocked trigger rests 45 % back, slide stop up when locked back; the barrel stays put. Slide = parts.slide. Magazine: its own (b92MagModel; rake 15.6 deg = magRot 0.272), MAG_DEFS.b92 measured at load; store 20-round = the body stretched 22 mm (b92MagDress / b92MagApply, like the 1911's).
- Hands: the Glock's fits turned 0.14 rad (8 deg: the 92's grip is that much more upright) about the web and lowered 11 mm (higher bore): HAND_FIT.b92 (shift / rot), VMH_FIT.b92 and VMH_FIT98.b92 = that turn x the Glock's (no run-time solve). Desktop: VM.byType pistol2 (VMH_FIT.b92.R places the mirror). Thumb rest lowered with the hand.
- Sounds: the Glock takes (HREC pistol family) at PIST_RATE 0.97; shots 'pistol'. 9 mm everywhere: brass (BRASS_TYPE), penetration round (CONFIG.ballistics.gun), barrel heat (HEAT.E). Magazines in the 9 mm pouches: inv.b92 (3, refilled with the Glock's), the HUD's 9mm count sums both.
- Gun locker: third pistol peg (GL.pistols; slots, peg and plate measured as for the others), two 92X magazines on the shelf. Enemy pistols drop a 92X 20 % of the time (B92.drop).
- Attachments: the wrap-around accessory rail (the model's mount: clamps the frame rail, wraps the slide, a Picatinny rail under the dust cover and an optic plate over the slide) is store hardware b92.wrap, owned (on) by default. The 92X has no optic cut, and no plate was made: the model's red dot rides the wrap's plate (frame-mounted: still while the slide runs), its lens drawn by the reticle shader (g17GlassMat 3 MOA, RETICLE brightness); g.sightY 30.7 mm with it. Step 112: one rail face (b92RailFace: the wrap's bottom rail, else the frame's own; base planes, spans and 3 recoil slots measured from the packed GLB). Muzzle: the step 103 chain on the threaded barrel (the Glock's MD3 / SB100 / comp; a rifle can only in front of a device: b92MuzRefuse). Grip panels: cosmetic overlays (walnut, black G10, OD G10) on the grip triangles (b92Grips).
- Store: WS_GUNS.b92 (Optic, Muzzle, Tactical (multi), Magazine, Grip Panels; listed after the Glock), WS_ITEMS b92.* (wrap, dot, sup, sup2, comp, light, laser, mag20, 3 panels), WS_REAL.b92 on B92_FX (afxOf / afxStats / afxDeriveFx), RAIL_ROWS / RAIL_FXT b92, B92_MUZ (store muzzle row = the chain's first device). First visit fits the dot (cqc.b92.v1). Vise RELEASE writes the parts back (b92FromGun). Paint groups: frame and controls, slide and barrel, grip panels.
- Hooks (one line each, step 120 comments): extLoad (baked), boot (b92Load), createGun (anim, b92SyncGun), buildMagModel, createMag, wbClose, attRefresh, muzRefresh, muzFx counted, muzBenchFit refusal, railFaces, RAIL_ROWS / RAIL_FXT, enemyDropType, refillInventory, invLabel, and table entries (ballistics gun, BRASS_TYPE, HEAT.E, PIST_RATE, VM / VMH byType, VMH_FIT, VMH_FIT98, GL, WS_GUN_ORDER, WS_GUNS, WS_ITEMS, WS_REAL, GUN_PAINT_GROUPS). ?dev adds B92, B92_FX, b92* functions.
- Checked: module parse (node --check), eslint no-undef (browser globals only); Node run on the page's own code and the packed GLB (scratchpad /tmp/b92/test.mjs, 26 checks): baked nodes, bore centred, def.muzzle on the barrel, both rails' undersides where B92.rail says, X300 / DBAL free on slots 1 and 2 of both rails (slot 0: clamp off the front), not in the gun, hands or sight line; the hand-fit turn maps the Glock's web onto the 92's and VMH_FIT stays rigid; the magazine stands upright after magRot, its top round inside it, the 20-round body 22 mm longer; b92Anim DA pull / fall / recock / SA rest / dry click / locked back; store bars (kicks less, heavier than the Glock; can and dot effects). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, the hand fit on the grip and the index on the long DA trigger, the hammer / slide stop pivots by eye, rail devices on the wrap, sounds.

## Release QA pass (headless Chromium, SwiftShader)
- Ran (Playwright, `--use-angle=swiftshader`, python http.server; scratch scripts in /tmp/qa, a persistent driver page per session): CQC `?dev` load (paint preset for every gun type and group in localStorage: solid, camo and wood finishes; wallet; night vision owned and worn), `?safe=1` load, a plain load; PLAY on computer; every locker gun (Glock 17, Beretta 92X, M1911, AKM, M4A1-S, AWM) taken, racked, fired through the trigger, aimed (desktop scopes on the M4 ACOG / AWM), light and laser toggled, reloaded; the GUNSMITH store opened on every gun (BUILD view rendered, PARTS, FINISH pages with camo swatches), every WS_ITEMS row bought and fitted in turn (grip panels, wrap rails, reticles, ACOG / 2x / 10x, cans, muzzle chains) with `renderer.compile(scene)` after each; heat glow on every can; night vision flipped down (green / white phosphor, IR modes); dazzle / NVG / build view overlay materials compiled in their own scenes; brass collected, poured into the bin, sold; SUPPLIES bought; walked into room 1, men shot (wounds, head shot kill), frag and flashbang thrown. After each step every program in `renderer.info.programs` was checked for LINK_STATUS, and FRAME_ERR, page errors and 404s were logged. Studio `index.html` (range.js: setup, spawn every gun, score) and Interstate (to its menu) loaded.
- Failed and fixed: step 106 paint put the camo injection on unlit MeshBasicMaterial gun parts (`objectNormal` undeclared: vertex shader compile error, "program not valid" every frame on 3 meshes). paintMesh now finishes MeshStandard / Physical materials only.
- Polish fixed: the desktop help panel (shown when a run starts) had developer text ("Step 11: ...", "Step 59: the M1911 lies on the bench", "Step 10: polish", the layout score line) and stale keys (N = kick only): rewritten for players with the current controls (selector, holster / sling, IR hold, night vision, injector, watch, gun locker, GUNSMITH computer, surrender). "Show project rules" only with ?dev. Start watchdog no longer blames cdn.jsdelivr.net (three / Rapier are vendored). Store: "Nothing for this slot yet" / "no parts ... yet" / "(coming with each gun's step)" / "ATTACHMENTS COMING" / the M16A1 fit message reworded. Interstate menu tag said "Build 1.17": now 1.40.
- Not failing: no other shader, page or frame errors; no 404s in CQC or Interstate (Studio: `/__studio/session` and the optional `claude.config.json`, both expected). Every store row has a model now (no MODEL COMING visible). Known harness limits: ~7 fps in SwiftShader, so trigger pulls only register when a frame lands inside the pulse; the page crashed once after ~1 h of continuous testing (renderer OOM, as in earlier notes), a fresh page passed the same steps. Not testable here: anything in a headset (XR session, hands, VR overlays drawn, Quest frame time), audio by ear.
- New docs/RELEASE_NOTES.md (player-facing: controls, features, known issues, credits incl. the Beretta 92X CC-BY-4.0 by KaL-ABIZZARE).
## CQC step 121: tracer rounds (CONFIG.tracer, ?tracer=off; headset check needed)
- Which: every 5th round (CONFIG.tracer.every; a counter per gun / per man starting at a random place in the mix) out of the rifles in CONFIG.tracer.guns (one table, gun type -> cartridge: M16A1 / M4A1-S 5.56, AKM / AKM-N 7.62x39, AWM .338). Pistols, buckshot and frag fragments never. His rifles too (his: true). GUNSMITH SUPPLIES row "Tracer mix" 1:5 / OFF (free, own draw in both the plain and the step 116 brass layouts via s.rowDraw), saved in cqc.tracer.v1 { mix }.
- Path: trcArm (fireGun / enemyShoot, before the pellet loop) arms the next trace; traceBullet now wraps traceBullet1 (the step 119 wrapper, unchanged) with trcBegin / trcEnd; traceBullet0 hands each straight piece to trcSeg (into a body, out of range, stopped by a surface with its normal, or through: step 119's turns and wall gaps are in it). Up to 6 pieces per tracer, fixed typed arrays, pool of 24 (oldest reused).
- Flight: s(t) = Lk ln(1 + v0 t / Lk), v0 from CONFIG.ballistics.rounds, Lk per cartridge (drag: 1000 / 900 / 2000 m). Dark until it lights 10-15 m out (ignite, ramp 3 m): shots under that show nothing. Streak = one frame of travel (persist, capped 7 m), never back past the light-up point or its own piece; burns out ~900 m (fade 60 m). A stopped round may ricochet (trcRicochet: chance 0.02 head-on up to 0.35 at a full graze, |cos| under 0.5; mirrored then flattened and scattered, one Rapier ray, max 40 m, x0.6 brightness, 3 sparks at the glance); else it dies in 7 hot sparks (TRC_SPARK, impact pool via fxEmit). Into a body or out of range: no sparks.
- Drawing: one Mesh, n camera-facing quads (head = position, tail = aA, aK brightness, 0 = collapsed off clip), ShaderMaterial additive, HDR red with a hot core (gain 26 computer for the post bloom, 7 headset where it is tone mapped in the shader, so the step 118 NVG tone map turns it green), at least minPx 1.4 px wide (dimmer when thinner). Built at weaponsPrecompile (trcPrecompile) and always in the scene, so it compiles with the warm-up. polishUpdate -> trcTick (idle = early out), polishShift -> trcShift. No light, no per-frame allocation.
- NVG: lit streaks add nvgI x k / d^2 to the gate's flash input (trcNvgF in nvgGain). AI: in the dark a lit tracer of yours shows where you fired from for seeMemory 3 s to men within seeRange 40 m of that spot (trcSeen in nightCanSee). His tracers show you where he shoots from by themselves.
- Not done: smoke trail, haze / LAIR tint, NVG halos for tracers.
- Hooks (one line each, step 121 comments): traceBullet0 (3 trcSeg), traceBullet, fireGun, enemyShoot, weaponsPrecompile, polishUpdate, polishShift, nightCanSee, nvgGain, brassSupplies. ?dev: window.__cqcTracer.
- Checked: module parse (node --check), eslint no-undef (XR browser globals only); Node run of the section with three r170 and stubs (scratchpad /tmp/s121/test.mjs: 2 tracers in 10 AKM shots, none for the Glock, a 40 m shot through a wall lit from 12.5 m and ended in sparks at the far wall, a 6 m shot never drawn, graze ricochets ~1 in 3, quads cleared after). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, bloom level, NVG brightness.

## Release polish pass 2 (CQC, fix pass, no new features)
- Can heat vs finish: the glow clone keeps heatBase / heatIt (heatGlowMat / heatGlowInstall). heatGlowDrop puts the source material back (and the viewmodel mirror) when the can cools under matAt - 40 or leaves HEAT.list; paintMesh reads through a glow clone (PAINT.orig never records it, a finish change drops it and swaps in the new finish; a still-hot can gets a fresh clone of that next tick). Checked headless: hot can -> glow on the factory material, APPLY carbon -> glow on the painted clone, cooled -> painted clone, RESET -> factory.
- Normal maps: every pack arm mesh has vertex tangents (GLB check), so GLTFLoader left normalScale.y positive; the glove104 hand and the vmhBake gun hands (both baked without tangents) now flip normalScale.y on their own material clones, as the step 109 sleeves do. Gloves, gun hands and sleeves read the same way (not checked by eye).
- Surrender: a man who gave up is out of the squad code (callout, shared contact, cover claims, man-down radio and morale, reload radio, cover-me, mates list, door alert); check fire still treats him as a body in the line while he stands, not once he kneels. Left in a room that unloads: surrSave -> roomMem(room).surr, surrRestore on activation (unarmed, hands up / kneeling / tied as he was, plan.dead stays, no second score). Respawn leaves him where he is, still surrendered (no reset to home). Checked headless: surrStart, save, remove, restore (kneeling, no gun, same spot), then ticking with no errors.
- Night vision in the headset: nvgUntoned (from nvgPre) gives the un-tone-mapped sprites (WM.flash, NIGHT.glow torch glows, NADE fire / flash) the phosphor colour at their own luminance while the tubes are down, original colours back after; a colour switch on a change, no recompile. The computer path is unchanged (its copy pass re-tints the frame).
- PC: R during a reload (or a busy gun) no longer resets every loose object in loaded rooms (only R with empty hands does, as the help says). Store: a light / laser row fitted on a gun with no rail face (an M4A1-S without quad or clamp rail) is unfitted by railSync with a message, instead of reading "fitted" with nothing on the gun (a bench-clamped one counts).
- Cleanup: the Netlify comment at the top of cqc/index.html removed; room QC, streaming wait and run summary console lines only with ?dev (a plain load and a run start log nothing but three's KHR_parallel_shader_compile warning in SwiftShader).
- PC sweep, measured headless (?dev, SwiftShader, Playwright keyboard / pointer-locked mouse): E drops / takes, CapsLock toggles crouch, Space held = ADS, R reloads, T / F tap toggles, held 0.6 s switches IR (device left on), N flips worn NVG, Shift+N does not; workshop by mouse: slot box opens, BUILD drag holds the view (mouse turns the gun, not the look; synthetic movementX), PARTS and FINISH tabs, finish APPLY. Desktop ADS: every optic's reticle glass sits on the camera axis at full ADS (0 mrad; the M4 holo window centre 2 mm high, its reticle is parallax-free): Glock / 1911 / 92X dot, AKM dot / holo / 2x, M4 dot / holo; ACOG and both AWM scopes switch to the full-screen scope (fov 72 / power). FOV 60 and 100: aimed fov 47.5 / 84.6, viewmodel fov follows. Final scan: 235 programs linked, no FRAME_ERR, no page errors, no 404s.
- Found, not fixed (listed in docs/RELEASE_NOTES.md): GLB attachment parts (dots, holo, ACOG, M4 / pistol cans) are skipped by the ATTACHMENTS finish (paintAttOk: their roughness factor is 1, maps carry it); NVG does not intensify scope pictures. Not testable here: anything in a headset, sounds by ear.

## Studio range part 2 (addons/range.js; first run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Run headless (Chromium + SwiftShader, scratch /tmp/rq): setup, all five guns spawned by command, picked up through the engine's desktop path (pick + E), fired hip and aimed, reloaded, racked, selector, auto, paper and steel at every distance, Props cards, palette, every Range command. Frames were ~1 fps under shared CPU, so most checks ran the engine's frame without drawing (desktop update, physics step, S.guns updates).
- Bugs fixed: the recoil spring was one Euler step a frame and blew up on a long frame (dt 0.05): the held gun flew off and was dropped (sub-stepped 4 ms + runaway guard). Aiming a rifle moved its hold target 0.40-0.45 m (hip to cheek) in one frame and the engine dropped it (hold error > 0.45); a desktop-held range gun now passes through the room (collision groups: props only) and `physics.holdError` is wrapped so a ghosted gun only counts as lost past 1.5 m. Rifles picked up while looking at the bench no longer jam and drop. Four target lines were blocked (25 m and 50 m steel behind the 5 m paper / 10 m steel, 300 m behind 100 m): new layout (near targets low, far ones spread), checked offline from the lane at eye 1.5-1.7 m and +-10 cm, all through the open middle pane. Time of flight and impact speed were the whole frame's (interpolated now: 9 mm 5 m 14 ms, .338 200 m 234 ms / 808 m/s). Hip fire ignored the targets (not in Rapier) when finding the aim point. Fire rate and reload follow-ups used wall-clock time / setTimeout (now game time). Racking ejected nothing; the AWM's spent case stays until the bolt. eyePoint / recoilMatrix / auto fire allocated every frame. Commands registered after `ready` (api.commands now).
- Casings: one InstancedMesh (96, 10-sided case scaled per calibre, brass), ejected right / up / back from the port with spin and the gun's velocity; arc + Rapier ray along each quick step (8 a frame, guns excluded) for walls, bench, floor; restitution 0.38, lies down on its side; synthesized brass tink (duller on the bench). Live rounds racked out fly slower. Cleared by reset / put away.
- AWM scope: lens positions measured from its glass at load. VR: `renderScope` (the engine's hook before the main render) draws a scope camera at the objective into a 256 (Quest) / 384 px half-float target shown on the eyepiece disc (ShaderMaterial: picture, mil-dot reticle, exit pupil darkening off the axis), only with your head 2-30 cm behind it and within 5 cm of the axis, fov = 2 atan(r / eye distance) / mag (true magnification). Computer: aiming fills the screen (DOM circle + mil-dot SVG, magnification label), camera fov from your fov / mag, gun hidden, mouse turn scaled 1/mag; wheel (and the VR stick via the engine) zooms 3-25x, default 6x. Zero 100 m; 300 m needs one mil dot of hold (tested: centre hold misses low, one dot hits).
- Scoreboard: canvas board (0.8 x 0.5 m) left of the lane ahead of the bench, redrawn only when a shot / hit changes it: shots, hits, accuracy, steel, points, last 5 hits (ring, distance, gun, time of flight, speed).
- Recovery: guns take a free bench slot; a gun below the floor (or far out) goes back to its slot (checked twice a second); "put the guns back" (new command return_guns, registry regenerated: 130 / 118 ready), Props card and palette entry; "put the guns away" also cancels guns still loading and clears casings; at most 8 guns.
- API adds returnGuns, targets, commands. Not verified: anything in a headset (scope picture, VR ghosting is desktop only), sounds by ear, Quest frame time.
## Release polish pass 3 (CQC, fix pass, no new systems)
- Attachment finish on model parts: paintAttOk judged roughness / metalness by the factors only, and the GLB parts (red dots, holo, 2x, ACOG body, M4 / MD3 / SB100 / .45 cans, ATS5, comps, flash hider) keep both in maps with factors of 1, so they read as rubber and were skipped. paintMapMean reads each map's mean once (64 px copy, cached per texture; null until decoded) and the test uses factor x mean; chrome now also needs a bright base colour (factor x map mean, lum > 0.25), so dark blued steel (M4 comp, bipod legs) takes the finish while screws, reflectors, glass, lenses, reticles (ShaderMaterial), emissive and rubber parts never do. Checked headless: every GLB / spec housing painted, those excluded; a hot painted can (AKM flash hider, AWM can) glows on the painted clone and goes back to it when cool.
- Night vision and scope pictures: no change needed. The PiP targets (SCOPE, M4 ACOG, AKM 2x) hold linear HDR and the eyepiece materials tone map in their own shader, so with the tubes down the tube exposure (nvgTone, same gain) applies to them. Measured: a dim picture reads 3/255 normally, (53, 103, 31) green / (87, 95, 103) white phosphor. The release notes' known issue was wrong and is removed.
- Slide lock: locksOpen(def) = every pistol or def.lockOpen. WEAPON_DEFS.rifle (so M4A1-S and AWM) and smg (MPX) lockOpen: true, akmn false. finishRack / fireGun use it. A locked M4 shows its charging handle forward (only slide, recip and bolt guns draw the action back when locked); a VR grab on its handle must pull all the way back (g.racked only when the part is really back); B / Y releases a locked-open M4 / MPX bolt (gunButtonB). Checked headless, last round: Glock, 92X, 1911, M4 lock; AKM closes; AWM locks open when the bolt is worked on an empty magazine; R releases and chambers.
- Press check: nothing existed except the M1911's own chambered round (parts.round). chkRoundTick (updateGun, after the slide pose): while the action is held part way back by a hand (VR rack hand, desktop task) past CHK.min of travel (a bolt: past its lift) and short of the extraction point (90 %), a brass case of the gun's calibre (step 116 lathe, under g.model so the viewmodel mirror shows it and vmHideGun hides it) shows at the ejection point, along the bore; VR gets a soft tick when it comes into sight. Letting go short of 90 % never ejected (unchanged). Computer: Shift+V (deskCheck -> chamberCheckTask: 22 % of travel, a bolt lifted and 12 mm back, held 0.5 s, toast "Chambered" / "Empty" / "Spent case in the chamber" / "Empty: locked open"; pump guns and locked actions: the line only). Checked headless on all six guns: 22 % (AWM 31 %), case shown, round still chambered, magazine untouched. Case position is the ejection point: seen in the Glock's port in a close-up, not checked by eye on the others or in a headset.
- Tracers (step 121) run for the first time: ?dev, AKM and M4A1-S fired in the safe room: paths recorded (AKM 1.3 m into the locker: never lit, as designed under 10 m), streaks drawn, its program links, a synthetic 30 m path lights, ends and clears with sparks, no errors. Nothing to fix.
- Walk-through: help row for Shift+V; release notes (controls, VR press check, B / Y bolt release, features line, two known issues removed, the finish one reworded). Seen, not changed: at ~1 fps (SwiftShader) a desktop rack can stop at 86 % of travel and not cycle (frame-rate artifact of the eased slide; fine at real frame rates).
- Harness: one browser at a time matters: a second agent's browser shares the 14 GB memory cgroup, and a stale driver process picking up commands looked like crashes. Rendering the scene into a new render target compiles a second program set (~6 GB in SwiftShader); measure on the canvas instead.
- Final scan (?dev, PLAY, all store parts fitted, press checks, slide locks, reloads, tracers, heat): 248 programs linked, no FRAME_ERR, no page errors, no 404s; plain load: no errors, no 404s. Not testable here: the headset (VR rack partial pull, tick, B / Y), sounds by ear.

## CQC: M4A1-S white fix
- Cause: step 105's m4Cut gave the carry handle, round handguard and stock their own geometries (X.geo) but no material (X.mat[k] undefined), so extBuild's `new THREE.Mesh(geo, undefined)` drew them with three's default white MeshBasicMaterial (unlit, no map). On a fresh save the M4A1-S showed a white handle, handguard and stock in the room, in VR and in the desktop viewmodel mirror (it shares materials); finishes skipped them too (paintMesh only takes lit materials). The paint system was not involved.
- Fix: m4Cut sets X.mat[k] = X.mat.body for M4_CUT_KEYS (one texture).
- Checked headless (fresh storage, ?dev): every M4 mesh MeshStandardMaterial with the 1024 map; close-up mean luminance 92 (5113 px over 200) -> 14.9 (0), AKM 8.3, Glock 2.4 in the same light; FDE finish covers the cut parts (paint clones), RESET puts the factory material back; flat-top, quad rail, dot, SOPMOD, VFG fitted look right; viewmodel mirror 29 meshes, none untextured. Syntax parse OK.

## CQC: store-only kits, bench lamp, longer flashlight (headset check needed)
- Kits: no bench kit cards (EXT_STORE; extKitSpawn clears any old set; ?kitcards=on brings them back). The 33 / 40 / 60-round magazines and the auto sear are GUNSMITH rows (g17.mag33, akmn.mag40, m4a1s.mag60, g17.sear: now a normal 'trigger' slot row, FIT / REMOVE, no longer hardware). EXT stays their state: cqc.ext.v1 is read at load and copied into WORKSHOP.fitted, a store change writes it back (extSearSet switches every Glock).
- Bench lamp (BLAMP, ?benchlamp=off): wall bracket, sprung two-link arm, LED bar head over the vise (room x 2.72, 1.53 m, 0.5 m out) with an emissive diffuser and a rocker. One SpotLight (benchLampInit after initLightPool, always in the scene, castShadow false, no map, range 1.7 m, 6.5 game cd); off / room not shown = intensity 0. The model hangs on the current safe room's group (benchLampRoom from activateRoom), the light follows the head (benchLampTick). Switch: E / click on it (benchLampDesk first in termDeskPress), VR fingertip on the rocker (VRTIP) or a grip squeezed within 12 cm of the head (benchLampGrip in squeezeStart after door levers); btnSfx('light') click + haptic.
- Flashlight: CONFIG.light.flashlight 78 x gain 1.6 = 125 at the hot spot = 20k cd (DZ cd 160), distance 0 (no cut-off, was 15 m), decay 2, shadow far 70; beam map weights spill / corona / hot 0.08 / 0.22 / 0.7 (FLASH_BEAM.w, dzProf uses them). flashPeak() feeds setFlashlight, HS.base, nvgBeam; a PL-2 leading is x0.55 (it.lum). Dazzle splashR 3 -> 50 m; NVG srcFallback scales with the intensity; haze / motes beam falloff gentler. His torches: torchReach 50 m (spot distance + shadow far), torchRange 16 still his seeing distance.
- Checked: module parse (node --check), eslint no-undef (browser globals only). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the lamp's look and placement, the beam at range, shadow precision with far 70.
## CQC: attachments collide (doors, walls)
- ?attclip=off = exactly as before (headset check needed). ATC section (end of the module): a held gun's fitted parts (visible meshes under g.model added after createGun's atcFactory tag; magazine, slings, AWM bipod legs, additive / no-depth meshes skipped) become up to 8 boxes from their drawn vertices in gun-root space (atcBuild: parts inside def.col dropped, near-free unions merged, then cheapest merges). Rebuilt only on a change: attRefresh / muzClipSet / railSync mark it dirty, a node signature every 300 ms on a held gun catches the rest, setDynamic (let go / dropped) refreshes it.
- gunFreeAt (so gunClipWorld, gunDropFree, the desktop path): the boxes against the step 30 set (walls, cover, heavy props), and every box plus the gun's own box against door leaves, shrunk by ATC.tol 12 mm: the step 8.1 probe now carries the same boxes (+4 mm) and pushes a free leaf first; a leaf at its stop holds the gun. gunTouch's surface falls back to what a part / leaf touched (leaf = metal knock).
- Loose gun: the boxes ride its body as zero-density colliders in p.extra (setKinematic / setDynamic switch them); its extra parts now also meet leaves (ATC.rest).
- Held loose part (kind att, VR or carried on the computer): atcItemClip = the empty-hand / step 30 search in its own frame against walls, cover, heavy props and leaves, with gunTouch knocks and buzz. Thrown parts already had COLL.prop (doors included, CCD); side hits now knock (atcKnock from knockTick; not while kinematic / on a gun or peg).
- No per-frame allocation (shapes, colliders and the item's clip state made on a change / once). ?dev: ATC and atc* functions.
- Checked: module parse (node --check), eslint no-undef (browser globals only). Not run in a browser or on Quest.
## CQC: bench zoom (computer)
- BZ (after scopeDeskView, ?benchzoom=off = old behaviour; COMFORT "Bench zoom" on the height row, comfort.benchZoom, default on): a gun clamped in the vise, empty hands, pointer locked, within BZ.near 1.4 m of the vise: the camera eases 0.4 s (cubic in-out) to a three-quarter view from your side of the bench (el 0.58, az 0.42 rad), the gun's glBounds filling half the width, aim nudged toward the table parts, fov 40-45.
- bzPre (top of tick) puts your own pose back, bzApply (before the draw) lays the zoom over it, so walking / look / stance are untouched. Body, hanging hands and viewmodel hidden while k > 0. Room-local maths via rLX / rWX / rWZ (turned safe rooms).
- Mouse moves a free cursor (look held, edge of the screen pans along the bench); click / E picks through the zoomed view (bzPick): table part = fit (wbDeskAtt via grab), part on the gun = take off, gun = take it out, VISE cap = RELEASE.
- Leaves: WASD or Esc (dismissed until re-clamp or stepping 1.7 m away), gun removed, walking away, picking something up.
- Checked: module parse; one headless load (?dev): no page errors, handle __cqcBenchZoom. Not checked by eye or in play (framing per gun, cursor picking).
## CQC: any attachment on any gun (syntax parse only; headset check needed)
- Bench: no cross-gun refusals. wbAttRelease / wbDeskAtt no longer toast "Made for the Glock / AKM / AWM / M4"; a gun's own fit (g17 / akm / m19 / m4 / awm BenchFit) returns null when the part is not its own or the gun lacks the interface (plate, kit, rail, flat-top, quad rail, bipod rail, stock module, 3-25x scope), so the part takes the generic wbSnap onto the gun's surface (step 74) inside its pick box. Light / laser: rail faces first (step 112), else the snap. Slot-taken rules kept (rail full, one optic / grip / stock, cover needs a dot, killflash order).
- Muzzle: muzBenchFit keeps only the MUZ.max limit and "base device on the bare barrel"; thread, calibre and interface refusals (akm / m19 / awm / b92MuzRefuse, Glock thread) are no longer called from it.
- Store: a slot lists the gun's own rows, then every other gun's modelled non-hardware rows of that category ("(<gun> part)"), paged ▲▼ by WS_SLOTS_MAX. Buying one ignores its home gun's requirements and does not auto-fit (it goes to the bench table); owned = "AT VISE" (fit it at the bench). Stats / afx unchanged: a foreign part fitted by the snap adds no afx entry (generic default = none).
- Not done: g.sightY for a snapped foreign optic (sight line stays the gun's own), auto-made risers / wrap rails as models.
