- Trees (TREE knobs in buildSnowWorld): the packed list (10637, some stacked on one spot) is a seed, filtered by a spacing hash (min trunk distance = spaceK 0.085 x height each); a jittered 3.4 m grid fills a 150 m band along every road (coarse 8 m distance-to-road field), clumps / clearings by value noise, denser near the road, clear 13.5 m of the centre line (past the shoulder) and 32 m of the service park, slopes under 0.85. Seeded RNG (same forest each load). Base on the lowest ground under the trunk, random yaw, height and width scale. One InstancedMesh per 192 m chunk (frustum culled), chunks beyond the draw distance hidden (treeCull: 1100 m computer, 300 m headset); trees grow in at 80-100 % of it in the vertex shader (uTreeFar; not in the shadow depth pass). Collision radius from height. Console logs the count.
- Snow: two camera-local wrapping boxes of instanced quads (near 15 m: 9000 / 3600 headset, far 52 m: 5000 / 1800), flakes 1-6 mm, fall 0.5-1.5 m/s, flutter, integrated wind with gusts (snowUpdate), stretched along the flake's motion relative to the camera over 1/45 s (streaks only at speed), sub-pixel flakes keep 1.4 px and lose alpha, near / far fade, slight sun forward scatter. Old 5000 point sprites removed.

- Workshop follow-up: glass 1.6 x 0.9 m at x 1.85, y 1.9; text x1.3 (WS_FONT via TERM.wsK in tFont, workshop only); 4 rows per slot page / build list; canvas fully cleared each redraw, scanlines removed and glass roughness 0.6 (the faint doubled letters: scanline aliasing in the mipmaps / glossy glass). Not seen by eye.
- City map removed (CITY section, cqc/city/, watch / M panel buttons, hooks).
- NVG: tube fov 95 deg, softer field stop (edge 0.12), grain / sparkle / hex pattern / bloom / halos about a quarter of before.

## CQC: cross-gun fit follow-up (sight lines, seating, default effects, multi-device, 2x / ACOG view)
- Sight line: optSight(g, own) after every gun's own refresh (g17 / akm / m19 / b92 / m4 / awm Refresh are wrappers round *Refresh0) and in attRefresh for guns without one. A snapped or foreign optic (it.snapped, or not the gun's own fit) gives g.sightY / g.sightX from its axis mesh (eyepiece / reticle / pip / glass bbox centre in g.model space), g.wr.eye on it, a per-gun ACOG SCOPE desc (objective from the item frame), a 2x picture on any gun (g.akLens; akmPipGun no longer AKM only). fireGun zeroes x too ((sightX, sightY) / zero). Taken off: own refresh values, or the saved ones (g._optOver).
- Seating (seatPart from wbSnap): optic = mount face 0.6 mm over the highest drawn point under its whole length (rays down through the gun's visible meshes, gunMeshes / gunSpan: over the iron sights too), centred, square to the bore; grip = top 0.6 mm under the lowest point under it, ahead of the magazine (>= 12 cm in front of the palm); stock = front just behind the palm. Lights / lasers keep rail faces / the surface snap. A snapped vertical foregrip becomes g.def.vfg / g.wr.vfg (step 23 support hold; g._def0 restores).
- Effects: foreignFx: each snapped non-muzzle part adds mass, kick / desk kick x M / (M + m), climb / sway / aim / ADS by its inertia about the grip (muzFx's rule).
- Multi-device: nearLightSwitch / nearLaserSwitch pick the nearest button (were first-in-reach, so a second identical device could never be switched alone). Rest of the g.lights / g.lasers code read: no other fault found.
- 2x (AKM): 512 px target, camera 20 deg (40 deg apparent), eye box 3 cm, relief tolerance 12 cm, softer thinner edge, anti-aliased reticle, eye window 6 cm. ACOG: afov 36, box 0.026, 512 px.
- Checked: node --check, eslint no-undef (browser globals only); Node run of the page's own optSight / seatPart / gunSpan with three (scratchpad sight.mjs): Glock+ACOG, AKM+Glock dot, AWM+holo, M4+2x, Glock+holo dropped crooked and 1 cm into the body: axis 0 mrad off the bore, mount 0.6 mm over the gun, POI = POA at the zero (0.000 cm). The 10x / 3-25x are not loose items (AWM store / factory), so "M4+10x" cannot be built yet.
- Not done: removable AWM 3-25x, desktop shot direction vs the drawn viewmodel barrel.

## CQC: trigger finger, two-gun hands, glove inside, doors, suppressed shots, bolt sounds (headset check needed)
- Trigger finger on the fitted (gloved) firing hand: vmhBake stores the index chain (3 joints + tip) and per-vertex weights (aIdxW); vmhFingFrame (vmhFit, side R) finds the hinge axis (knuckle line, + = curl) and three poses: along (straightened, parallel to the bore, a touch up: trigger discipline above the guard), on (pad on the trigger face, slightly open from the pack's idle curl), pull (curled). vmhFingPose (vmhTake) picks along / on->pull from the controller trigger (touched or value > 0.02; no gamepad = on the trigger; reload / rack = along), eased 22/s; the vertex shader bends the finger (VMH_FING_VS in gloveInside). Procedural hands already used HAND_FIT along / on / pull.
- Two guns of one type, one per hand: both took the single fitted 'R' mesh, so it followed the last hand and the other hand drew nothing. vmhTake now keeps one mesh per hand (S.hm; own materials and finger uniforms, shared geometry).
- Glove inside: gloveInside(mat) on the step 104 glove and every fitted gun-hand material: DoubleSide (cuff closed), back faces a flat dark lining with the normal to the eye, dithered fade within GLOVE_IN.near 5 cm of the eye. Program keys +|gloveIn(F).
- Doors: CONFIG.polish.audio.doors.gain 1 -> 0.5 (lever, spring, rattle, shut, stop, creak, recorded open / slam); kickSfx 'door' (kick / breach) x0.75.
- Suppressed shots x1.4: supPlay 1.25 -> 1.75, the rifle can layer 0.28 -> 0.39 (yours and his).
- AWM: HREC family 'bolt' (HREC_FAM.awp, any def.manual): AK takes pitched down for bolt back / home, AK / SMG magazine takes, new events lift / close from x64Anim's handle angle (dry-fire clicks / a soft high AK slam; synth 'selector' without the files). Cases already tink (brassTink), magazines use magDrop.
- Checked: node --check, eslint no-undef. Not run in a browser or on Quest: finger angles (computed, not seen), shader compile.
## CQC: workshop zoom, stairwell men
- Workshop zoom (WZ, computer, ?wszoom=off): the dot on the GUNSMITH glass within 1.6 m narrows the field of view (eased 0.35 s, about 0.62 m of glass fills the view height, min 22 deg); the centre ray does not move, so E / click aim as before. wzPre / wzApply beside bzPre / bzApply; off the glass 0.6 s = back. Not with the bench zoom.
- Stairwell men (CONFIG.stairs.men 0.5, men2 0.3): stairMenActivated (enemiesRoomActivated, kind 'stair') rolls per slot (fixed seed) 1 or 2 men, plan in room memory like a combat room's (dead stay dead). - Trays (wbTrays from wbSpawn, once per safe room): a black polymer bin (2 mm floor, 22 mm walls) under every table spot of every gun's list (WB_TABLE, WB_G17, WB_AKM, WB_M19, WB_M4, WB_AWM), sized to the gap to its neighbours, a white-on-charcoal label strip in front (store name of the part, else the kind), rims as static colliders. The light rides the head; the spot is kept (room-local) in every safe room.

## CQC step 122: trousers and boots look real, damp layer (BWEAR, ?bodywear=off; headset check needed)
- bodyWearShader(mat, kind) chains onBeforeCompile on BODY.mats pants (0), leather (1), rubber (2), nylon (3); key '|bw122-<kind>' (one program each, compiled with the body). Detail is computed in the body's rest space (identity bind: vBwP = position, ground y 0, ankles z 0, toes -z): trousers roughness / tone mottle, out- and inseam grooves with stitches, hem fold, knee and seat wear (lighter, smoother), creases behind the knee, dust low down; boot leather grain mottle, flex creases over the vamp, welt stitching, toe scuffs, polished toe (roughness 0.44, normal 0.7); rubber lug edge grooves, sole dust. ## CQC: removable AWM scopes, dead-code purge (not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- The AWM's factory 3-25x (awp.scope) and the 10x (awp.s10) are att parts (awmTpl 'scope' = the AWP's own scope meshes + glass + its eyepiece picture; 's10' as before), seated at the design origin (awmSeat), in AWM_BENCH (store FIT / REMOVE attaches / detaches them, RELEASE writes them back) and on the bench table (WB_AWM, x 1.45 / 0.9, z 2.34) when owned and off. The factory scope meshes are always hidden. awmOptic(g) = the scope part on that gun. One optic on the AWM (awmRefuse). Killflash / sunshade: only on a 3-25x part (any gun: they seat on the scope part's own frame), dropped by the store sync when it comes off.
- On other guns the scopes go through seatPart / optSight: it.scopeDesc + it.obL give the picture (P.scope, objective in that gun's frame) and the eye at the scope's relief behind the eyepiece. Node (sight.mjs): 3-25x on M4 / AKM, axis 0 mrad, POI = POA at the zero.
- Purge: unreferenced module bindings removed by an acorn + eslint-scope scan (refs from the ?dev lists not counted, names stripped from them), repeated to none: the old muzzle refusal helpers, *_NOT_HERE, the ?dev gap probes (g17 / akm / m19 Gaps), and the whole brass bag / pour / hand pickup code (brassGrab, brassRelease, brassDesk, bag build / tick, pour, rustle, collect, bin aim...). VMH_FIT98.m4a4 and WS_SKIP gone; sounds pstMagOut / pstMagIn deleted (unused). Viewmodel rigs pistol / makarov and vmLoad's armsFrom branch removed with cqc/models/vm/vm-pistol.glb, vm-makarov.glb, vm-saiga.glb (16 MB; nothing loaded them since step 89). The ?flag=off old paths were not removed (each is a switch the notes above document; cutting them is a larger, riskier pass).

## Interstate: patrol car fixes, light bar, siren, Pursuit and Wanted (headset check needed)
- One steering wheel: the GLB's own wheel and column (Interieur pieces inside GLB_CARS.police.strip, model space) are cut out of the drivable car's own geometry copies (buildGlbCar opt.own; the showroom keeps the model whole). The game's wheel (turns, pads, horn, key) sits on the model's measured wheel: body (-.3398, .9202, -.4162), tilt -.194, rim .16. WHEEL_R0 = .185 (built radius): the driver's hands and VR grabs use it (were CABIN.wheelR in scaled space).
- Mirrors: MIRROR_GLASS.police on the model's 'Miroir' glass (inner at the windscreen top, doors in their housings); mirror aim and the windscreen fit use the body's own eye (GLB_CARS.cabin.eye).
- Cabin: CABIN_LAYOUT.police (key, stalks on the game column, corner lamps, a console siren controller with LIGHTS / WAIL / YELP / HAZARD touch buttons). cab.beacon, cab.siren (0 / 1 wail / 2 yelp). Keys: J siren cycle (gamepad D-pad down), G hazard also runs the bar. GLB interior materials get cabinOcclusion; the A-pillar spotlight glass no longer glows with the headlamps.
- Lights: policeFlash / POLICE_FLASH_GLSL (2.4 s: quad flash then wig-wag), same on the drivable bar, grille flashers (Flash_light, split per side), rear white wig-wag (Feux_blanc) and the traffic patrol cars' bars. One PointLight (DriveWorld.beaconLight, always in the scene: no recompiles) flashes over your bar or the nearest traffic unit with lights on; x0.3 by day. cabinOcclusion now blocks point lights from above inside the cabin (CABIN_ROOF_GLSL) and, as "shell", the light right over the patrol car (its own beacon, within 2.4 m) on its own body.
- Siren audio: tools/cut_siren.py cuts interstate/sfx/siren-wail / siren-yelp (.m4a + .ogg, padded like the engine loops; SIREN_SFX) from freesound 692525 newlocknew (licence not verified, credited). VehicleAudio #sirens: your car from the roof (muffled in the cabin), up to 3 traffic units from DriveWorld.sirenSources (positional, Doppler from closing speeds, air low-pass, yelp within 45 m); voices built on demand, torn down after the fade.
- Chases (§17b ChaseMode, CHASE knobs; menu Pursuit / Wanted -> startRun(mode), which picks the car): TrafficSystem roles 'suspect' (fast, weaves by the longest clear lane) and 'cop' (closes, rams / leans on your rear quarter, boxes from ahead, free lateral steering only when nothing is alongside, sturdier in contacts), never recycled, recover from a stopped crash. Traffic moves over for a siren behind (#yield; your patrol car too). Pursuit: hold meter (close, faster when he is stopped / boxed / PIT-spun), escape after 20 s grace when > 420 m for 8 s or > 950 m, 240 s limit; contact with the suspect costs no chain. Wanted: busted under 16 km/h with a unit within 14 m for 3.5 s (-20 % score), escaped with nobody within 300 m for 8 s (heat +1: more, faster units, one ahead). Score bonus / penalty / note; chase bar (#chase-hud); notices via 'chase:note' (headset too).
- Checked: node --check, eslint no-undef; headless Chromium (SwiftShader, ~1 fps rendered, physics stepped directly for the AI runs): cockpit, mirrors, lights by night, console, Pursuit catch path, Wanted approach / bust / escape. Not checked: on Quest, by ear (siren loop seams measured offline), a human-driven chase.

## CQC: facility section (?facility=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Every 4-6 combat rooms (FAC.gap) the plan runs into 1-3 facility rooms (FAC.run; no kind twice in a row; a stairwell neither counts nor breaks a run; a range taking the slot postpones it). State rides on the plans (facGap / facLeft / facNeed via facPlanPick / facPlanTag in roomPlan). New ROOM_TYPES: corridor (pillared corridor, 4.4-5 x 24-30 m, h 3.3), checkpoint (security checkpoint, 13.2-14.6 x 9.4-10.6, h 3.3), ops (operations centre, 16.6-18.4 x 16.4-18.2, h 3.3), medical (medical wing, 7.6-8.6 x 6.6-7.4, h 3.0); men 3 / 3 / 4 / 2, normal squads. h stays <= 3.3 (floor-to-floor) so a wing over or under never overlaps.
- Code: the FACILITY SECTION after ROOM_TYPES. facParams (normalParams), facLayout (generateLayout: built to a plan, no furniture, props or partitions), facMaterials (roomMaterials: board-formed grey concrete walls with a dark band, white paint for medical; tile floors; RM.ceil = dark exposed soffit / concrete / tiles), facShell (the observation window's hole: the side wall's geometry replaced by wallWithOpening), facBuild (roomBuilder, before the batch: everything batched), facActivate (activateRoom: colliders, walk boxes, room.facNav, room.facMz), facCoverPoints (coverPoints), facFloor (roomFloorAt / updateFloor: the landing), roomAcoustic (type.acoustic). Troffers may carry y ([x, z, y], the island height) in roomBuilder now.
- Materials made once in facKit (end of buildFurnitureKit, in GEN.mats, programs shared with the paints / floors / column concrete / door / LED); KIT.proto.facBulk (bulkhead lamp). Panel joints, tie holes, floor tile joints (L.joints), rubber walkway strips.
- Corridor: 0.5 m columns (dark base band, rubber skirting) every ~4 m 30 cm off both walls, a 0.45 m beam over each pair (collider), troffers between the beams (the two nearest the middle lit by the pool), bulkheads, closed grey-blue side doors (fixed leaf, lever, vision panel, frame), two pipes and a cable tray behind the columns, sprinklers, double door at the exit (a fixed second leaf beside the real one).
- Checkpoint: 0.99 m landing (6 risers, 2.8 x 4.0 m) in the back corner opposite the exit, straight concrete stair down the side wall, 42 mm tube rails and kick plates; walkable (facFloor ramps along the nosings, holds while you lean out; no vertical re-base on it), the block is a walk box only below its top (lvTop), the rails walk boxes only (rounds pass), the stair is kept off the men's nav (room.facNav). Four speed-gate pedestals (three 1 m lanes, green LED) and low steel barrier panels across the room; observation window (3.4 x 1.05 m, sill at the dado) into a dark control room (console, blue wall screens), steel frame, glass collider 9 cm 'metal' (stops rounds). Ceiling islands (4 secure + 1 public) at h - 0.45 with troffers, duct, pipe rack on the landing wall, cable tray in the exposed ceiling.
- Ops: four 0.8 m pillars, alcoves between 1.3 m concrete fins along both side walls (bulkheads, low grilles), 3 x 3 ceiling islands with a troffer each (centre and exit-side first), duct, pipe rack on trapeze hangers, cable tray, double doors at both ends, rubber walkway cross.
- Medical: two curtain bays (ceiling tracks on blocks, bedhead trunking with outlets and labels), a 1.15 m half wall between them, a fixed stainless counter with basin and tap, drop ceiling, single door.
- Cover: piers (hard, faces up / down the room, peek toward the middle), fins (at the free end), landing front and inner faces, pedestals, barrier panels, half wall, counter (low).
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef; Node run of the section with the page's own mulberry32 / wallU / fixtureAt / boxDist / ceilingPlan (scratchpad t/test.mjs): planner over 200 slots (runs 1-3, gaps 4-6, no repeats), 300 layouts per kind (no solid outside the room, doors clear, every free nav cell reachable from the entry, exit reachable, >= 2 troffers, islands clear of piers), landing walk up / down in 1 cm steps (max floor change 7 mm), leaning cases; three r170 rotation checks (rails, roses, islands, wall-local x). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, light levels in the islands, the stair in VR, frame time of the ops room (9 diffusers, many batched parts).
## CQC: outdoor start "Outpost 17" + stealth (?start=cqc|outdoor|outonly, ?stealth=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Start choice (cqc.start.v1, default CQC ONLY): title screen "Start" row, terminal RUN page (bottom left), watch / M panel "Start:" cell. OUTDOOR + CQC: slot 1 is the yard (plan.type 'yard', a normal combat room), slot 2 a stairwell down (the 17 block's entry hall), then the rooms. OUTDOOR ONLY: updateStreaming never wants slot 2 (yardStop), so the block's door stays locked. A change re-plans slot 1+ only while you are in the first safe room and nothing ahead was fought (yardReplan, like rangeReplan); otherwise it applies on reload.
- Code: the OUTDOOR START section at the end of the module. One-line hooks: roomPlan (yardPlanAt), normalParams (yardParams), roomBuilder (yardBuilder), activateRoom (yardActivate after room.cover), roomFloorAt / updateFloor (yardFloor / yardFlat: stair ramps, landing, catwalk 4.5 m, tower cab 3.6 m; no vertical re-base off the ground), assignLights end (yardLights), updateStreaming (yardStop), coverPoints (yardCover), spawnEnemy (yardSpawnSpot, yardManPlaced), enemyFloorY, loopPlan (men 8 / 6 headset, rifles, list[0] = tower sniper), wearWetAt (yardWetAt), roomAcoustic ('range'), tick after nightUpdate (yardTick), watch / M panel / terminal rows.
- Yard 40 x 56 m (YC, yardLayout: one table for visuals, colliders, walk boxes, floor, nav, cover, spawns). Back wall = the 12 m "17" block facade (exit door + fixed second leaf, canopy, lamp, B2 stencil, slot windows); front = the 7.2 m start building (entry door). Side perimeter 3 m concrete + 2.2 m chain-link, outriggers, barbed strands, concertina. Building A (west, loading bay with roll-up shutter, canopy, bay lines 1-3, drain, pipes, door + DANGER plate) and B (east, ground and catwalk doors, conduits); steel stair up A to the catwalk bridge A -> B; guard tower (concrete base, steel cab, stair, flood on the roof); 9 jersey barriers (F-profile, 1.07 m), bollards, helipad (ring, H), drive lane, hatch, two flood poles. Facades stay inside the 0.2 m wall gap (never into the next room). The exit and entry are the chain's doors; the five side doors are real leaves too (below). The double doors' second leaves on the two facades are fixed (inactive leaves).
- Look: generated PBR sets (yardTextures, time-sliced): board-formed concrete 2.4 m tile (boards, fins, panel seams, tie holes, bug holes), broom-finished slab 6 m tile (sawcut joints, aggregate, cracks), painted steel 1 m (orange peel, scratches, rust chips); 1024 / 1024 / 512 px (headset 512 / 512 / 256), albedo + normal + ORM. One surface program (yardInject, key yard17; kind per material): macro noise, rain streaks, rust runs on steel, moss at the foot of walls and on ledges, curvature edge wear, wet model (darker porous albedo, lower roughness), puddles (yPuddleF, ~13 % of the ground; the CPU yardWetAt is the same noise, so splash steps match) with animated rain ripples, 10 analytic lamps (uYLampP / uYLampD through RE_Direct, no shadow), height fog with lamp in-scatter. Decal atlas (17, B1 / B2 arrows, DANGER KEEP OUT, bay numbers, NO ENTRY, KEEP CLEAR, H, rust and water runs).
- Sky: cqc/sky/sky.webp (4k) / sky2k.webp (headset) / sky.json from the user's Forest Clearing 1 Ground Skybox (Sketchfab, Luis Vidal, CC-BY-4.0) by tools/pack_sky.py (the GLB stored the equirect upside down; flipped, row 0 = zenith). Graded in the shader to a rainy night (desaturate, cool tint, expo 0.055, horizon into the fog colour), turned YC.moon.rot 75 deg so the bright cloud sits over the 17 block; the moon direction = sky.json's sun. The panorama's ground is projected onto an outer plane beyond the walls. IBL: the graded sky into a 256 px cube -> PMREM (the probes' size: no recompile), on the yard materials and as scene.environment while you are in the yard. Loaded only when a yard is built. Replace: drop another equirect at sky/sky.webp + sky2k.webp and edit sky.json.
- Lights (light count fixed): pool.main[0] = moon (spot 140 m out, shadowed, ~0.22 at the ground), pool.main[1] = the tower flood (shadowed, far 70), pool.rects over the two working doors (warm); restored on leaving. Other lamps analytic + emissive lenses (bloom on a computer) + halo quads (headset) + additive light-shaft cones. Rain: 7000 / 2600 streaks in a camera box (vertex-shader wrap, clipped to the yard, lit by the lamps with forward scatter), 600 / 220 ground splashes, rain audio bed. camera.far 220 in the yard (60 elsewhere). Night mode (power out) turns the lamps off; the moon stays.
- Side doors (yardDoorsActivate / yardDoorsUnload / yardDoorsTick): the door system now takes secondary leaves (door.sec { i, id, locked, hinge }): own dynamic body, collider (doorByCollider), revolute joint on the yard's body, walk box, in doors[] like room.door, so lever / push / pull, closer, kick, breach, holes (session only: door.sec holes are not saved), creaks, shut / stop sounds and the VR hand all work. Core hooks (one line each): doorHinge (door.sec hinge), updateDoorsPre (locked = door.sec.locked instead of "next room not built"), doorPace / updateDoorsPost (no reveal), addDoorHole, unloadRoom, shiftWorld. The men open unlocked ones they walk into (yardDoorsTick). Doors: A south -> guard room, A east -> bay office, B west -> stair core (a flight up to a 3.0 m landing), all three working; the core's top door and B's catwalk door lead nowhere: locked (rattle, a kick only thuds).
- Interiors (L.rooms, architecture only): the buildings with interiors are solid cells (yardCells: footprint minus interiors and the 0.24 m door openings), ceiling slab and solid above, lintels over the openings; counters (cover points), skirting, ceiling lights (analytic lamps with a zone: outdoor lamps never light indoors, interior lamps only their room; uYLampZ / uYRoomR in the shader); dry inside (no puddles, streaks or ripples); rain and splashes stop under every roof, canopy, the catwalk and the cab (uYRoof). Nav runs into them through the openings.
- Enemies: 5 + 2 x threat (cap 8, headset 6): a squad of three on the block's door, the rest across the yard at barriers / corners / poles / counters, and a sniper in the tower cab with an AWM (ENEMY_LOADOUTS.awp: 5 rounds, dmgK 2.6 on a hit, gap 2.4-3.2 s, bolt worked after each shot: case out and lift + back at 0.45 s, forward + close at 0.85 s with the recorded bolt family (yardBoltTick), sound 'awp', the .338 for penetration and tracers; drops the AWM (enemyDropType); the rifle loadout only until the AWM model has loaded). Stair hold, sight 75 m, half the aim error, a glint sprite + laser line for 0.9 s before each shot (yardSniperGate in enemyShoot). Nav grid rasterised per box (yardNav, 12 ms), rails up on the catwalk / tower left out.
- Stealth (every room; cqc.stealth.v1 { on, diff, meter }; terminal ENEMIES, watch, M panel): stealthLight 4 Hz at your chest (pool spots with one shadow ray each, rects, yard lamps with a ray for the strongest, your flashlight, muzzle flash) -> L = E / (E + 0.6 x diff.light). enemyThink: an unengaged man's e.seen build x stealthK (light, crouch / speed, off-axis, distance, difficulty); he only sees you within sight x range x (0.22 + 0.78 sqrt L) (stealthSees); 0.12..0.45 = searching (aiNoise toward where he glimpsed you); 0.45 = engaged as before. Hearing ranges x difficulty (aiListen). Meter: a chevron on a ring toward whoever notices you most, filling to 0.45 (DOM on a computer, a head-locked mesh 25 deg off axis in the headset); minimal = smaller, further out. Off = the old maths exactly.
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef; every yard shader through glslang (Vulkan GLSL after a mechanical conversion, scratchpad gl/test.mjs) and the Node run of the builder / collider pass / lights / tick against stubs with three r170 (gl/build.mjs: 56 draws, 150k vertices, 156 colliders, 77 walk boxes, both chain doorways and the three side openings clear, interiors clear, cells + carved = footprints, every side leaf swings away from you about its hinge, the two locked, nav into the interiors, the AWM plan / loadout / bolt sequence, lights restored on leaving, builder slices <= ~80 ms). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look, light levels, rain, the stealth feel, the sniper, frame time.
## CQC outdoor: nature and the wet night (?nature=off, ?wetx=off; headset check needed)
- cqc/models/nature.glb (3.7 MB) by tools/pack_nature.py from the user's "Free Low-Poly Nature Assets Pack" (Sketchfab, BozhidarKN, CC-BY-4.0, credited in docs/RELEASE_NOTES.md; source GLB not committed, pass its path). The pack has no conifers: pines (3), dead pines (2), dwarf-pine bushes (2), pine bark, needle-spray atlas (alpha + normal), forest floor and the LOD2 impostor atlas (a numpy rasteriser of each tree's LOD0, two sides) are generated there. From the pack: one broadleaf tree, two of its trees bare (dead), two rocks (greyed to wet granite), three stumps, three ferns / weeds; bark normal + ORM derived from its photo atlas, leaf colour bled under the alpha. Real scale, origin at the base; extras h / r (trunk at 1.3 m) / top / crown / cb / hull (rock and stump convex points).
- Code: the NATURE section before STEALTH (NC, NAT_INSIDE / NAT_BEDS, natLoad, natPlan, natTerrain, natMeshes, natTick, natBuild, natActivate, natCover, natConceal, natDrip*). Hooks (one line each): boot preload (the first rooms are built without returning to the event loop), yardRegister (prefetch), yardBuilder (natBuild before the merge; KIT door / steel swapped for yard wet materials before the freeze), yardActivate (natActivate before the nav grid), yardCover, yardLights (moon uniforms), yardTick (natTick, uYQ, drip px), penRoomLayers (info.solid: a trunk is solid wood), stealthK (natConceal), yardRainMaterial (uHalfR: rain over the forest edge), the outer panorama plane lowered to -0.6.
- Placement (seeded, room-local): six soil beds with flush kerbs in the margins (NE approach bed, NW, east margin, SW, SE corner) with pines (8-11 m), a dead pine, boulders, stumps, bushes, ferns; two boulders on the approach past the helipad; weeds along the wall feet. Node-checked: crowns clear walls, pilasters, buildings and the tower; nothing on doors, lane, helipad, stair feet; the tower sniper's line to the entry door open; no nav cell cut off. Forest: jittered grid (3.6 m at the edge, 5.8 m far) on |x| >= 20.3 + crown beside the yard and |x| >= 26 beyond its ends (the corridor of rooms stays empty), to 122 x 100 m (Quest 88 x 72, 1.3x sparser, fewer kinds); undergrowth (boulders, stumps, bushes, ferns) within ~30 m of the wall. Ground: natTerrain grid (2.5 / 5 m), 0 at the wall foot, hummocks, rising ~15 m into the mountainside.
- Draw: InstancedMesh per kind / LOD / primitive (shared instance buffers); forest LOD0 < 30 m, LOD1 < 64, impostor < 140 (Quest 16 / 42 / 92), ground cover culled 34-95 m; rebuilt at most 4 Hz after 1.5 m of movement (~1.3 ms). Compound plantings static, LOD0, cast shadows; the forest casts none. Desktop ~1.4k instances, ~360k forest triangles near the wall; Quest ~400, ~90k; 63 / 46 draws.
- Shader (yardInject, every yard material): instancing-aware world position / normal, wind sway (height^2, gusts) and card flutter (uYWindA / uYFlut per material); kinds 10 bark (moss up the foot, lichen, triplanar grain bump), 11 rock (triplanar grain, lichen crust, moss on top and at the foot, rain streaks), 12 foliage (both faces light as the crown, wet sheen), 13 forest floor (moss, puddles on the flat, CPU yardWetAt matches); rivulets running down every outdoor vertical face, beads swelling under every outdoor overhang (uYWetX); end-wall door faces toward the next room stay dry; the moon as an unshadowed fill beyond the shadowed moon spot's cone, dimmed under the canopy. Drips (one draw, 900 / 360): drops hang, fall and splash from every free overhang edge (canopies, catwalk, decks, cab roof, parapets, flood heads) and the compound's tree crowns.
- Physics / AI: trunk cylinders 'wood' (solid) for the compound and the forest within 60 x 70 m, rock and stump hulls 'concrete' / 'wood'; walk boxes and nav for the compound's trees and rocks (low stumps stepped over); cover points (trunks high with a side to step out, boulders over 0.85 m); bushes, ferns and low pine branches cut a man's detection build-up (natConceal, to 0.22).
- Checked: syntax, eslint no-undef, Node run (scratchpad nat/t/test.mjs, desktop and xr: placement, clearances, colliders, nav, cover, spawns, concealment, LOD counts, terrain, drips) and glslang on every changed shader (surface, instanced alpha cards, tangent rocks, drips, rain). One headless Chromium run: boots with the yard and nature loaded (no page errors, 264 programs, 1649 instances planned); SwiftShader drew the yard with the forest at well under 1 fps, so no screenshot was taken. Not verified on Quest: frame time, wind feel, the look in the headset.
## QA pass (headless Chromium; CQC, Interstate, Studio)
- Fixed: added parts' inertia factors compounded per device with no limit (muzFx per chain device, foreignFx per snapped part): a Glock with every bench part took 650x longer to aim, swayed 2000x and was drawn metres out of the hand. muzFx now sums the chain's ratio and applies it once; both use FX_CAP (ADS 2.5, sway 3, aim 2.5, climb >= 0.3).
- Ran with no page errors, frame errors or unlinked programs: CQC ?dev and plain loads, PLAY, all five locker guns (grab, rack, fire, aim, reload, light / laser), every store row bought and fitted, every gun's BUILD / PARTS / FINISH pages (solid + camo per group, 247 programs linked), vise cross-gun fits on all guns, AWM 3-25x (+ shade, killflash) and 10x scopes on the AWM and the 3-25x on the M4 (desktop scope 6x / 10x), trays (42), bench lamp switch / carry / put down, workshop zoom + press, SUPPLIES (NVG buy, tracer mix, brass sell), NVG worn / flipped / white / IR, frag + flashbang, room 1 (range), downed man, surrender + detain, walk through 7 rooms incl. a U-turn stairwell. Interstate: menu, Lexus and patrol car driven, 270 traffic agents, 116 programs linked. Studio: range ready, five guns spawned on the desk (two on the pile when full), 8 targets.
- Found, not fixed: Rally map entry in Studio logs 256 WebGL "Mismatch between texture format and sampler type" (pre-existing: same with rally.js before the forest commit; not reproduced with a getError hook on the draws, so the draw is not identified). Lamp can be put down through a wall when carried facing it. Not testable here: VR, sounds by ear, Quest frame time.

## CQC: outdoor start from any safe room
- The watch / M panel / terminal Start choice only worked in the very first safe room (slot 0, before any peek through the door), so after a restart or later in a run it always refused. yardReplan now works in any safe room you stand in while no fight ahead has started: the room past its door becomes the yard (YARD.base = that slot; yardPlanAt / yardStop use it), the stairwell down after it. A respawn (death or restart) with an outdoor start chosen does the same for the new run (yardRespawned / yardSafeEnter from updateStreaming). Refusal toast: "Choose it in a safe room before the next fight". Syntax parse only; not run in a browser.
## CQC outdoor: fog, shorter draw distance, baked shadows (performance)
- Fog thick: YC.fog dens 0.034 (headset densXR 0.05), max 0.97: nearly opaque past ~70 m (~50 m headset). Camera far in the yard 220 -> 125 m (headset farXR 85); the sky dome (r 25, no depth test) still draws the panorama's mountains. Forest LOD [22, 45, 115] / XR [12, 30, 75], forest extent 95 x 85 (XR 65 x 58), rain 4500 / 1500 streaks, splashes 400 / 140, drips 600 / 240.
- Baked shadows: the moon and tower flood spots (pool.main[0/1] in the yard, userData.yardBake) redraw their maps only on sch.room (entry / light change), never for motion; yard men cast no shadows (yardManPlaced), so no ghost shadows. Flashlight and his torches update as before. Syntax parse only; not run in a browser.
## CQC outdoor: no instant detection, pathfinding without stalls (headset check needed)
- Detection: the yard moon counts x0.25 in stealthLight (rainy overcast: it shows the ground, does not light you up), so in the dark L ~0.1 and a man sees you only within ~13 m; the tower sniper's unengaged sight is enemy.sight x1.4 (his 75 m only once engaged); a contact shout outdoors reaches mates within 18 m (others alert 0.4 and get your last spot, not engaged); aiDoors skips the yard (no turning to the entry door across 56 m).
- findPath: buffers kept per nav grid (generation stamps, typed binary heap) instead of fresh 56k-cell arrays + array-of-arrays heap per search (GC stalls in the yard once men moved); expansion cap max(20000, cells) (long yard paths failed at 20000 and were retried); weighted A* x1.6 on grids over 30k cells. Node: open-field yard path 0.56 ms, round a 36 m wall 22 ms.
- Headset: light-shaft cones hidden, rain 900 streaks / 80 splashes, drips 120.
- Syntax parse + Node test of findPath only; not run in a browser.
## CQC: enemy LODs, yard stealth light calibration (tested headless in the yard)
- Measured in the yard (headless Chromium, ?start=outdoor&dev): 2.2M triangles drawn, ~650k of them the men (each ~130k: body ~82k merged parts + the AK ~66k). JS per frame is small (profile: main thread mostly idle), so the cost is the GPU.
- Enemy LODs (ENEMY_LOD, ?elod=off): cqc/vendor/meshopt/meshopt_simplifier.js (meshoptimizer 1.3, MIT) loaded after the enemy models; each merged skinned part gets 30 % / 10 % index buffers over the same vertex attributes (LockBorder, so material seams stay shut), his gun's parts get theirs the first time they are seen (enemyLodGeo; non-indexed parts welded with mergeVertices for the LOD buffers only, LOD 0 is the original). enemyLodTick (from enemySkinSync): < 7 m full, < 16 m 30 %, beyond 10 % (headset 5 / 11 m), 12 % hysteresis; a new gun in his hands is re-listed, the old one gets LOD 0 back. Measured: body 82k -> 25k -> 8.6k, AK 44.5k part -> 13.4k -> 4.5k; the yard drew 1.04M -> 0.46M.
- Yard stealth: E0 x4 outdoors (night-adapted eye): under the entry lamp L 0.88 -> 0.45; the B1 wall lamp beside the entry at half; the tower sniper builds sight x0.5 while unengaged. Measured standing still at the spawn under the lamp: of 5 men only the sniper (12 m) and one at 23 m engaged within 8 s, the three at ~48 m stayed calm.
- Yard surface shader: lamps over 40 m away skipped per pixel.
- Yard lamps sorted nearest-first every frame (yardTick); the surface, fog in-scatter and rain shaders loop over the first uYLampN (10 computer, 5 headset) instead of all 14 for every pixel.
- Picking the outdoor start mid-run (watch Start in a safe room) builds the yard in the background at 2.5x the usual build budget while you stay in the safe room; headless it built and activated, no errors.
- Tested headless (?start=outdoor&dev, 480x270, SwiftShader): yard builds, men calm in the dark, the block door's stairwell and the room after it stream in (the yard becomes the safe room behind you, as every room does), sniper + rifleman deaths and drops, no page / frame errors. Yard view 174 draws / 472k triangles (safe room 102 / 297k) at a frame rate equal to or better than the safe room's here. Not checked on Quest.
## CQC outdoor: guards patrol and look round, bodies are found, lightning (tested headless by stepping the AI)
- Cause of "seen instantly" (part 2): every yard man stood at a cover point turned to the safe-room door, looking within ±0.7 rad of it.
- Roles (yardManPlaced -> e.yardRole, yardIdle from the 'idle' case): plan index 0 sniper = slow sweep across the yard (±1.3 rad); 1 and 3 sentries at the block door = turn up to ±1.6 rad every 3-7 s; the rest patrol = slow walk (0.55 x walk, gun low, facing the way he walks) to a ground point 6-22 m off within 16 m of his post (yardPatrolPoint: nav-free, ground only, never the safe-room doorway), stop 3-8 s and look round. enemyMove follows an idle man's path; enemyFace split out of enemyMove so stairHold men (the tower sniper, stairwell men) turn too (they never turned before).
- Bodies (yardBodyCheck, 2 Hz): an unengaged man who sees a dead mate in front of him (14 m, sniper 30 m, clear line) raises the alarm there (aiNoise 1: engaged, hunts to the body; shout reaches mates within 18 m). loopEnemyDied in the yard: only mates within 10 m hear a man drop, no "man down" radio from a quiet kill.
- Lightning (yardLightning, ?lightning=off): every 30-80 s, 2-3 flickers (sky exposure x up to 10, environment x up to 5, half in the headset), thunder 0.6-7 s later (crack when close), the flash counts in stealthLight.
- Stepped test (enemiesUpdate at 30 Hz, no render): patrols moved 5-10 m legs and paused, sentries turned, the sniper swept; a quiet kill 14.6 m from the nearest mate: nobody reacted for 10 s until the other patrol walked up, found the body and searched it with the two sentries, the sniper stayed calm. No page / frame errors.
## CQC outdoor: room access check, no mid-fight shader compiles, failing lamp, drain steam (tested headless)
- Access, walked with the game's own locomote + updateFloor + physics (headless, scripted routes): guard room (round the counter), bay office, stair core up the flight to the 3.0 m landing, A's stair to the catwalk and across to B, tower stair to the cab, the block door. All reached, right floor heights, no stuck spots; ~0.5 ms per move step.
- Hitch found: the men's materials (Mark_*, arms) and the sniper's AWM (sniper, ScopeGlass, Plexiglass) compiled the first time they came into view in the yard (the yard's environment differs from the load-time warm-up): a stall mid-fight. yardTick now runs renderer.compileAsync(scene, camera) once on entering the yard (Y.warmed); after it no program compiled at any spot.
- Measured per spot (desktop path): 87-175 draws, 0.43-0.60 M triangles (yard middle, guard room, office, core landing, catwalk, cab, entry).
- Graphics: lamp 8 (B's catwalk door) is a failing lamp (stutters 0.15-0.75 s every 4-13 s, Y.flk); the lane's three drain grates steam (YC.steamAt, haze pool, 1.5 puffs/s each within 25 m, headset 0.5/s forced past its haze-off).
## CQC: men killed on stairs die and lie on the treads
- Cause: a lethal body hit on a stairwell man (or the tower sniper) took the step 91 downed path: the floor loop poses him at one fixed height (e.stairY) on a flat nav floor, so on a flight he sank into the treads and lay there alive for 60-120 s (the downed life). downedFor(e) = downedOn() and not in a stair room and not stairHold; enemyShot and the frag overpressure (nadeDownHit) use it, so there he dies into the ragdoll, which lands on the real tread / landing colliders (head shots already did).
- Checked headless: the tower sniper shot in the torso is dead (not downed), his ragdoll parts rest at 3.64-3.81 m on the 3.6 m cab deck after 400 physics steps, no errors. A stairwell man was not reached headless (the stairwell streams in only once the yard is cleared).
## CQC: lighting in large rooms, more walls, shaped drywall damage (tested headless)
- Room fill (TFILL, ?tfill=off): only a room's first two ceiling fittings had real lights (the pool's shadowed spots + area lights); every other fitting was emissive only, so long / large rooms were dark past them. tfillChunk (top level, before anything compiles) patches three's shared light chunks: lights_pars_begin declares uTfP[11], lights_fragment_maps adds up to 8 unshadowed cosine downlights (headset 3, diffuse only) from uTfP (world positions; ShaderLib standard / physical / lambert / phong / toon get the uniform, and cloneUniforms copies the array by reference, so every material shares it); [8] colour + full / diffuse flag, [9] / [10] the current room's box (no light leaks through a doorway). tfillTick (after updateFlicker): the current room's troffers past [1], not shot out, not in the dark, nearest first, the last one fading out before the cut-off; outdoors none. I 14, colour the area light's halfway to white. No light objects (the light count never changes), no new programs.
- More fittings: ceilingPlan gives rooms of 40 m2 and up a grid of troffers ~3.4 m apart (cell centres; the two nearest the middle first, so they keep the pool lights). The mechanical room in the test (8.6 x 7 m): 6 fittings, 4 lit by the fill.
- More walls: rooms of 55 m2 and up get 1-3 more drywall partitions (95 m2: 2 + a 35 % chance of a third), up to 3.4 m long, at least 45 % half walls, through the same placement rules (door-to-door walk kept clear, nothing through a full-height wall, half wall under a fitting).
- Shaped drywall damage (PDMG, ?pdmg=off = the old 20 cm cells): each face is one sheet; damage is a per-partition R8 mask (2 cm texels, both faces; pdMake / pdStamp) the sheet's own material copy reads (pdMaterial: chained onto the wing paint's macro hook, key +|pd; open = discard, a crushed cracked rim, the gypsum core at the torn edge, the sheet's inside face brown paper; ragged edge from noise). Every round punches its own ragged hole (entry ~1 cm, torn exit 2-3 cm); a cell's damage breaking out (CONFIG.cover.cellHp / splash unchanged) stamps a ~17 cm blob round the hits (between the last hit and the cell's middle), so bursts chew organic, merging holes. A round passes where the mask is open at its own point (pdOpen). Holes kept in room memory (pholes, cap 400 a partition) and re-stamped on rebuild; decals the blob swallows are purged. Shadow pass reads the mask (pdDepthMaterial).
- Checked headless: both pd programs link; 15 rounds into a test sheet: 708 cm2 open, irregular, rounds pass through it; 10 traceBullet 9 mm rounds through the safe room's half wall: front 708 / back 504 cm2 open, 21 holes stored; fill on vs off seen in screenshots (light pools on the floor, the far side lit); 327 programs linked in the outdoor map with the patched chunks; no page or frame errors. Not checked on Quest: the fill's cost there (3 diffuse lights per pixel), the holes by eye in the headset.
## CQC: the tower sniper can be shot, outdoor loading panel, full-size yard textures in the headset
- Sniper would not die: enemyRayTest's quick reject measured the ray against his middle at world y 1.0 (and ignored the room's floor height), so a man standing higher (the tower cab at 3.6 m, a stair landing, a wing above) was skipped by every round. enemyBaseY(e) = room floor + his stand height (enemyFloorY) now feeds that reject, the off-screen test, rounds passing near him (aiRoundPassed), his contact shadow and his body foley. Checked headless: AKM from the entry door at the cab (12 m): a chest round lands (plate, 9 hp), a head shot kills him.
- Loading panel (YLOAD): while the outdoor map builds after choosing it in a safe room (stream.building plan 'yard'), a panel 0.9 m in front of your eyes (camera child, on top; x1.7 on a computer) says BUILDING OUTPOST 17 with a bar from the builder's steps (the last build's count in cqc.yardSteps.v1, first time an estimate of 400), then OUTPOST 17 READY for 1.5 s. Redrawn only when the percent changes. Seen headless.
- Yard textures in the headset at the computer's size (concrete and ground 1024, steel 512; were 512 / 512 / 256): the ground tile is 6 m, so 512 px was 1.2 cm a texel underfoot. ~36 MB more texture memory, the yard build takes longer (the panel covers it).
## CQC: surfaces (SURF, ?surf=off; tested headless)
- The room shells (every wing's floor, wall paint and ceiling: applyMacro's programs, key +|sf1) and the yard's concrete walls and ground (yard17|sf0 / |sf1) get surfInject: anti-tiling (stochastic sampling after Inigo Quilez's texture repetition #3: low-frequency noise picks one of 8 offsets per patch and blends two neighbours, textureGrad with shared derivatives; map, normal map and roughness in register), the procedural albedo pits flattened toward the map mean (textureLod 12; rooms keep 55 / 30 / 50 % of its contrast, yard 70 / 75 %), real photo-scanned layers from cqc/textures (copies of the Studio's concrete diff / nor / orm, plaster nor / orm, grit nor): floors take the concrete scan's albedo variation (relative to its measured mean, 0.75), relief (whiteout blend, 0.55), roughness and AO; walls and ceilings the plaster's relief and roughness under the paint plus a faint concrete albedo variation; a grit detail normal (11-16 cm tiles) fading in within ~3 m. Scan tiles 2.4 / 1.2 / 1.6 m. Headset: roughness / AO scans sampled once. Off (uSfK.x 0) until all six scans have loaded; means measured from a 64 px copy (surfMean).
- Checked headless: 201 programs linked in the CQC rooms, 338 in the outdoor map (yard variants included), no page or frame errors; before / after screenshots of room 1: the floor reads as concrete (no repeated blotches), the walls as painted plaster (the cork-like pits gone). Not checked on Quest: the cost (two samples per map on floors / walls), the look in the headset.
## CQC: lasers that went dark (IR mode by accident)
- Cause: in the headset a fingertip left on a laser's pad after the press (not lifted past re-arm) for 0.6 s switched every laser to IR (step 118 nvgSwitchHold), which draws nothing unless night vision is down. It worked without owning night vision, was saved (cqc.nvg.v1), and the "Laser: IR" toast is a page element the headset never shows: the laser looked broken for good. Same for the weapon light (IR illuminator = dark flashlight).
- Fix: IR modes need night vision you own (nvgModeHold returns without it; nvgIr, nvgLaserHide, nvgBeam, the lens and laser look all check NVG.owned), so a saved IR mode without night vision draws the visible laser / white light again; the headset hold is 1.5 s; the mode change shows in the headset too (vrNote: a head-locked message panel, camera child, made on first use; vrNoteTick from tfillTick).
- Checked headless: store-fitted Glock laser and bench-fitted AKM / M4 lasers switch on and draw beam + dot; IR saved without night vision = drawn red; a hold without night vision changes nothing; with night vision owned, IR + tubes up = hidden (as intended).

## CQC: perf / robustness pass 1
- Surfaces (SURF): sfTexS fetches once inside an anti-tiling patch and twice only in its blend band (explicit gradients, so branching is safe); headset band narrowed (smoothstep 0.43-0.57). About 60 % fewer map / normal fetches on room and yard surfaces.
- Physics NaN guard (physNanGuard, right after the world is made): every RigidBody / Collider / desc setter that takes a vector drops a non-finite (or > 1e6) value and warns once with the caller, instead of passing it on. One NaN made Rapier's next step panic ('unreachable') and the physics world never recovered (game frozen). Found by a test that gave enemyDie a 2D point (NaN torque into the ragdoll); no game path producing NaN was seen, the guard is hardening.
- Bench lamp carried on the computer: its head is pulled back along the view to 12 cm short of any static collider (no more putting it down through a wall).
- Measured headless: game JS ~2.9 ms a frame in a combat room (render stubbed); no new shader programs while walking rooms 1-2 after load.
- Painted steel texture (shadePaintedMetal): chips were 6 % of the tile in big 1/8-tile patches (bare metal reads black in dim rooms: cabinets looked blotched); now ~0.9 % small chips (fbm 24 + 6). The texture cache key hashes the recipe, so it regenerates on the next load.
- Walls (shadeWall): pinholes much sparser (they read as cork at 2-3 m), hairline cracks fainter. Tan wing paint is now putty / taupe #a39a86 / #6e675a (the ochre read as cardboard under the warm tubes). Brushed steel prop wear (kind 1): grime and tea-stain streaks weaker (big ducts looked smeared).

Kit protos cloned in yardBuilder onto yardLit copies of their materials (yardLitMat: the kit material + yardInject as kind 7: zone lamps, fog, no rain); they join room.layout.furniture, so activateRoom gives colliders, walk boxes, soft cover and breakable crates as in any room; yardNav takes their walk boxes (extra) since it runs before them.

The counter cap was painted steel (read as a pale sheet with rust blotches under the lamp).

Follow-up from them: interior paint uses the CQC rooms' painted drywall set (KIT.sets, CONFIG.tile.wall; the board-formed concrete read as wooden planks under paint), counter tops matte dark laminate (roughness 0.82: the glossier top blew out to white under the lamp).

## CQC: real boxes on the shelving (SHELF, ?shelfbox=off; in test)
- makeShelf no longer bakes its boxes (shelfA storage / shelfB records): their slots (pose, size, tone, tape) sit in proto.userData.slots. shelfBoxesBuild (roomBuilder after the furniture, yardBuilder, genPrecompile) draws a room's boxes as one InstancedMesh (M.cardboard, instance colour = tone) + one for the tape; shelfColliders (activateRoom) gives each a static cuboid ('wood', colliderInfo .sbox). The shelf's colliders are its five decks (FURN.decks, shelfDecks), so the gaps are open.
- shelfWake: a round into a box (bulletHit .sbox branch; penHit leaves no exit hole for it), a frag (shelfBlast from fragBlast, 4.5 m, J 9) or a woken box pushing into one (shelfTick, 3 s per box while it moves) hides its instance, drops its collider and puts a 'cardboard box' prop of its exact size there (mass 110 kg/m3, brk 2.4 hp -> 2-3 flaps) with the push. Rebuilt rooms have full shelves (not remembered). ?dev: window.__cqcShelf.
- Tested headless (storage room, 66 boxes on 6 shelves): 3 AKM rounds woke the 3 boxes hit and shelfTick knocked 9 more off (12 awake, some on the floor), a frag in front woke 44 / 66 (21 on the floor), no NaN, 208 programs linked, no errors; before / after screenshots match (full shelves, then knocked bare). Static and prop colliders 3 mm inside the drawn box (touching neighbours popped each other up 3-6 cm).

## CQC: physics to real-world numbers (in test)
- Audit: gravity 9.81, door 38 kg, prop masses (box, block, two-by-four, crate, chair, cans, boards, drywall), player 1.4 / 2.6 m/s, men 1.2 / 2.7 m/s, frag fuse 4.2 s / flashbang 1.6 s, fragment ranges, casing ejection 1.5-3 m/s: already realistic, unchanged. Cardboard flap 0.25 -> 0.06 kg (0.3 x 0.26 m of corrugated board).
- Blast push (fragBlast, shelfBlast): was mass x 6 m/s for every prop (a 12 kg block flew as fast as a flap) and a flat 2.2 N s per body part. Now blastI(d) (positive-phase impulse of ~0.2 kg TNT, reflected: 160 / max(d, 0.5) x (1 - (d/5)^2) Pa s) x the thing's mean projected area (colArea: a quarter of the collider's surface), capped at 18 m/s (props) / 12 m/s (body parts); the door: its 1.8 m2 face x |cos| to the blast, max 160 N s.
- Bullet push (bulletHit props / doors, tableHit, ragdollHit, the kill shove): the round's real momentum bulletP = sqrt(2 m E) of the energy it still has (9 mm 2.9, 5.56 3.7, 7.62x39 5.6, .338 14.6 kg m/s fresh) instead of d.impulse x energy (rifles 2-3x, an AWM 4x into ragdolls, and the kill shove 6x on top). enemyDie keeps its x6, fed bulletP / 4 (1.5x the round's momentum; his collapse does the rest). ?pen=off = the old pushes.
- Kick (kickJ): a ~10 kg leg's sole at 6 m/s, restitution 0.3: v = 7.8 x 10 / (10 + m) (a 0.4 kg can 7.5 m/s, a 2.4 kg box 6.3, a 26 kg table capped at 55 N s) instead of 2.6 m/s whatever it weighed; body parts the same.
- Ragdoll mass: RAG table = de Leva segment masses (80 kg man); gear where it is worn (RAG_GEAR: chest +11, hips +3, head +1.4), limbs x1.06: ~94 kg (was every part x1.3, 102 kg with heavy-swinging limbs).

## CQC: OPTIONS page (terminal; in test)
- Every player-facing ?flag switch is now also on the terminal's OPTIONS tab (between GRAPHICS and RECORDS), so the headset can reach them: groups PHYSICS (penetration + real bullet push, downed, surrender, stealth, shelf boxes, attachment clip, ragdoll self / limits / sleep, recentre guard, can heat, muzzle chains, rails, brass, facility rooms, outdoor nature), GORE (Extreme / Mild / Off + footprints, puddles, damp clothes), GRAPHICS, EFFECTS, AUDIO, CONTROLS (OPT_GROUPS: [flag, label, onValue]).
- Saved in localStorage cqc.opts.v1 ({ flag: 'off' }, or 'on' for kitcards); read once at start-up: const QS = location.search + the saved flags, and every module read of location.search is QS now (131 sites), so a saved switch acts exactly like the address flag. Address flags still win. RELOAD button on the page (confirm; in the headset you enter VR again after it loads). Debug / test-only flags (contact, palmslab, hitch, gltfblob, safe, light=, fill=...) are not listed.

## Interstate: warehouse garage fixes (tested headless; headset check needed)
- Lag: the key light's shadow map was redrawn every frame (each car ~230k triangles drawn again); now on demand (key.shadow.autoUpdate false, #shadowNow on place, display copy built, warehouse loaded, graphics change, and each frame only while a car moves). Headless: 715k -> 371k triangles a frame standing in the shed.
- Walking: WASD starts it; click locks the mouse for look (Esc frees it, Esc again back to the orbit / shop), Shift runs, pad L3 walks (left stick, right stick look, A take / drive, B stop). The shop panel and its dark backdrop fade while you walk or drive; a one-line hint shows the keys. Walls and car boxes also stop you in the headset (collide() in #garageWalk).
- Test drive: E / click on the other car takes it and puts you straight in (pendDrive waits for its body). Esc gets you out beside the driver's door, walking. Keys while walking / driving / at the door menu come from keydown (GarageWorld.keyDown), so a quick tap (Esc, 1-4) is never missed; the UI no longer turns them into Back / tab changes there.
- After a run: a run driven out of the roller door comes back to the garage (End run, summary Menu, quit), Back from there = the main menu. quitToMenu no longer throws without a drive world.
- Checked headless (SwiftShader): walk, wall clamp, Esc stays in the garage, test drive of the other car, out by Esc, door menu -> run -> End run -> garage, a second run -> garage, no page errors. Not checked in the headset.

## CQC: riser mount under cross-gun optics (?optmount=off; headset check needed)
- An optic seated on a gun that is not its own (seatPart, any-gun fit) now sits on a riser mount (optMount, child of the optic: comes off with it, removed in wbGrabbed): anodized base plate, Picatinny teeth with slots under the optic, jaws down both sides of the slide / receiver top (width measured by side rays, optGunHalf), two cross-bolts with a hex nut and a round head. Footprint under the optic's middle, 35-110 mm, kept over the gun. Lift 6.5 mm (dots, holo, ACOG), 11 mm (tube scopes), so the optic and its own rings stand clear of the body and sights; the sight line follows (optSight reads the raised axis).
- Checked headless (?dev): AWM 3-25x and 10x on a Glock and an M1911: mount 20 parts, base on the gun top (+0.6 mm), jaws 6 mm down its sides, no errors. Not checked in the headset.

- Reflection probe (envSurroundings, IBL only): the treeline is back over the ridge (dark conifers, sun-lit side), and straight down is dark wet asphalt instead of bright snow, so clear coats mirror a horizon with depth and the lower body / sills / wheels read dark and wet instead of milky.
- Saloon cabin: CEL_LOOK per material (the cabin atlas Material_68 was colour 0 = black with only reflections, the plastic look; roughness-0 parts got real values; walnut lacquered). Fine tiling leather / moulded-plastic grain normal maps (cabinGrain, generated once; cabinGrainOn), also on the patrol car's cabin materials. Material_38 / _39 keep roughness 0: they are the mirror glasses MIRROR_GLASS finds by roughness.

## Interstate: tail lamp lenses (headset check needed)
- Tail lenses were flat translucent red sheets over nothing (no housing behind them in the models): they looked broken. TAIL_LENS_VERT / TAIL_LENS_PARS (tlCells): opaque lens, a staggered reflector facet grid (11 x 8.5 mm) 16 mm behind the glass by parallax in the lens's own frame (metres, plane by the dominant object normal), 4 mm flutes on the outer lens, deep red between facets; lit, the facets glow more than the gaps. Used by tailGlassMaterial (GLB cars, key tailglass2, clear coat kept), the traffic patrol cars' tail lenses and the traffic lamp material for red / amber lamps (plus a lens colour by day and a sky glint at grazing angles). Head lamps unchanged. The saloon's own photo-textured lamps unchanged.
- Checked headless: garage patrol car close-up, night run behind traffic; no errors.

## Interstate: crash physics (?crash=off = old model; headset check needed)
- Crashed traffic used to slide straight through the traffic around it. TrafficSystem.#crashPairs (each step after #interact): every crashed car against up to 4 neighbours either side in its sorted carriageway list, oriented boxes in the road frame (crashBox: x along s, y = u, obbSAT), penetration split by mass, two-body impulse with rotation (restitution CRASH.e .15, Coulomb friction .4); a car struck hard enough (dv > 1.5 m/s or a spin kick > .2 rad/s) goes into a crash itself (chain reactions), a lighter knock slows it (crashApply). A hard hit makes the struck car sound its horn.
- #crashMove: tyre friction in the car's own frame (rolling with the brakes on .55 g along its heading, sliding .85 g across it, x (1 - .3 wet)), so a car sideways stops sooner; the spin is scrubbed off by the tyres (3.2 rad/s^2) instead of decaying exponentially, so a stopped car stops turning (it spun on like ice).
- Your car (VehicleDynamics.#jolt from contact() and the barriers): the impact impulse pitches and rolls the body on its springs (frontal hit dives the nose, rear squats, side rolls away from the blow), capped 1.4 rad/s.
- Checked headless: a crashed car shoved at 30 m/s into a 6 m/s car 32 m ahead: contact at 1.41 s, the struck car crashes, closest centres 4.9 m (no overlap), both come to rest, no NaN, no errors. The body jolt is not checked by eye.

## Interstate: rain, windscreen water in the headset, rain grip (headset check needed)
- Rain grip (assists.rainGrip, default on, Assists tab): the wet road costs a fifth of its grip loss and standing water a sixth of its aquaplaning (film x .16, wetPenalty x .2). Measured in a full downpour: surfGrip .94 on (.70 off).
- Rain streaks: per-drop size / speed / brightness, soft tapered streaks brighter at the drop, lit by your headlamps (the snow's cone maths) and glowing when backlit by the sun / moon (forward scatter), tone mapped; opacity .32 -> .42.
- Splashes (graphics.rainSplash): 900 crowns (45 % in the headset) that open and drop on the plane of the road under the car, within 15 m, none under the car; one draw, compiled at load.
- Windscreen water in the headset (graphics.windscreenVR, default on): the finish pass's drop / runner / film / wiper code (cut from finishShader's source at first use, WS_VR_GLSL) on a 12 x 12 layer 4 mm inside the fitted glass (wsVRBuild, child of the body), fed from the same ws state each frame (DriveWorld.#wsVR): beads catch the sky, dark rims, a hot glint, blades and arms drawn. #wsGlass key now follows the body (the patrol car used the saloon's eye). wsVRTest = true draws it on a computer for tests.
- Fix: renaming GarageWorld.key -> keyDown had also renamed CarMirrors.key, so every key press during a run threw (keyHook). Back to key().
- Checked headless: downpour on the open road (chase and cockpit), splashes drawn (5400 indices), VR layer visible with the wipers' state, no errors. Not checked on Quest.
## Interstate build 1.41: freeway route, tunnels, viaducts, more interchanges (§13.2c; ?route=ring = the old ring; headset check needed)
- Main road A (Open road only): no longer a wobbly circle. polySegs: a skeleton polygon (7-10 corners round the origin at random radii, every other one sometimes pulled in: bays and lobes), each corner rounded by clothoid + arc + clothoid (radii 650-2600 m, transitions 70-300 m, so curvature ramps and never jumps), long edges broken by a gentle S-bend; closeCourse walks it and closes it exactly by changing tangent lengths (weighted least squares, active set at 220 m). freewayRoute takes the first valid seed from ROUTE.pick (12): 30-50 km, within ROUTE.reach of the origin, no two parts of the road within ROUTE.clear unless 2.6 km apart along it. courseProfile: terrainBase along it, smoothed, held to 4.5 % and rounded (vertical curves). Track takes the dense control points (adaptive Catmull-Rom subdivision; the old ring keeps 400). Current A: 36.4 km, min radius 672 m, max grade 3.95 %.
- Tunnels / viaducts (routeFeatures, TERRAIN_FX added to terrainHeight): 3 ridges over tunnel sites (straight enough, not urban, the land already high) and 3 valleys across viaduct sites; feature height solved for the cover / drop wanted. Spans are then measured from the terrain: tunnel = hill over the whole bore (12 m at the centre, 9 m beside the walls), viaduct = ground 9 m under the deck. A.K1 (earth weight: 0 in both, faded over 20 m) drives Network.ground / vergeLimit / FarTerrain like a flyover's span; A.KD (deck) only for viaducts (road sound: Track.deckK, so a tunnel is no concrete deck). A.tunnelSpans feed realism 3's eye adaptation (it reads world.tunS now). Overpasses, gantries, signs, lamp columns and exit signs keep out of both.
- Streaming: the main streamer has portals (buildTunnelProfile for A): RoadChunk.#bores = the tunnel profile on both carriageways (twin bores, casting shadow) + lamp boxes, only in chunks meeting a span, rows outside the span collapsed onto the portal; verge and noise walls off inside. Two-way roads with spans (A's viaducts, a side road over A) get the concrete deck near them. RouteStatics: an extruded headwall (bores cut out, 1.6 m out of the hill, ~78 x 16 m) at every portal and a hemisphere fill light that is always in the scene. NetStatics piers now for every road's spans (two columns under a two-way deck). Distant hills ring moved out to 14 km.
- Light in a bore (routeTunnelLight, end of the open-road WorldEnvironment.update): k from the camera's place on A (0 outside, 1 from 60 m in to 60 m before the exit): scene.environmentIntensity, sun, fog colour and in-scatter scaled down, the street-light pool moved to the bore's fittings, fill on, no rain / snow, road dry. ROUTE_TUN holds the knobs.
- Network (buildFreewayNetwork): the side roads are courses of their own (turtleSegs: random bends incl. S-bends, a fixed 1.5 km tangent beside A where they meet, clockwise), placed on A's tangents (curvature < 1/1500 over 1.4 km) 3 km apart, clear of the start and the features, checked on 50 m distance fields (A 600 m except at the junction, each other 750 m, features). A side road may cross A only on a bridge (lifted 7.9 m over A's paving, 4 % approaches; Network.#bridges spans it) at >= 40 deg; none does on the current route. NET_FREEWAY: B (fly), D (fly), C (inside), E, G, F tried in turn; current route: B, D, C, E = 4 interchanges, 12 slip roads (4 flyovers over A). netJunction (shared with the old ring) = the old loop-ramp / flyover / sign code, plus rampCheck on the freeway (loop ramps R >= 80 m and <= 7 %, flyovers R >= 170 m and <= 6.5 %, else dropped) and a 2 km exit sign on A. Junction zones +-1.5 km (+-2.6 km with flyovers).
- Fix (both layouts): Network.#gaps no longer opens a road's guardrail where it is on a bridge over another road (flyover rails used to drop over the ring).
- Load: route ~0.15 s, network ~1.2 s (Node; the old ring ~0.6 s).
- Checked (Node, the page's own code, scratchpad hw/verify.mjs and t11.mjs; lanes 3/4/5): every road closed, tangent continuous, curvature change over 20 m <= 32 % of the curve's own (the old ring: up to 150 %), grades <= 4 %; no road crosses itself; the only crossings are B's flyovers over A at 7.9 m; every slip road starts and ends in a lane of its roads at the same height; tunnels 930-960 m with >= 4 m over the vault, viaducts 530-1270 m with >= 9 m under the deck; start clear. RoadChunk / RouteStatics with three r169 and stub materials: 80 chunks round every span, no NaN, bores only inside spans, portals on the span ends facing out. node --check, eslint no-undef (browser globals only). Not run in a browser or on Quest: the look of the bores, portals, hills, valleys and decks, tunnel light levels (computer and VR exposure), frame time, traffic / autopilot / chases on the new roads.
## Interstate: Yamaha YZF-R6 motorcycle (third garage slot; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- Model: the user's "Yamaha YZF-R6 RACE" (Sketchfab, dragos12_, CC-BY-4.0; credited in the garage Body description, the Credits page, docs/RELEASE_NOTES.md and the GLB's asset.extras). tools/pack_bike.py <source.glb> -> interstate/cars/r6.glb (2.97 MB from 29.5 MB) + r6.json: stand dropped, stood on both tyres (the source sat nose-down on its paddock stand: turned 0.050 rad), wheelbase 1.375 m (x1.0016), front -z, origin on the ground between the axles. Parts by connected component and position: chassis, forkU (outer tubes, clamps, bars, levers), forkL (inner tubes, axle clamps, calipers, fender), wheelF, swing (swingarm, hugger, chain, hoses), wheelR, lamps head / tail / indFL / indFR (mirror indicators) / indRL / indRR; each node's origin on its pivot. Steering axis fitted to the fork tubes: rake 23.6 deg, trail 97 mm (R6: 24 deg / 97 mm). Textures resized + WebP in Python (EXT_texture_webp), geometry compressed by npx gltfpack@1.3.0 (-cc, float UVs: the source tiles UVs up to 80x; the npm gltfpack has no WebP encoder). Runtime: GLB_CARS.r6.meshopt loads three's meshopt_decoder.module.js (added to interstate/vendor/three/examples/jsm/libs, r169, MIT).
- §14.14 (after buildGlbCar): BIKE_SPEC, BIKE_POWER, BIKE_SFX, BIKE_CAM, bikeConfigure / bikeTorque / bikeDrag / bikeLean (physics), bikeMaterial / bikePaintPatch / buildBike / bikeStance / bikeAnimate (look and pose tree: stance -> head (rake) -> steer (about the axis) -> offset -> forkU + slide (forkL, front hub); swing pivot -> swing + rear hub), bikeLights, bikeWet, bikeApplyModel, bikeCabTick, bikeVrBars, bikeGripWorld, bikeStats. Hooks are one line each, marked "§14.14".
- Physics (chosen, stated in the section): the car's VehicleDynamics with motorcycle numbers: 270 kg with rider, 51 % front, virtual track 1.2 m with roll centres at the CG (0.3 m: no roll, little load across), open rear differential, 599 cc torque curve (61.7 Nm / 87 kW), six speeds 2.583..1.150 x 2.073 x 45/16, idle 1350, limiter from the garage, light crank (Ie 0.026), automatic clutch that slips high in the revs (BIKE_SPEC.slip), slower steering rack (wn 9.5 x trail factor). Car literals turned into fields holding the car values (fin, revCut, revMax; idle, clutch, Ie, shift points x shiftK, reverse ratio, barrier box): the Lexus and the patrol car give identical numbers before and after (Node run of the page's own class). Lean = visual roll: lagged atan(v r / g), upright below ~3 km/h, max 54 deg; pitch clamped (no wheelies / stoppies); the bars show counter-steer from the roll rate. Node: 0-100 4.6 s (the automatic clutch's slip limits the launch; a real R6 does ~3.2 s), top 256 km/h stock, ~1.06 g braking, a 0.8 g bend = 38 deg lean, inner patches stay loaded, stands up in ~1 s when the bars are let go.
- Look: wet clear coat on the livery (MeshPhysical cc 1 / 0.03 + the car paint's rain beads and film on uWet), tyres darken and gloss with wetness (bikeWet), chrome / machined parts roughness x0.6-0.85, rims and pipes coated, smoked screen, amber indicator lenses that glow, dash glows with the key. Headlamp SpotLights move to the central projector (beam maps as the cars'). The lean pivots on the tread's curve (body shifted / lifted: tyres within about 1 cm of the road at 45 deg).
- Mirrors: MIRROR_GLASS.r6 (mat_mirrorview, no inner; `want`: per-body aim); CarMirrors skips a missing inner mirror and renders both mirrors from the seat. Adjust / fold work as the cars'.
- Garage: slot r6 (x 0, z -1.6) with per-slot size (pick box, walk box, drive collision); the live car's wheels hide while it is the bike (showroom copies of the cars show their clones). Stance tab (bike.rideF / rideR / rake / offset) and Performance tab (bike.power / revLimit / sprocket) rows, enabled only on the bike; stored in settings.bike (DEFAULTS.bike), so the cars keep theirs. Stats panel and showroom label use bikeStats.
- Camera: chase 3.9 m / 1.22 m with 32 % of the lean; cockpit = helmet at r6.json's eye (the head levels half the lean). Traffic contact box, autopilot wheelbase and tyre effects (spray, skids, tracks) use the bike's size; no windscreen water on the bike.
- Sound: BIKE_SFX: the Lexus V8 loops pitched x1.32 through audioRpm, the synth fires twice a revolution, rpm scaled to the red line. For recordings: put loops in interstate/sfx and set BIKE_SFX.layers = [[file, recorded rpm, loop s], ...] (the rpm is then used as is, no pitch-up).
- VR: seated at the eye point (the body-motion setting scales the lean); squeeze within 12 cm of a grip to hold it (the glove settles on it); both held = the bars steer (the hands' line), the right wrist rolled back = throttle (0.9 rad full; triggers still work); taking both grips with the engine off starts it (no key on the bike; I on the computer).
- Not built: a rider (the chase view shows the bike alone), wheelies, a bike dashboard display, the bike's own engine recordings. Not verified: anything on screen (no browser run, per instructions), sounds, VR feel, Quest frame time (244k triangles, two mirror renders a frame from the seat).
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef (browser globals only); Node with three r169: the packed GLB loads with the meshopt decoder (nodes, tyres on y 0, wheelbase 1.3748, trail 97.2 mm), the page's buildBike / bikeAnimate pose tree (wheels on the road within 0.4 mm through suspension, stance, rake and offset; lean as above; steering, spin, VR bars and twist, the indicator relay's self-cancel), the page's VehicleDynamics with the bike (numbers above).

## Interstate: motorcycle steering (bikeSteer; headset check needed)
- Cause of the bad feel: the input went straight to a front wheel angle (the cars' rack, lock aimed at 1.3 g), so full input at speed ran the tyres past their grip (old: up to 1.3 g peaks, 9.8 deg/s yaw wobble at the limit at 30 km/h), VR bars were wheel angle / lock (~7 deg of bar = full at 120 km/h), and the two patches of each tyre made yaw from their drive-force difference (open rear diff: the straight-line jitter, 0.18 deg/s rms / 0.74 max at 200 km/h), plus ESC and Ackermann acting on one tyre.
- Now (§14.14 BIKE_STEER / bikeSteer, called from the rack in VehicleDynamics#sub when not direct): input = lean request s x atan(aMax / g), aMax 0.8 g slow -> 0.9 g from 60 km/h (x road grip); yaw rate g tan(lean) / v, or the bars' own angle (s x 0.48 rad) when slow; a critically damped rider lag (w 8.5 -> 5.2 /s with speed, x1.3 standing up); front wheel = kinematic angle + measured understeer (0.0006-0.0014 rad per m/s2, more near the limit) + yaw-rate P + I (I limited to a trim that shrinks as it stands up), clamped to the front's grip; rack 18 rad/s. Bike only: drive / brake forces of the patches make no yaw moment, no Ackermann, no ESC. VR bars: the hands' turn is a lean request (bikeBarFull: the bar angle itself when slow, 0.34 rad = full lean at speed), not st.direct. Autopilot keeps its direct wheel angle. Cars: identical numbers (Node: Lexus / patrol step responses byte-identical before / after).
- Node (page's VehicleDynamics, straight road, speed held; before -> after): straight-line yaw jitter at 200 km/h 0.18 / 0.74 -> 0.000 deg/s rms / max; full input steady g 0.97 / 1.04 / 0.97 / 0.90 -> 0.80 / 0.91 / 0.94 / 0.92 at 30 / 60 / 120 / 200 km/h; yaw wobble in a full-lean turn at 30 km/h 9.8 -> 1.2 deg/s rms; t90 (step) 0.41 / 0.55 / 0.74 / 0.75 s; return to straight 0.3 / 0.43 / 0.64 / 0.82 s with 0.01-0.03 g past straight (was 0.07-0.17); gamepad 50 % = 21 deg lean (0.39 g) at every speed (was 34-38 deg); 10 % = 0.08 g (was 0.16-0.22). Bang-bang throttle at full lean still moves the yaw (4.5 deg/s rms): the rear tyre's friction circle.
- Checked: module parse, Node runs (scratchpad bst/steer.mjs, misc.mjs). Not checked by hand on keyboard / pad / headset.
- VR test (IWER emulator, Studio's chunk as an init script; scratch /tmp/qa/s/vrt.js): enter VR, the watch on the left glove's cuff (10 cm from the grip), X in the lobby starts Pursuit in the patrol car, B opens the garage and the left stick walks you (moved, at the emulator's ~1 fps). Found: entering VR from the garage screen started a run at once (VRRig.enter: menu -> startRun), so you never got to walk the garage; now it stays in the garage.
- Beacon light fix: the one scene PointLight over your own patrol car's bar lit the whole cabin and your body (no occlusion through the roof). From the seat (VR driving, cockpit or hood camera) your own bar gives no scene light; the bar, grille and rear flashers still flash, and nearby traffic units still light the scene.

Rocker / diffuser still switch.
