## Interstate build 1.42: stack interchange at junction B (§13.2d; Open road freeway route only; headset check needed)
- NET_FREEWAY B has `stack: true`: netJunction calls netStack after the loop ramps and flyovers. Three more one-way RampRoads (rr.stack), each laid with rampPoints and lifted (finishRamp, lift fn clearAll, cone STACK.cone 5.5 %) over everything built before it: A / B paving + 9.2 m (6.8 m under the 2.4 m box girder), any earlier ramp's deck + 9.0 m (parapet + 5.5 m), lateral reach roadOuter + 6 m so decks side by side never touch. `aim()` lays a ramp in plan, measures where it crosses A and shifts it along A so its crossing lands on fly-from's (the +d X); rampCheck 'stk' (R >= 100 m, grade <= 6.2 %) drops a failing ramp with a warning.
  - stk-c: A far -> B far, a Hermite U (hermiteMid 2.1, tangent at both ends since A may curve at the site) over both roads, level 3 at the X.
  - stk-d: B far -> A far, the mirror U round the junction itself (dd 175 m), concentric outside the loop ramp, level 2 over A and B; it merges onto A's far carriageway just before fly-to leaves it (STACK.flyToA 720 moves that diverge out at a stack junction).
  - stk-a: A far -> B near, a 2 km S-curve with a 700 m elevated approach beside A (it climbs over stk-c's diverge), level 4 at the X, 520 m run down to B.
- netStackFinish: a stack ramp is a viaduct (K1 0, spans) wherever its deck is 5 m or more over the land, not only over a road. RoadChunk: STACK_DECK profile (trapezoid box girder 2.4 m deep, 0.95 m concrete parapets that fold away where the ramp lies on A / B's paving, W-beam hidden). NetStatics: stack piers every 36 m, width 2 + 0.045 h (<= 3.6 m), hammerhead 1.5 m, skipped where the column would pass through a lower deck (throughDeck) and off every paving as before. netStackGantries: 3 A.gantries under the stack where every deck is >= 11.5 m up (A.stackS = the zone).
- Measured (Node, the page's own code, scratchpad stk/test.mjs, lanes 3): at A s ~1500 four levels: A 0, fly-from 8.0, stk-c 16.2, stk-a 24.1 m (stk-d 9.2 m over A at s 2199); every ramp ends on its lanes (height off <= 2 mm), grades 3.5 / 6.0 / 5.5 %, radii 199 / 176 / 362 m, every crossing >= 5.5 m under the upper soffit, 255 piers, none on a carriageway or through a deck, tallest 24 m; gantries at 1288 / 1408 / 1568 with 14-19 m clear. `?route=ring`: unchanged (no stack). Network build ~1.5 s. Not run in a browser (shared memory was crashing headless Chromium): the look of the decks, parapets and piers, traffic (it does not use slip roads, so the ramps are player-only), Quest frame time.

## Interstate: traffic car pack (ten passenger-car classes; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- interstate/cars/traffic-pack.glb (1.0 MB) + traffic-pack.json by tools/pack_carpack.py from the user's "Generic passenger car pack" (Sketchfab, Comrade1280, CC-BY-4.0: credited in the Credits page, docs/RELEASE_NOTES.md and the GLB's asset.extras). Each body re-framed (metres, front -z, wheels' bottom y 0, origin under the body centre; heading from the four wheels' PCA, the front = the end away from the red lenses), its wheels measured (axle z, radius, width, track half x) and dropped; optics split per triangle by the lens photo under it into optics_head / tail / indL / indR (rear clear lenses count as tail); glass kept. Body base colour 1024 px RGBA WebP: alpha = paint mask (glossy non-chrome from the metallicRoughness map, or the body's dominant hue for the matte off-roader), the paint turned to a luminance grey normalised so its 95th percentile is white; MR 512, the shared optics atlas 512; gltfpack meshopt.
- index.html: TRAFFIC_PACK (share / mass / speed / frontal per class), applyPackTraffic (after applyPoliceTraffic at run start: sedan / hatch / suv / pickup take the pack bodies, compact / wagon / coupe / minivan / offroad / sport are pushed; the Lexus stays as one class 'lexus', share .05), packTrafficGeometry (per class one merged body: MeshPhysical paint with the mask (colour where a = 1, clear coat .2 elsewhere, road grime low band, uWet), the shared traffic glass, lamp materials per kind lit by iLamp (head w, tail / brake x, indicators y / z) through the lens photo), GLB_CARS.pack (loaded by GlbCars.load('pack') in the run-start Promise.all). Without the pack (load failure) every class wears the Lexus as before.
- Checked: node --check of the module; Node load of the packed GLB with the game's three r169 + meshopt decoder (every node and material found, boxes match the JSON lengths, wheels inside the body). Not checked by eye (screenshots skipped: shared memory): the paint tint, lamp kinds, the compact / sport heading (their cabins sit ahead of the middle, the red lenses decided).

## Studio: sky loads when the UltraHDR file is broken by the host
- "THREE.UltraHDRLoader: Not a valid UltraHDR image" on PC: the host or a CDN served env/spruit_sunrise_4k.hdr.jpg without its gain-map metadata (re-compressed or stripped; the file in the repo is intact). `un()` now catches the loader error and `__sdrSky()` builds the sky from the plain JPEG instead (sRGB to linear half-float, highlights stretched x4 above 85 % luminance, flipY like the loader), so the room loads with a slightly flatter sky. Syntax check only.

## Interstate: performance pass for weak laptop GPUs (MacBook Air; save v7)
- Cause found: detectGPU gave every Apple GPU without "Pro/Max" in its name (MacBook Air M1-M3) and Intel Iris the High / Medium preset: a 2x Retina frame (maxDPR 2, ~4x the pixels of 1x) with a depth prepass, AO, bounce light, light shafts, depth of field and SMAA + 4x MSAA on half-float targets. Now weak GPUs (Apple base M, Intel, software) start on Low, Apple Pro on Medium, Max / Ultra on Ultra. Save v7: a weak GPU still on Medium / High / Ultra from an older build is moved to Low once (toast); every named preset is re-applied so the new Low values arrive.
- Low / Quest presets: no bloom on Low (the 10-pass mip chain), and windscreen water, rain splashes, slush shading, headlamp beam patterns (sign glow, oncoming glare) and road grime off (new keys in QUALITY_PRESETS; Medium and up keep them on). Low was already 1x density x 0.85 with FXAA, no prepass, no AO.
- Mirrors on Low: one mirror refreshed every other frame (each is a whole extra scene render: ~1 per frame -> 0.5), mirror far plane 700 m (was 2400).
- Traffic: every class's near / far InstancedMesh (12+ classes x up to 8 material groups) was drawn even with 0 instances: one draw each in the main pass, the shadow pass and every mirror. Empty ones are hidden per frame (userData.perfHide; RenderSystem.compile shows them for the shader warm-up so no first-sight hitch).
- Low / Quest: no beacon PointLight (it stayed in the scene at intensity 0, still a light in every lit pixel's loop).
- Dynamic resolution: Low / Quest may fall to 0.6 (was 0.85 at 1x); steps scale with the overload (0.05 / 0.08 / 0.12) and repeat after 0.6-1 s (was 0.05 every 1.2 s: ~4 s to reach the floor).
- Edges: on Apple GPUs (tile-based: MSAA resolves in tile memory) FXAA also gets 4x MSAA on the HDR target, so silhouettes and lamp details stop sparkling on Low without SMAA's three passes.
- Black screen ("flooded") on the Mac, likely cause: degenerate triangles in the new procedural meshes (van / semi lofts and draped lamps, deck profiles) got zero vertex normals from computeVertexNormals; normalize(vec3(0)) is NaN on Apple GPUs (Metal), and one NaN pixel in the HDR target is spread by the bloom mips (and TAA history) over the whole frame. Fixes: patchSafeNormals (BufferGeometry.computeVertexNormals wrapped at boot: zero / NaN normals become +y), NAN_SCRUB (NaN / negative / inf to 0) on the bloom high-pass input, the OutputPass input and the TAA output, tail-lens shader normals divided by max(length, 1e-6). Not reproduced here (SwiftShader does not make that NaN); verify on the Mac.
- Expected on a MacBook Air: from ~4x Retina pixels with 6+ heavy full-screen passes to 1x x 0.85 with sky + scene + output + FXAA + finish; fewer draw calls (empty traffic meshes, half the mirror renders). Not measured on the real GPU (no headset / Mac here); checked: node --check of the module; one headless load to the menu (SwiftShader: Low picked, bloom off, 31 programs linked, no page errors). A headless run of the drive itself was killed by the shared memory limit, so draw-call / CPU numbers were not measured.

## CQC: performance pass for weak laptops (LITE graphics; MacBook Air check needed)
- Biggest costs found (code reading + one headless load): auto quality started at the TOP level (step 123 Sharpness raised the desktop max to 2x: on a Retina MacBook that is 4x the pixels through the whole post chain: MSAA half-float target, bloom, SMAA, film pass with CAS) and stepped down one level every ~4 s; every lit pixel ran up to 8 extra full-BRDF fill lights (TFILL), the scanned-surface layers (SURF, two samples per map), floor / wall / prop / cloth wear macros, the torch-haze term and the bench lamp spot (5 spots + 2 rect area lights + a point light + the fill per pixel). Draw calls are moderate (title: 213 calls, 0.5 M triangles; renderer.info.autoReset is false, the profiler sums, so info.render.calls looks ~7x too high).
- LITE (beside OPT, before QS): ?lite=on|off, OPTIONS > GRAPHICS "Lite graphics (laptops)" (OPT.lite on / off), else auto from the WebGL renderer string (Intel, Apple M1-M4 without Pro / Max / Ultra, Safari "Apple GPU", software, low-end Radeon / mobile). Never in a headset. Fixed at load: lite adds `&surf=off&wear=off&wallwear=off&propwear=off&bodywear=off&torchhaze=off&benchlamp=off&wetx=off` to QS (an OPTIONS switch you turn back ON is stored 'on' and wins), so the lite programs lack that code (not zeroed uniforms). TFILL: 3 lights, diffuse only, loop bound 3 (compiled that way; non-lite unchanged 8 / RE_Direct).
- Lite numbers: maxPixelRatio 1 (SHARP.maxPr 1), boost levels 0.6-1.0 in 0.1 steps, SMAA off, GTAO 8 samples, shadows 1024 / standard (Real lighting only), anisotropy 4, enemy LODs at the headset distances, men off screen posed at 10 Hz, CONFIG.detail.minPx 2 (small parts cull sooner), gore drop pool 300, paint map copies 512, outdoor map with the headset's sky / textures / rain / drips / forest / 5 lamps per pixel / one triplanar plane / draw distance and fog. First load that detects a weak GPU switches to Sharp once (cqc.lite.seen.v1; your later choice stays).
- Auto quality (every computer): perfStart: lite starts one below its top, others at the highest level <= 1.25x (was the top); a frame rate under 70 % of the target drops two levels at once (hold 1.2 s); still far off at the lowest level for 3 samples = one mode lighter (Cinematic -> Sharp -> Max FPS, toast), at most twice a session.
- CPU: enemySkinSync updated each driven bone's whole subtree (O(n^2) per man per frame) and allocated 6 vectors per arm bone: now own matrix per bone + one subtree pass, scratch vectors (_skW). tfillTick no longer allocates and sorts a new array every frame.
- Bug check: eslint no-undef / dupe keys / redeclare / const-assign on the module: clean (browser globals only). One headless load (?dev&lite=on): no page errors, pixel ratio 1, 190 programs.
- Expected on a MacBook Air: Cinematic auto at <= 1x instead of 2x (about 4x fewer pixels, the post chain at a quarter of the cost); Sharp / Max FPS shading roughly a third to half cheaper per pixel (no SURF / wear layers, 3 diffuse fill lights instead of 8 BRDF, one spot light less). Not measured on a real Mac GPU.

- Raw in cqc/sounds/src/brass_drops_318964.wav. 20 drops cut by onset (silence gaps > 0.35 s), grouped by their ring frequency: brassL1-3 (4.5 kHz, big cases), brassR1-4 (6.4 kHz, rifle), brassM1-8 (9.9 kHz, mid), brassS1-5 (10.6-13 kHz, small); loudnorm -20 LUFS, 4 ms / 60 ms fades, mono mp3 (cqc/sounds/).

CQC wiring follows. node --check only.

## CQC: auto quality no longer causes repeated stalls
- Every auto-quality level change resizes the renderer and the whole post chain (a stall on a Mac). One stall made the 1 s sample read as a low frame rate, it dropped two levels (resize), climbed back later (resize) and repeated. perfTick now skips a computer sample with any frame over 0.2 s; a level that failed once is never retried, and raises are at least 25 s apart. Syntax parse only.

The gun is turned about the firing wrist (B.sides.R.W) until its front-part line (mean x / y of the drawn points ahead of 45 % of its length, bore -z) passes 4 cm from the support palm (wrist 0.4, knuckles 0.6), at most 20 deg; no change when it is already within 5 cm. Syntax parse only, not seen.

## CQC: hitch pass
- Auto quality: a sample with one stall now leaves that frame out instead of being skipped (constant stalls had stopped auto quality from ever stepping down); desktop starts at 1x without GTAO and climbs. roomCompile only where the browser has KHR_parallel_shader_compile (otherwise it is a stall itself).

## Interstate: menu road band
- Headless check of the menu scene: a dark diagonal band across the road was the shared edge of the road's two 600 m triangles (bump map slopes break along triangle edges; not a shadow: it stayed with shadows off). Menu road subdivided 24 x 28 and drawn without the bump map.
- Floating black layer over the map (found headless: a raycast through it hit "traffic" meshes 12-26 m away; every pack traffic car's geometry spanned 65535 m): the car pack (and other meshopt GLBs) store quantized 16-bit positions; geometry.clone().applyMatrix4 wrote metres back into the integer arrays. geoFloat(g) converts attributes to Float32 first; used in packTrafficGeometry, policeTrafficGeometry, the tree prep and the car-body batcher.
- Measured on Low (headless): 649 draw calls, 1.6 M triangles in a run.

## Interstate: fewer draw calls (Low preset; measured headless)
- Breakdown per frame on Low (onBeforeRender counts): traffic ~190 draws, road chunks ~145, your car ~120 (cabin, column, body), trees 1 per kind.
- mergeByMaterial(geos, mats): parts sharing a material merge into one group (one draw per material per instanced class). Pack traffic: the four lamp kinds share one material (lamp_all, kind from the aLk attribute), paint materials cached per source texture. Police traffic: one clone per source material (srcMat), then merged.
- Your cabin interior and column (cabin-* / column-* rigs) are not drawn from outside views on Low / Quest (car.cabHide from RenderSystem.render; CabinControls.update applies it).
- Road streaming ahead on Low / Quest: ~900 / 750 m (was ~1330 / 1000).
- Police traffic (31 materials, x2 for near / far = 62 draws): untextured opaque parts share two vertex-coloured materials (metal / non-metal, colour from each part's own), glass parts share one; textured parts, the light bar and tail lenses keep theirs.
- Fix (no roads or trees on the map): the road-streaming edit put a // comment in the middle of setDrawDistance's line, which commented out maxPool, so the main road never got any chunks (pool 0). maxPool restored.

## CQC: safe room appearing inside a stairwell

The safe room (8 x 6 m) is bigger than many rooms and overlapped the room you were in: gun locker rifles standing in the stairwell, GUNSMITH screen and bench lamp floating beside the stairs.

Checked: module parse; slot geometry probe headless (safe room n overlaps room n-1 at slots 20, 44, 54, 67 of the default plan).

One notch per 13 deg (0.22 rad), two at 31 deg (OFF -> START was 72 deg); back the same way. Haptic tick per notch as before; START held while gripped.

Now #watchOnArm (from #armsIK, after the left forearm is placed) puts it on the drawn forearm: 3.2 cm up from the wrist joint toward the elbow, on the side the back of the hand faces, case up off the arm, strap round the forearm axis, 12 toward the fingers. The old fixed spot stays as the fallback when the arms are not drawn. Touch-to-pause unchanged. Module parse only; not seen in a headset.

- TrafficSystem.#interact only ran player contacts for gF (cars going the traffic view's way). The ring has a reversed view for its far carriageway (trackRev), but the side loops (B, C, D, E) do not: on a loop's far carriageway, where a flyover or slip road can put you after a while, the cars around you are gO and you passed straight through them. #interact now also runs #contact (no scoring, no pass / near-miss events) for gO cars within reach. ## Interstate: hands in other views, snow in tunnel mouths, signs in walls, mirror refresh (headset check needed)
- VR chase / hood views: gloves, arms (and the watch on the glove) are not drawn (#handsHidden); cockpit as before.
- Tunnel mouths: a chunk crossing a portal built its verge along its whole length, so inside the bore the verge followed the ground (faded to the hill over the road) and a snow wedge rose across the far carriageway. #verge now collapses rows inside the tunnel span onto the nearer portal. Headless before / after at 15 m from the first portal: wedge gone.

- Checked: module parse; headless tunnel screenshots.

## Interstate: Lexus tail lamps are plain red glass (headset check needed)
- Cause of the black marks: the saloon's rear lamp mesh is a grid of reflector facets with gaps between them (the photo texture's alpha used to hide the gaps); drawn as a solid red lens the gaps showed the black lamp housing as blotches. Not shadows, reflections or other meshes in front (tested headless: the marks stayed with shadows off, reflections off, unlit and depth test off).
- celTailCover (buildCelsior): the rear-facing facets are rasterised into a 6 mm x / y grid (outermost z per cell), filled by scanlines per row and lamp and per column, and each filled cell becomes a quad 5 mm proud of the facets: one smooth plain red lens over each lamp (celTailMaterial: brighter red, emissive .3 base, + lamps / brakes in setLights). The facets stay behind it (and on the wrap-round sides); celTailOuter removed (it dropped triangles too); lens meshes receive no shadows; Material_106's photo glass stays skipped; blended layers inside the lamp boxes are cut (celCutTail).
- Traffic Lexus (CEL_TRAF): rear lens keeps every facet shell (the outer-shell filter is gone) but has no cover yet, so far traffic lenses can still show gaps.
- Checked headless: before / after close-ups of the player's car on the open road (plain red, no marks).

## Interstate: crashes no longer stall the engine
- VehicleDynamics: a hit (contact or barrier, dv > 0.5 m/s) sets hitT = 2 s. The stall (engine under ~430 rpm with drive engaged) now happens only with the manual-clutch transmission and not within 2 s of a hit; otherwise the clutch / converter slips (locked false, engine held at >= ~570 rpm) and the idle control brings it back, so no key restart after a crash. Module parse only.

Now four strips on the outer faces only. Found headless by a vertical raycast through the core (layers at 3.57 / 6.00 / 7.17 / 9.15 m; 6.00 is the core's ceiling, 9.15 the roof).

## CQC: AWM red dot usable
- Cause: seatPart measured the gun's top from gunMeshes, a cached list (g._vcloud) made while the AWM's factory 3-25x was still drawn, and awmDotSync ran before awmRefresh0 hid it. ## world/: working rules (from the user's feedback: do it right the first time)
- Before calling any visual change done: render the same framing as the matching photo in world/docs/references (same direction, height, field of view), put them side by side, list every difference (light and shadow, haze with distance, colour palette, density, detail at each distance), fix them, and only then report. Sample palettes from the photos (k-means of the relevant crop), never guess colours.
- Budget in the first pass, not after: headset triangles / draws / texture memory designed with the feature and measured with `?q=quest` (headset settings on a computer) before reporting. Tile big instanced sets so the frustum culls them.
- Shadows and aerial perspective are part of "realistic", not extras: no outdoor surface ships without the sun's shadow and distance haze.
- Realism max, from photo references, for everything: every surface textured and lit to fool the eye from any angle (walk round it, look up close, in VR), checked against the references. Listing / third-party photos are calibration only (copyright); real textures come from photo-scanned CC0 libraries (Poly Haven, ambientCG) or our own bakes.
- Far views are baked, never live geometry: what is seen through a window or past the play area is a baked panorama (depth for parallax), so the frame rate goes to what the player can touch. Use this tactic everywhere in game design (distant city, skyline, terrain beyond the playable area).
- Keep the whole project in view: don't sink all the time into one piece. Finish a piece to the bar, then move on (order now: window view done -> apartment interior, which matters a lot).
- No props or assets in the game that the user has not seen first: show them (renders next to the reference photo) and get a yes before they ship. Model only what the photos show.
- Geometry budget by clusters, not by cutting detail: big meshes are split into small clusters (meshlets, ~128 triangles) that are culled (frustum, back-facing cone, distance LOD) and drawn with multi-draw, so triangle counts can rise without losing frame rate. WebGL / WebGPU / the Quest browser have no mesh shaders, so this is the CPU / multi-draw form of a meshlet pipeline.
- Realism beats photo matching (user, Nov 8): the photos are a guide for layout, sizes and materials, not a colour target. Judge every change by "would this pass as a real photograph": physically based light (sun, sky, bounce, soft shadows, contact darkening), real-world material values and scans, real proportions and detail up close. Do not tune colours toward a photo at the cost of physical correctness.
- These rules belong to the repo (this file), so every session and environment of this project gets them.

## world/: surface realism (reflections, wet, darkness; master list 13B)
- The user's rule is now section 13B of world/docs/MASTER_PROMPT.md (every surface reads as its real material: reflections where it reflects, clear layers on polished / lacquered finishes, real glass, wet looks where water is, darkness where light cannot reach, darker joints; Quest form + PC form; check against references).
- New `world/src/surface.js` (setupSurfaces from main.js after the city): one onBeforeCompile patch on every apartment material (kinds in / glass / wet, one program each). Builder colliders now carry their material (`mat`), shell colliders `'shell'`.
- Reflections: 15 probes (every room with a reflective floor or walls + the terrace) baked at load with a CubeCamera (128 px), PMREM'd and copied into one HalfFloat atlas (4 x 4 tiles of 384 x 512, one sampler; shader reads a tile with three's cube-UV maths), box-projected per room; whole-floor fallback boxes. Each surface uses the box its reflected ray goes into (a point 12 cm along it), so a pane between a room and outside reflects the side the eye is on. Bake 2.8 s in SwiftShader (GPU: well under 1 s).
- Darkness: 5 cm top-down field (walls at chest height per floor, blurred 0.4 m; lowest down-facing surface overhead) -> floor / ceiling edge, inside corner, door head, under-stair occlusion; 20 cm daylight field (24 rays to glass or outside) -> sky fill down to 52 % deep in the core. Indirect light only.
- Finishes (PC clearcoat, Quest glossier plain): oak 0.5 / 0.16, parquet 0.6 / 0.13, stone and grey marble 0.85 / 0.06-0.07, onyx 1 / 0.04, travertine honed 0.25 / 0.3, doors and trim lacquered.
- Wet terrace: uWet (default 0.8, R cycles 0 / 0.45 / 0.8 / 1): pavers x0.42 albedo when soaked, roughness 0.1 film, puddles (roughness 0.02, flat normal) in a world-space noise. Glass: premultiplied blend, Fresnel reflection, 22 % grey absorption.
- Measured headless: hallway 22 % darker with the fields; wet terrace 26 % darker than dry with puddles mirroring the facade; no shader / page errors, 33 draws. Not checked on Quest (frame time of the probe loop: 17 box tests per reflection lookup).

- Found by comparison: no shadows (a third of a sunny view is deep shadow), haze wrong (first too weak, then grey and thick; the photos have clear air), pale sky, dark tar roofs dominating from above, shade too bright (no sun-to-sky contrast), too many towers on the park walls, an empty strip and buildings inside the park edges, oval lakes, cartoon park.
- city.js now: static two-cascade sun shadow map of every building part and tank (layer 2, MeshDepthMaterial override, rendered once at load; 3072 + 2048 px on a computer, 2048 + 1024 on Quest) sampled by every city material (cityInject: direct light x 4-tap PCF; height-integrated aerial perspective before tone mapping, uHazeK 0.85e-4; sky light x uSkyK 0.55, less in the canyons); palette k-means-sampled from the view photos and warmed (cream, tan, taupe, limestone, muted brick); light grey / white roofs with plant units and their shadows; belt courses, grouped bays, stains, setback shading; park as individual crowns (jittered 9 m grid, lit by their own normals, shadowed by the buildings), lawns and meadows, paths only on open ground, irregular lake shores; streetwalls stop at the park avenues; park walls 15-20 storeys with the odd tower; glass in grey / blue-green / bronze, more crowns; antennas rare.
- Sky deeper blue (zenith #2a5fb8, gradient ^0.38); exposure 1.32; apartment glass absorption 0.08.
- Budget: parts tiled in 2 km instanced meshes (frustum culled); detail by distance (CLOSE_R cornices / belts / second roof units, NEAR_R silhouettes and narrow streetwall, MID_R tiers, beyond one box; wider far buildings). ## world/: the view is real Manhattan (baked); Google 3D tiles optional (in progress)
- User: "exactly like the photo references". The window photos are real Manhattan from 111 W 57th St, so the view is now built from public data and baked offline. Chosen with the user: both a baked public-data view (default) and Google Photorealistic 3D Tiles live with their own key.
- Data (tools/nyc, Python; downloads in world/.cache/nyc, processed data in world/bake/data, both gitignored, re-fetchable): fetch_aerial.py (USGS NAIP 2019 RGB + near infrared, USGS hydrography; resampled into the game grid: x = street-grid east, -z = grid north, 28.9 deg), prepare_lod2.py (NYC 3D Building Model, OTI multipatch, DA12 + DA13 = Manhattan: 3.76 M triangles, 47.8 k buildings), prepare_prisms.py (NYC Building Footprints for everything else and post-2014 towers: 63.6 k prisms; one bad record of 649 km skipped), pack_attrs.py (year, height, landmark facade overrides by BIN), prepare_ground.py (water, tree crowns as paraboloids per detected treetop, NJ Palisades, terrain, de-lit and plant-graded aerial mosaics G0-G3). config.json: origin, grid angle, anchor (111 W 57th = BIN 1023728, excluded), probes (9 eye points), suns.
- Baker (bake/index.html + bake.js, headless Chromium + SwiftShader via tools/nyc/bake_probes.js): depth pre-pass, then per visible pixel: sun shadows and sky occlusion ray-marched through a 4-level height map of buildings + crowns + terrain, facades by era (windows in world metres, rows and columns fade separately), the aerial photo as ground under the game's sun, crowns relief-marched, water with sky reflection, height-integrated haze, neutral tone map. A 2048 face at 1.5x supersampling: ~80 s.
- Game: src/view.js draws each baked face as a grid mesh displaced to the baked distance (parallax), nearest bake point with a fade, T = next time of day (afternoon 245/27 deg matches apartment-21, morning 70/22 deg matches apartment-16); the interior sun follows. src/tiles.js: Google 3D tiles (vendored 3d-tiles-renderer 0.4.28, Draco), key on the start card (localStorage world.gkey), G switches; attribution shown. ?city=gen = the old generated city.
- Calibrated side by side against apartment-21 / -16 (same direction and lens): park colour, sun direction from which facades are lit, haze, palette.

## world/: meshlet-style clusters (both scenes; headset check needed)
- view.js clusterFace: each baked face = one shared (dsize+1)^2 vertex grid, index lists per quadrant of 16x16-quad tiles; all-sky tiles dropped, tiles mixing sky / big depth jumps at full detail, the rest stepped by distance (<200 m 1, <500 2, <1.5 km 4, <4 km 8, else 16; headset at least 2); quadrants culled by direction cone (viewHalfAngle). 655k -> 207k triangles, 23k-122k drawn.
- clusters.js: apartment + furniture meshes (opaque, non-indexed) -> meshoptimizer 1.3 meshlets (64 v / 124 t, vendor/meshopt, MIT) in one BatchedMesh per material; per-cluster frustum culling (three) and normal-cone culling (meshoptimizer bounds) in onBeforeRender; shadow passes draw all clusters. 69 meshes -> 4292 clusters (310k triangles); drawn interior triangles 3-16x fewer. ?clusters=off = whole meshes.
- Fig leaves: ambientCG Leaf001 photo atlas (leaf_ficus), 4 x 6 curved blades, ~380 leaves.
- Real depth instead of painted detail (Nov 7): ceilings are cut open round every downlight (surface.js srHoles: per-floor lamp offset textures, 5 cm cells; down-facing fragments within 5 cm of a lamp at its height are discarded) and an 8 cm black can with reflector ring and lens sits in the hole; floors are cut open over the window grilles (4 strip rects) over a black trough 6 cm down with real aluminium bars; stone cladding is laid as separate slabs (onyx 1.2 x 1.2, travertine 0.6 x 1.2, grey marble 0.6 x 0.6) with 1.5 mm reveals over a dark setting bed, cut on world multiples so the real joints meet the scans' joints; vanities have separate drawer fronts with 3 mm gaps, bronze pulls and a recessed toe kick; built-in travertine tub with a deck cut-out and enamel basin; wall-hung egg toilet. Downlights also light the probe bake (pool of 8 spots per probe, 1.05 rad, power 16).
- Room review (Nov 7): every room rendered N / E / S / W / down / up (78 deg) next to its photos (scratch /tmp/qa/review.py). Ranked findings: 1 colour cast (peach) -> white balance 1.16 / 1.0 / 0.88, lamps #fffaf4 (daylit walls now 0.38 / 0.34 / 0.28 like the photos); 2 flat lighting (no falloff from windows, no pooling) -> irradiance volume; 3 flat ceilings -> tray ceilings with LED coves on floor two (45 cm band, 22 cm drop, lit strip on the inner face); 4 stone repeats -> stone tiles 4.8 m at 3072 px, each 2 x 2 bookmatched set from its own window; 5 generated art / rugs -> painterly canvas (layered bristle strokes + relief normal), tone-on-tone distressed bedroom rug; 6 bath details -> primary bath mirror wall with amber backlit panels (vanity wall param), towels, wood block and travertine stools; powder room and third bath honed grey marble to 1.2 m with a textured bronze wallcovering above; sheers at the bedroom / library corners. Still open: kitchen L-return and cooktop, art on the halls and landing, gold leaf chandelier, nickel tub reads white.
- Irradiance volume (surface.js IV, srIV): L1 SH probes every 0.5 m (37 x 41) on 8 layers (0.35 / 1.2 / 2.2 / 3.3 and 4.95 / 5.8 / 6.8 / 7.8 m), probes inside colliders or outside the plate filled from neighbours; captured at 16 px with the outside at daylight level and the room's 8 nearest downlights on; stored as textures/light/volume.json + .bin (12 half floats per probe), sampled trilinear within a floor at p + 0.18 n (8 fetches). tools/interior/bake_volume.js bakes it offline (~70 min in SwiftShader). ?volume=off = per-room SH.
- Real-shaped fixtures (src/hw.js, catalogue sizes, round sections): lever handles (54 mm rose, 130 mm round-bar lever, both faces of every door leaf and closed door), butt hinge knuckles, 19 mm bar pulls on standoffs (fridge / pantry pairs at the door seams, oven handles, vanity drawers), countertops extruded with real cut-outs, undermount basins with depth (kitchen stainless 600 x 420 x 220, vanity ceramic 460 x 340 x 150) and drains, pull-down kitchen faucet, deck bath faucets, toilet seat ring + lid with hinge caps, dual-flush plate, shower set (trim + lever, wall arm and 200 mm head, hand shower on hose and holder), towel ring. Checked in close-up renders.

Gallery south end (floor plan): a fig in a stone planter.
- Spiral stair vs apartment-12: closed stringers both sides (7 cm, smooth helical top 12 cm over the nosing line; the old side faces stepped up tread by tread), treads run 1 cm into them, balusters are 17 mm round bars standing on the inner stringer, the outer face stands 5 mm off the curved wall. The stair hall's curved wall is greige plaster (#c9b9aa, sampled from the photo), drawn with 192 segments. The volume bake still has the old cream wall (bounce slightly off until the next bake).
- Upper landing (apartment-11): travertine waterfall console (console_ stone option) with a ribbed ochre stoneware vase of dried seed-head stems and a brass sphere, instead of the dark oak bench and bowl.
- Lighting pass vs the photos (apartment-17 / -19 / -10, same framings): contact shadows were missing (chairs, sofas, beds floated). bakeFields now rasterises every furniture collider standing on a floor (c.furn, set in main.js) into a 5 cm field (taller = darker), blurred 15 cm twice (ctTex, uSrCT); srAO darkens floors round the footprint (up to 60 %) and the base of vertical faces within ~15 cm of the floor (40 %); indirect light only, Quest too. Measured floor / chair tones match the photos within ~5 %; walls read yellower than the photos, so the probe white balance is 1.1 / 1.0 / 0.93 (was 1.16 / 1.0 / 0.88). Volume re-baked with it and the greige stair hall.
- Clipping pass (world/): /tmp/qa/check.js style test in the page (window.__world.furn.art lists wall-hung art): door leaves vs furniture / walls, furniture vs walls, art vs leaves / furniture / walls and a wall behind every corner. Fixed: library door swung into the shelving (shelving now 2.1 m, east of the swing), bedroom three's door grazed the bed (opens 0.22 rad wider), library lamp table stood in a column, media shelving ran into the closet block (2.3 m, centred 4.5) and the curved sofa into the south glass, bed three nightstands overlapped the bed, the media print hung over a slot window, the stair hall's curved wall was 15 cm thick and showed through the floor-two bath wall (now 1.5 cm, inside the flat walls). Check now reports nothing but intended overlaps (chairs under tables, stool under the vanity, sofa segments).
- Dressing hall (apartment-07, floor-two closet): white lacquer vanity on fluted pill legs, 1.05 x 1.62 m framed mirror, bouclé barrel stool, onyx plinth with a white carved sculpture, upholstered bench and two prints, four downlights and a slot diffuser.

## The apartment replaces the office (Nov 8)
- Deleted: the Studio office (assets/ bundle, addons/, env/, textures/, backend/, commands.json, tools/make_registry.py). Root index.html redirects to world/index.html (with ?from=door when you come back from Interstate / CQC, so you arrive at the front door). Their "Back to studio" buttons now read "Back to the apartment". Rally lived in the office engine and went with it.
- world/src/hub.js: one canvas touch screen drawn twice: the library computer (furniture.js desk: dark oak on bronze legs, monitor on a stand, keyboard, mouse; chair; the armchair and lamp table there were removed, the shard print moved to z 5.3) and a bronze-framed panel beside the front door (floor one, end of the hallway: the old fire door is now a 1 x 2.74 m dark oak entry door). Rows Interstate / CQC Loop, GO (fade, ends the VR session, navigates); the computer adds time of day, terrace wet / dry, frame meter. Destination saved in localStorage world.door.dest. Computer: centre dot + click or E within 3.2 m; headset: controller ray + trigger (laser shows only on a screen).
- Tested headless: root redirects, both screens draw, choosing CQC + GO lands on cqc/index.html, no page errors. Not tried in a headset.
- Next (asked, not built): training enemies only at the apartment's farthest spot or outside in a wooded courtyard for sniping; needs guns in the apartment first.
- Guns and training enemies in the apartment (world/src/arms.js, enemies.js): the CQC Glock 17, AKM and AWM (../cqc/models, the old studio range's bake numbers) held in front of the camera / in the right hand; hitscan against enemy capsules (three) and the apartment colliders (Rapier ray, player excluded); bullet holes (80 pooled) and dust; recorded shots and magazine sounds from ../cqc/sounds; recoil kicks the view; AWM 6x scope (DOM mil-dot, wheel 3-25x, mouse slowed by the zoom); AKM full auto 600 rpm. Keys 1 / 2 / 3, Q away, click fire, right button aim, R reload; headset: right grip cycles, trigger fires (not while pointing at a screen), A reloads. Enemies: the CQC soldier, relaxed pose, turn to face you, flinch, topple, get up after 7 s; spawned (computer screen "+ Enemy", N) at the clear standing spot farthest from you (12 spots, both floors; floor difference counts 7 m); at most 6. Headless: AKM shots killed one upstairs from 2.5 m, guns render at the hip, no page errors. Courtyard / sniping outside not built.

## CQC: the apartment as the start room (start mode 'apt', the default; ?start=apt|cqc|outdoor|outonly)
- APARTMENT START section at the end of cqc/index.html, built like the outdoor yard: slot 1 = plan type 'apt' (ROOM_TYPES.apt, no men), slot 2 a stairwell down, then the loop. CQC's first safe room (slot 0: locker, gunsmith, bench) is behind the apartment's east passage; the front door (floor one, end of the hallway) is a vestibule through the fire-stair core to CQC's own exit door in the west wall. You are put in the great hall (aptTick) once the room is active; a death respawns you in the safe room as before.
- aptBuilder imports ../world/src/materials.js, apartment.js, furniture.js (preloaded at boot by aptLoad: the first rooms are built without returning to the event loop) with Q { base: '../world/', cqc: true } (apartment.js carves the two doorways and drops the old front door panel when Q.cqc). Frame: local x = 9.625 - apartment z, local z = apartment x - 8.8 (APT, aptLX / aptLZ). Room 19.25 x 17.6 x 8.6 m.
- aptActivate: the shell pass's walls go; every apartment collider (box / obox; hulls and trimeshes as Rapier only) becomes a Rapier collider on the room body and, except floor / ceiling slabs, a walk box (floor-two ones get bot); end walls in the gaps to both neighbours; nav grid by yardNav (floor one). aptFloor: the helical stair ramp (26 risers over 330 deg) when within 0.9 m of your floor, floor two (4.6) when you come from up there, else floor one; only the ground re-bases. Pool fittings: the 8 floor-one downlights nearest the great hall's middle.
- world/: materials / furniture / view take Q.base for their texture and city paths.
- Headless (lite, SwiftShader): boots into the apartment, 285 walk boxes, 349 colliders, PLAY shows the great hall with CQC's help panel. Not yet: city view and sky outside the windows (black), apartment-quality light, the Interstate panel, enemies at the far end, root page redirect.
- Apartment in CQC, part 2: the baked city view outside the windows (world/src/view.js: uCmp squeezes the city along each ray into 30-57 m so CQC's 60 m camera keeps it; modelMatrix now applied; APT_CAM gives view.update the camera in the apartment's frame), a DirectionalLight sun (shadowed, aimed from the view's sun) and a HemisphereLight fill, both at 0 outside the apartment; glass made plain clear (no surface.js). Front door panel (APTP, wall beside the vestibule): GO Interstate, + Enemy (CQC's own AI, spawned at the floor-one nav cell farthest from you), Clear enemies; desktop dot + E / click, headset fingertip (glassFinger). Root index.html opens cqc/index.html?start=apt; CQC's title link now goes to the Interstate. Nav grid: floor / slab / roof boxes give no walk boxes.

## CQC apartment: world surfaces (surface.js) inside CQC (?aptsurf=off; not run in a browser; headset check needed)
- world/src/surface.js setupSurfaces takes `frame` (the apartment group) and `bakeLamps` (default true). With a frame the patched shaders (cache key sr5f-<kind>) read apartment coordinates: vSrPos / vSrNrm = uSrInv (inverse of frame.matrixWorld) x world; reflected rays / normals go to apartment axes for the box tests, box projection and the irradiance volume (SR_A), and back to world axes for the probe atlas lookup (SR_W, transpose), whose cube captures are world-aligned at toW(probe pos); the SH probes stay in world axes. syncFrame() (returned) updates uSrInv when the frame's world matrix changed. No frame = identity macros, same cache key sr5-: the world page behaves as before.
- cqc/index.html: aptLoad imports surface.js as A.mod[4]; aptBuilder calls setupSurfaces after the group is placed (furniture colliders copied with furn: true for contact shadows, furniture meshes noOver, bakeLamps false: no temporary spot lights, CQC's light count stays fixed), loads world/textures/light/volume.json + .bin into useVolume, keeps the plain clear override for the balustrade glass (and for the window glass only if setupSurfaces fails). aptViewTick calls syncFrame every frame and bakes the probes once (setTimeout, outside the builder and the frame) when the scans are in (M.ready), the view is loaded and you are in the apartment; after it the hemisphere fill drops to 0.12 (the probes carry the bounce), view gain back to APT.viewGain.
- Not verified: anything on screen (GLSL not compiled here), the bake stall length in CQC (cube captures render the whole CQC scene), downlights not in the probes (bakeLamps false), baking while in VR.
- Apartment surface shaders in CQC are opt-in for now (?aptsurf=on): headless they compile and link (229 programs, 0 failed, probe bake 57 s in SwiftShader), but every headless PLAY with them on ran the tab out of memory (the probe bake renders the whole CQC scene into cube maps). Without them PLAY works. Check on a real GPU / Quest before turning them on by default.
- Apartment start, choices: the start screen says "The apartment" (title script checks ?start / cqc.start.v1; body cam reel hidden). Nothing past the apartment is built until you pick on the front door panel: "CQC loop" (stairwell down, then the rooms) or "Outpost 17 (outdoor)" (the yard, then its stairwell) via aptChoose (re-plans slot b+1 on, like yardReplan; refused once a fight out there started); until then the exit door stays locked (next room not built). Interstate, + Enemy, Clear stay on the panel. Headless: title text, re-plans cqc -> stair, outdoor -> yard, stair, back.
- Raids (library computer, floor two: APTC panel on the desk monitor, "Apartment security"): Breach (4 through the front door), Pincer (front door + armory passage), Terrace assault (3 over the terrace), Siege (4 waves, the third with 2 men holding spots upstairs, waves also on a 28 s clock), End the raid. Men spawn at APT_ENTRY points (apartment coords; APTS.forced overrides aptSpawnSpot), 0.9-1.3 s apart, already hunting you (aiNoise 1.2 at your position), skill 0.55 / aggr 0.7, SMG / shotgun / pistol; a breach plays the door kick at the front door; toast + vrNote per wave; next wave 4 s after a wave is down; "Raid over" when the last is down. The screen blinks red while a raid is on. Upstairs men hold (stairHold, stairY 4.6); floor-one men cannot climb the stair (nav is floor one). Front door panel keeps its rows; both panels share aptPanelMake / aptPanelDraw / aptPanelTick (desktop dot + E / click, headset fingertip).
- Reflections without the probe bake (aptsurf off, the default): aptEnvCapture once after you enter (setTimeout, outside the frame): the baked city + sky alone and uncompressed in a 256 cube (128 headset / lite) from the great hall, PMREM, envMap on every lit apartment material, envMapIntensity 0.18 + 0.55 x gloss + 0.3 x metalness + 0.15 x clearcoat. Headless: 0.5 s, 92 meshes; raid test: wave 1 = 3 men at the front door all hunting, wave 2 (4) after they were down, End clears. Not checked by eye or in a headset.

## CQC apartment: loose furniture is physics, windows shatter (?aptprops=off; headset check needed)
- world/src/furniture.js with Q.cqc: dining chairs (7 kg), armchairs (16), counter stools (6), the barrel stool (9), side tables (30) and table lamps (3.5) are built on their own (movable(): origin at the foot, unturned) and returned as furn.movables { kind, mass, group, box, x, y, z, ry } instead of being merged with static colliders. The world page is unchanged.
- cqc/index.html aptMovables (end of aptActivate): each becomes an addProp box (its drawn bounds, 4 mm in) at its place, resting on what is under it (aptSupport: the highest furniture top within 12 cm above its foot, e.g. a lamp on a nightstand or a side table placed earlier). Rounds, kicks, blasts, bodies and you push them; they tip over and knock (KNOCK_MAT chair / block / glass); lamps break like glass. Props go with the room (spawnRoom), rebuilt fresh on re-activation. ?aptprops=off = the same boxes as static colliders, the pieces drawn fixed.
- Windows: world/src/apartment.js with Q.cqc records every window pane (glass runs cut at their mullions) as its own collider (c.pane) and draws them as one InstancedMesh. aptPaneBreak: a round (bulletHit, also on the way through: no exit hole) or a grenade within ~6 m (aptPanesBlast from fragBlast) hides the instance, removes its collider and walk box, bursts glass along the shot, shards on the floor (glassFloor), the crash (glassSfx). Broken panes stay broken while the room lives.
- Known: with ?aptsurf=on the instanced panes get surface.js's glass shader, which does not read the instance matrix (opt-in path only).

## CQC: men on both apartment floors, corner peeks, enemy grenades (?aptnav=off, ?enade=off; headset check needed)
- APT NAV section (end of cqc/index.html): aptNavBuild (from aptActivate) adds a floor-two grid (room.aptNav.G2: over the slab north of the south glass, less the stair void, blocked by walk boxes that hold at L2, colOnFloor's rule) beside floor one's (room.navCache; its stair disc is shut below flight angle 3.1 rad, the low treads; the high half is walked under). findPath hands apartment searches to aptFindPath (man found by his position): same floor = one grid; else grid -> foot (east of the first riser) or top (off the landing past the guard's end, clear of the lift core) -> tread-middle waypoints every 0.22 rad -> the other grid; replans from mid-flight too. e.aptY (aptEnemyY from enemyMove: aptFloor with his own hint, 1.6 m/s on the treads) drives enemyFloorY / enemyBaseY, his collisions (fy) and teammate pushes; e.aptKLv = your floor when his last-known point changed. Target floor: a cover point's own (his own when a corner is on both), near his last-known -> aptKLv, else the only floor that has it, else his own. Siege upstairs holders now really stand on floor two (enemyFloorY ignored stairY in the apartment: they were drawn on floor one). Men on the stair / upstairs die into the ragdoll (no downed crawl there). "+ Enemy" may spawn upstairs (the other floor counts 7 m).
- Cover (aptCoverBuild / aptCover via coverPoints): corners of tall walk boxes (wall ends, jambs, columns, tall furniture; not glass, not the curved wall's chords) as high cover that steps out along the face (cover.source.aptPv: enemyMove peeks along it, the lean follows), low furniture 0.95-1.3 m as low cover; per floor (c.lv, c.y0); chooseCover / aiDecide take only his floor's and test "hidden" at its floor height. 52 + 57 corners, 8 + 8 low.
- ENEMY GRENADES section (every CQC room; CONFIG.enemy.nades, OPTIONS PHYSICS "Enemies throw grenades"): enadeTick after enemyThink. Twice a second an engaged man in cover / peek / fight / ambush / search / suppress scores pressure (you hidden 2.5-18 s 0.5, suppression up to 0.55, mates down 0.25 each, you within 7 m up to 0.45, hurt, just hit, aggression); over press 0.55 a roll (rate 0.35). Per man 1-2, one a room per 12 s; never with a teammate within 6.5 m (frag) / 3.5 m (flash) of the landing point, a frag not within 4.5 m of himself; flash when you are within 6 m or 45 %. Shout (radio call + "Grenade!" / "Flashbang!" toast / vrNote when you can hear him), gun down, standing, out past his corner; left arm IK from the vest to cocked over the shoulder (enadeArm, grenade drawn in the hand), 0.7 s later a real grenade item (createNade, pin out, nadeSpoon: real fuse, NREC swing + spoon) on a lob solved for the target (36 / 26 / 17 / 46 / 9 deg tried, each arc checked by 10 LOS rays: walls, ceilings, doors, props), a frag aimed 0.7 m short, error by skill. It goes off through fragBlast / flashBlast, his men flee it (nadeEvade), you can throw it back. Killed with the pin out: it drops live. ?dev: ENADE (stat counts), enade*, APT_NAV, apt* nav functions.
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef; Node with the real apartment colliders (world/src built in Node, aptActivate's walk-box rule) and the page's own nav code: 7 cross-floor / same-floor routes walked with enemyMove's push-out (great hall <-> primary bath / bedroom, kitchen -> bedroom three, primary bath -> media room, bedroom -> hallway, both mid-flight replans), stair link ends open, siege spots free on floor two, every cover point and step-out open; grenade planning (lands on the target, 3 m ceiling -> flatter lob, blocked arcs -> no throw, teammate rules, pressure) and the throw flow with stubbed services (throw within seconds of you hiding, room cooldown, drop when killed). Found: floor two's library is closed off for the men by its furniture (sofa segments + shelving leave no 0.52 m gap). Not run in a browser (other agents' browsers were using the shared memory): the arm pose and grenade in his hand by eye, the lob in real rooms, peeks at jambs, the stair walk on the skinned model, Quest frame time.
## CQC apartment: gun rack beside the raid computer, walls tear (?aptrack=off, ?aptwalls=off; headset check needed)
- Gun rack (APTR, cqc/index.html "APARTMENT GUN RACK" section after the apartment section): wall-mounted on the library's west partition (floor two, apartment x 12.78, z 3.89..5.35, between the desk and the abstract print; the print moves to z 6.05 with Q.cqc in world/src/furniture.js). Dark oak board, crown, felt-lined oak shelf on black steel brackets, brass screws, APARTMENT ARMORY plate. AKM / M4A1-S / AWM on rubber-sleeved J-hooks (rows bottom up from the drawn bounds; hooks under each gun's measured section at 36 % from the muzzle and 20 % from the butt; muzzles south, left side out), Glock 17 and M1911 standing muzzle-in on pegs, two magazines of each gun standing on the shelf. Real items, kinematic at rest (GL's rules: grip / E / click takes them, never a second of a type you carry). Stocked by aptRackTick once the gun models are in (after PLAY), on every activation and when a raid starts (aptRaidStart). Board and shelf colliders, one floor-two walk box.
- Wall damage (AWALL): the 53 apartment partitions (plaster boxes 8-24 cm thick of its own colliders) are stud walls with PDMG-style shaped holes. One R8 mask atlas (2048 x 1178, 2 cm texels, both faces), a 5 cm top-down index texture (channel per floor) and a float data texture; an onBeforeCompile on the apartment plaster (key +|aw) reads them in apartment coordinates (uAwInv = the apartment group's inverse, aptWallsTick): open = the view looks into the cavity (far board's paper back, a galvanised stud web every 406 mm where the view crosses one) or through (discard) where the far face is open too; crushed paint and gypsum core round the holes. Rounds: coverHit hook (aptWallHit): entry sheet, stud, exit sheet (CONFIG.cover.resist), open points free; each round tears its own hole (entry ~1 cm, exit 2-3 cm), cells (CONFIG.cover.cell / cellHp / splash) break out as ~17 cm ragged blobs with board chunks and dust, decals in a blob purged. Holes and cell damage kept in room memory (awh / awc) and re-stamped on a rebuild. Thick plaster, cores and stone keep step 119's rules (holes, spall). Not done: the walls' shadows (depth material) ignore the holes; skirting / stone cladding over a holed wall stays drawn; ?aptsurf=on path not checked with it.
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef, Node test with the real apartment colliders (scratchpad aw/test.mjs: picks, atlas packing, index / data, stamp / open, entry + exit + stud costs, blobs, memory, world mapping, rack clearances) and the shader through glslang; one headless run (?dev&start=apt&lite=on): 53 walls, 3 |aw programs linked, rack stocked after PLAY (5 guns + 10 magazines), 8 AKM rounds through the library wall opened 1652 cm2 (35 holes); the page then ran out of memory (shared cgroup). Not seen by eye, not in a headset.
## Apartment: real sofas, recessed basins, working mirrors, wood tops (world/src; headset check needed)
- Soft upholstery kit (furniture.js): softBox (densely gridded rounded box, tan-spaced edge rows, every vertex through a deform(p, n); faces nobody sees left out), piping (welt tube along a face seam, moved with the same deform), cushionDeform (crowned faces, boxing bulge, seat sag, seam wrinkles, fill lumps). cushion() uses it everywhere (chairs, banquette, towels). SOFT grid: computer 4.5 cm / 3 edge rows, headset 8 cm / 2.
- Sofas (sofaParts): recessed black plinth, sprung base, back frame, rolled arms with piped fronts, loose seat cushions (12 mm gaps, each sat in differently, piped top and bottom), back cushions leaning back 12 deg (fuller at the lumbar), throw pillows with a chop dent. curvedSofa bends the same parts round its arc (bendArc). Soft contact shadow cards (M.contact, MeshBasic multiply) under sofas and beds; surface.js now skips non-standard materials.
- Triangles (furniture, all): computer 628k -> 753k, headset 628k -> 662k.
- Basins: the vanity and kitchen carcasses were solid to the counter, so their tops covered the bowls (basins read flat). Now solid only below the bowls with an 18 mm frame round them; vanity basins get an overflow slot.
- Wood: new scans wood_walnut (Poly Haven natural_walnut_veneer) and wood_oak (oak_veneer_01, 1.83 m), CC0, via world/tools/interior fetch_scans.py / build_scans.py; M.walnut (lacquered), M.oakNat (oiled). boardTop lays tops as glued boards along their length, each its own cut of the scan (wcut), grainUp for legs. Walnut: dining table top (on the travertine pedestals) with undercut, desk, nightstands, oval breakfast table, console; oak: coffee table frames (second one solid oak top). Dining table bowl + brass candlesticks, kitchen walnut board and utensil crock.
- Mirrors (new world/src/mirrors.js, setupMirrors(group, renderer, Q, meshes, opts) -> tick(camera)): every mirror has its own material with userData.mirror { p, n } (furniture.js mirrorMat; furnish returns mirrors). The nearest you face within 6 m is a planar reflection (mirrored camera, oblique near plane, drawn in onBeforeRender per camera / per eye, half the screen on a computer, 512 px on the headset); others get a cube capture from 1.2 m in front (opts.cubes; the world page passes false, its probes do it). CQC hooks: aptBuilder imports it, aptViewTick ticks it. clusters.js skips mirror meshes. ?dev exposes window.__aptMirrors.

## CQC apartment: blood and holes on the drawn surface (?aptsnap=off)
- The apartment's colliders are boxes round what is drawn (sofa boxes over the cushions, the curved stair wall as chords, rugs on the floor box), so blood and bullet holes placed at collider hits floated. aptTriBuild (once, 0 ms after activation, ~0.3 s headless) puts every static apartment triangle (architecture + fixed furniture, apartment space, 683k) in a 0.5 m grid; aptSnap (from addBloodDecal and addRoomHole) casts 30 cm either side of the hit along -n and moves the decal onto the drawn triangle nearest the hit, with its face normal. No drawn surface within reach = no decal. Headless: build 332 ms, queries 0-7.5 ms, floor points land at 0.000 with normal up.

## CQC apartment: performance (clusters, shader warm-up)
- Profiled a raid headless: game JS under 2 % of a frame; the cost is drawing (the apartment's merged meshes were drawn whole: ~772k triangles a frame) and shaders linked mid-fight.
- world/src/clusters.js now runs in CQC too (aptBuilder, needs WEBGL_multi_draw; ?aptclusters=off; skipped with ?aptsurf=on): 87 meshes -> 12,179 meshlets in BatchedMeshes, per-cluster frustum + normal-cone culling. Fixed for a moved / turned parent (the cone test now takes the camera in the mesh's own space; the mesh's matrix is copied). Old geometry disposed; aptTriBuild re-runs on the cluster meshes. Headless: 772k -> 214k triangles a frame (216k mid-raid).
- On first entering the apartment: enemyPrecompile + renderer.compileAsync(scene, camera) (A.warmed), so the men, their guns and grenades compile under the apartment's lights before a raid. Mid-raid new programs 5 -> 2.

## CQC: men fall back under fire; apartment lights, glass and furniture break (?fallback=off, ?aptbreak=off; headset check needed)
- Fall back (MEN FALL BACK section, end of cqc/index.html; CONFIG.enemy.fallback { trip 2.6, off 0.8, tau 1.7, hit 1.1, gain 1.5, reach 13, hold [2.5, 9], cool [7, 13] }; OPTIONS PHYSICS "Men fall back under fire"): every round passing within CONFIG.ai.passBy of a man's head (efbPass from aiRoundPassed, more the closer, +0.35 inside 30 cm) and every hit on him adds head pressure e.fbP (eased over tau). Past trip x aggression (0.75-1.4) x rush x morale an engaged man (fight / peek / cover / suppress / move / ambush / search / hunt) breaks off (efbTick after enemyThink): cover farther from you by gain m, hidden from you, never a move toward you (efbSpot), else straight back 3-5 m; aiGoTo 'fallback' + radio call; going there he faces you (enemyFace) and throws blind bursts back at where you were (efbBlind into enemyFire's blind branch); in the ambush he arrives in he stays down (no peek) while it is hot, at least hold[0] s, re-engages (enemyDecide) when fbP < off or after hold[1]; then cool s. Flashed / cornered (back in a fight) ends it. AI_ON only. ?dev window.__cqcFallback.
- Apartment destruction (APARTMENT DESTRUCTION section): world/src/furniture.js with Q.cqc builds breakables on their own (breakable() / brkAdd(): pendants, faceted pendants, leaf / bead / gold chandeliers with their rods and roses apart, bowls, shelf vases and bowls, the dried-stem vase, the coffee table's glass top; furn.breakables), never clustered; the floor lamp is now a movable (physics prop). aptxBuild (aptBuilder) hangs each fitting on a pivot at its rose; aptxActivate gives every standing breakable a ray-only cuboid (member 0x100: only traceBullet0's filter has it; no contacts, no LOS). aptxPass (traceBullet0, before coverHit): the round flies on through it: glass pendants shatter (rod swings, damped pendulum in aptxTick), chandeliers swing and shed crystal, fall after ~3 rounds (gold ~4) to the floor or table under them (one ray) and shatter / clang, vases / bowls burst into chips, the glass top shatters; shards on the floor (glassFloor). bulletHit (aptxHit, before the troffer test, which no longer runs in the apartment): a round up into a downlight lens pops it (dark disc in the can; a pool fitting's light goes out: tr.out, tOut); mirrors crack (star decal), the third round shatters (mirror hidden, dark backing, shards); a breakable inside the collider the round met (vase behind a shelf front, glass under a table top) breaks; furniture colliders (aptxTag: class from the material's key in M) take their own hole and dust: fabric = small hole, stuffing puffs, fibres, soft thud; wood / lacquer = splinters; stone = chips; metal (they used to read as plaster). fragBlast -> aptxBlast: all of it by distance. Broken things in room memory (aptxB, aptLens, aptMir) and re-applied on a rebuild. OPTIONS PHYSICS "Apartment lights, glass break".
- Checked: module parse, eslint no-undef (browser globals only); Node run of the page's own section text with stubs and the real world/src apartment + furniture (scratchpad ad/test.mjs, 30 checks: trip / hold / re-engage / cooldown / pushy vs shaken men / no-cover retreat / flashed; 28 breakables with colliders on their meshes, pendant pass + swing, chandelier fall and land, lens pop kills its pool light, mirror crack x2 then shatter, shelf vase behind the shelving face, sofa = fabric, grenade, reactivate, rebuild from memory). Not run in a browser (another agent's browser held the shared memory): the look of swings, falls, crack decals, stuffing; sounds; the AI behaviour in play; Quest frame time.
## CQC apartment: stair guards let rounds through, library sofa clear of the door, no white view
- Stair balusters: world/src/apartment.js tags the stair's inner / outer guard hulls (railTag: `rail` + the same guard as an oriented box `ob`), the floor-two void guard's oboxes and its radial box `rail: true`. aptActivate gives a rail a walk box only (bot at its foot), no Rapier collider: you and the men are kept out, rounds pass between the 17 mm bars. The world page ignores the tag.
- Library (floor two): the curved sofa was across the door's walk (x 13.3..16.3, 0.56 m into a 0.7 m aisle north from the door). Now a shorter curved sofa (sw 0.6, outer chord 2.0 m) at x 15.55 (x 14.58..16.52), round table, rug, books, bowl and the gold chandelier moved with it. Box test (Node): door swing clear, aisle from the door clear by 0.23 m, 1.53 m from the sofa to the gun rack, 0.5 m to the shelving, 0.18 m to the east wall.
- White view: the city faces were toneMapped false (baked already tone-mapped, x APT.viewGain 1.6). Through the post chain three tone-maps the render target anyway, but straight to the screen (headset, Max FPS / auto quality's lightest mode, post off) they went out untoned: x 1.6 clipped to pure white, next to the tone-mapped sky dome where quadrants were culled (so it changed with the view angle). view.js: CITY_FS has tonemapping_fragment, `toneMap(on)` sets the faces' toneMapped (default off: the world page is unchanged); CQC calls v.toneMap(true).

## CQC apartment: you start in the library (?aptspawn=hall = the great hall)
- aptTick puts you on floor two (level.floor = origin + APT.L2) at APT.lib (apartment x 14.0, z 4.3: in the door's walk, between the desk chair and the moved sofa), facing the north glass, beside the raid computer and the gun rack. Headless: eye 6.30 m (4.6 + 1.7), at the planned point.
- Follow-up: bloodDropLanded takes the height it landed at (drips, your bleeding, drag-trail drops), so drips on the apartment's floor two snap there instead of being dropped; mirrors.js skips hidden (shattered) mirrors when picking the live one.
## CQC apartment: real paint, contact darkening, the sun's own shadow, downlights (APARTMENT LOOK; ?aptlook=off, ?aptsun=off; headset check needed)
- Walls, ceilings, stair white: Poly Haven painted_plaster_wall (CC0, 2 x 2 m; tools/interior/build_paint.py -> textures/scans/paint_plaster_*, quest/ 1024): scan normal (roller stipple, plaster waves), scan roughness remapped to eggshell ~0.8 (ceilings flat ~0.95), grey diffuse ratio around 0.85 x the paint colour. world/textures/plaster_nor.jpg removed (unused). Skirting = the dark oak scan under a satin clear coat; doors / casings = white lacquer with the wood scan's grain in the normal only.
- world/src/finish.js (finishMat / finishAll / bakeContactField): paint is sampled with stochastic anti-tiling (value-noise patches ~1.2 m, one fetch, two only in the blend band), roller-lap sheen bands, sparse scuffs 13-50 cm above the floor, hand grime + burnish on outside corners / jambs at 0.8-1.7 m. Contact field (5 cm cells, baked once from the colliders, ~120 ms): furniture footprints (blur 12 cm, by height) and wall occupancy at chest height (gaussian 10 cm) per floor, sampled 6 cm in front of each face: floor-wall / ceiling-wall junctions, inside corners, floor under and round furniture lose indirect light (and 35 % of it on direct, for the unshadowed fill). Positions in apartment coordinates (uFinInv). surface.js now chains onto it (world page, ?aptsurf=on); there the field stays off.
- Sun: A.sun.castShadow false (default CQC has shadow maps off, and its map redrew with every shadow request). aptSunChunk (boot, after tfillChunk) patches three's directional-light loop: every lit program multiplies the apartment sun by aptSunVis (sampler2DShadow, hardware bilinear compare, 3x3 taps / 2x2 headset and lite, normal offset 1.4 texel, 2.5 mm bias; outside the apartment's box: no sun; other scenes' directional lights untouched by a direction check). The map (2048 / 1024) is drawn by aptSunRender into its own target from its own Scene: a positions-only proxy of every opaque apartment + furniture mesh (merged at build, DoubleSide, CPU copy dropped after upload) and proxies of the movable furniture props; ortho camera fitted to the apartment box seen from the sun. Redrawn on sun aim, placement / world shift and when movables move (computer every 0.25 s check, headset 1 s). Men receive but do not cast it.
- Downlights (aptLampTick): the pool's two spots go to the two fittings nearest you on your floor (both floors, hysteresis 0.8 m), downlight beam 0.48 rad / penumbra 0.6 in the apartment (restored outside); the room fill takes the next ones.
- Checked: node --check (module + world files), Node bake of the field on the real apartment colliders (296, 121 ms), a minimal headless page running the real aptSunChunk + finish.js (programs link on both tap paths, shadows and contact darkening seen), and one CQC ?dev&start=apt&lite=on run: apartment built, proxy 673,746 triangles, one sun-map render, 71 movable proxies, downlights re-sorted, 15 s of play frames with no shader or page errors. The CQC views themselves were not captured (the tab was OOM-killed in the shared memory cgroup at the first screenshot; the unmodified build ?aptlook=off&aptsun=off died the same way). Not seen: the look in CQC, Quest frame time (a 673k-triangle depth pass on a sun / movable change; candidate for meshopt simplification if it hitches).
- Follow-up: the sun's shadow proxy is simplified in the background (aptSunSimplify: meshoptimizer, welded, 2 cm error, ?aptsunlod=off): 650k -> 97k triangles per redraw. Headless raid on the merged build (surfaces + sun shadow + destruction + clusters): no errors, no programs linked mid-raid, ~186k triangles a frame.

## CQC apartment: interior doors swing (?aptdoors=off = fixed open leaves; headset check needed)
- world/src/apartment.js with Q.cqc: every white interior leaf (12) is built on its own (no static collider) and returned as apt.leaves { group, hinge, y0, w, h, open yaw, shut yaw }; the shut direction is the quarter turn whose leaf lies in the opening (no wall collider at its centre / 0.8 w out).
- cqc/index.html aptDoorsActivate (from aptActivate): each is a door-system secondary leaf (door.sec: hinge, unlocked) on a revolute joint; its body sits on the hinge square to the room (a revolute joint's zero is the bodies' relative turn, so zero = shut), the collider turned and set out inside it; drawn by a hinge group > turn > leaf. Starts open as drawn. door.free: no closer (updateDoorsPre: target = its own angle, k 0, c 3), so a leaf stays where you leave it. aptDoorWalk keeps its walk box on the leaf (the stock walk update assumed the body at the leaf centre). E / click / VR hand, kicks, rounds and holes, creaks through the door system; the men open the ones they walk into (yardDoorsTick from aptViewTick). Library: the floor-two path map now reaches the library (the sofa move opened it).
- Checked headless: 12 doors, start at their drawn open angles (±1.35-1.67 rad), steady over 6 s; a push swings each toward shut / through and it stays. Not checked by eye, by E press, in a headset.
- Follow-up: a man who surrenders on the apartment's floor two steps back on floor two's grid (surrNav).

## CQC apartment: fewer draws (plain materials share one batch; ?aptmerge=off)
- Measured headless from the great hall: 63 draw calls, 40 of them the meshlet batches (one per material: furniture 27, architecture 13). world/src/clusters.js clusterize(meshes, { mergePlain, prep }): meshes with a plain material (standard, no maps, opaque, front side, no emissive) are merged per parent into one mesh with colour (vertex colour) and roughness / metalness (aRM) per vertex, drawn by one shared material ('plainRM' program; prep = finish.js finishMat 'ao', so contact darkening stays). 30 meshes merged. Now 52 calls, batches 24; 433 programs, all linked. The world page calls clusterize without the option (unchanged).

## CQC apartment: baked bounce light from the irradiance volume (?aptvol=off; headset check needed)
- world/src/finish.js: FIN.vol (read when a program builds; the world page leaves it false, so its programs are unchanged) adds VOL_PARS / VOL_APPLY after lights_fragment_maps: the world's volume (textures/light/volume.json + .bin, L1 SH every 0.5 m, 8 layers) as one RGBA half-float Data3DTexture per coefficient (loadVolume, no float conversion), 4 fetches (hardware trilinear; y mapped to a fractional layer index clamped inside its floor, so the floors never blend), at the apartment position + 0.18 n with the normal-mapped geometryNormal in apartment axes. It replaces iblIrradiance (the env capture's diffuse; three splits it between diffuse and the specular multiscatter) inside the apartment box and scales the hemisphere's irradiance by uFinV.z; the contact field (aomap hook, after it) still multiplies it. Key '|fin-<kind>v'. vFinN now includes the batching / instance rotation.
- cqc/index.html aptLookBuild: FIN.vol = true and loadVolume (not with ?aptvol=off or ?aptsurf=on); aptViewTick: A.hemi = APT.hemiVol 0.5 (men and guns, not patched) and uFinV = (1, APT.volK 1.0, 0.12 / hemi) so apartment surfaces see a 0.12 fill as surface.js did. OPTIONS PHYSICS row "Apartment baked bounce light".
- Checked: node --check (finish.js, the CQC module); a minimal headless page with the real finish.js + volume: Mesh, BatchedMesh and InstancedMesh variants link and read the same (grey card in the great hall 81 -> 176 with the volume, warm). Full CQC run headless was OOM-killed (another session's browser in the shared memory), so not seen in CQC: the levels against the sun / pool spots (APT.volK is the knob), Quest frame time.
- Merged check (headless CQC ?dev&start=apt&lite=on): 434 programs linked, 0 failed, no page errors; 58 draw calls from the hall (city view 12 that frame). Not checked by eye.

## CQC apartment: performance pass 2 (measured headless; headset / real-GPU check needed)
- Measured (headless, A/B per feature, render loop stopped, gl sync): the apartment's meshlet batches were ~65 % of a frame's draw time. WebGL multi-draw is a loop of single draws on most drivers (ANGLE on Metal / D3D, the Quest browser), and they held 10,814 clusters of 124 triangles (thousands of draws a frame). world/src/clusters.js now groups the meshlets into spatial chunks (Morton order of their centres, up to opts.chunk 4096 triangles or opts.span 6 m) with a merged normal cone (cull only when every member faces one way, margin 0.06). Same for the world page.
- Mirrors (world/src/mirrors.js opts range / scale / samples / vrPx): CQC passes no cube captures (they rendered the whole CQC scene 6 times per mirror, a hitch on the first approach; the city env stands in), the live planar reflection only within 3.2 m, 0.4 of the screen, no MSAA, 384 px per eye in the headset.
- Reflections without the recompile: aptEnvAssign gives every apartment material its envMap at build time (an empty-scene PMREM, A.envRT); aptEnvCapture renders the city cube into that same target (pm.fromCubemap(rt, A.envRT)), so no apartment program compiles a second time after you enter.
- Downlight pools no longer jump: aptLampTick queues the swap (APTL.pend, hysteresis 1.6 m), aptLampFade fades the two spots (and rects) out in 0.22 s, moves them, back in over 0.3 s.
- Gun rack: aptRackCull hides the rack and the guns / magazines resting on it while you are on floor one (walls hide them; ~100k triangles the frustum took in from the hall).
- Spent casing pools (every CQC room): InstancedMesh.count = last case in use + 1 (all slots were drawn every frame, ~26k triangles).
- Enemy LODs closer: full mesh within 4.5 m (was 7), 30 % to 12 m (was 16); headset 3.5 / 9 (was 5 / 11).
- Your own body, gloves and sleeves (skinned, ~120k triangles) were built with frustum culling off and drawn every frame (the mirror pass too). skinCullTick (after vmTick; ?skincull=off) gives each a sphere from its bones (per bone the farthest vertex it moves, bind pose; per frame the bones' mean + max reach + 16 cm), found by a scan every 2 s of skinned meshes outside the men. Headless: looking ahead 4 of 10 meshes drawn (61k triangles), looking down all 10.
- City view (world/src/view.js, also the world page): faces decoded off the main thread (ImageBitmapLoader, flipY at decode; falls back to TextureLoader), uploaded one texture per ~18 ms before the probe is shown (renderer.initTexture), the last 4 probes kept loaded (2 in the headset; walking back used to reload them), a kept probe at zero opacity is not drawn, failed fetches retried (a burst could fail with ERR_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES and leave a probe stuck "loading": no city in that direction).
- Apartment sun shadow: 4 hardware-filtered taps on the computer (was 9). Raid screen blink: once a second, only the library screen redraws (both canvases were re-uploaded twice a second).
- Raid stutters (CPU profile of a siege wave): renderer.debug.checkShaderErrors is off unless ?dev / ?shadercheck (?noshadercheck turns it off with ?dev): the check read every new program's info logs, a sync wait per shader. His gun is rebuilt per man (GUN_SPECS makes new geometry), so enemyLodGeo welded and simplified it again on every spawn: LODs are now cached by a signature of the vertex data (ENEMY_LOD_SIG; one man's LODs serve every copy). aptRaidWarm (after the apartment warm-up compile): one gun of each loadout gets its LODs, the men's and those guns' textures are uploaded one per 30 ms. Measured: no mergeVertices / LOD / enemy texture work during a siege wave; per-frame uploads left are skinning bone textures only. The raid blink is a red additive layer over the library screen (no canvas redraw).

## CQC apartment: occlusion culling (PVS), sharper floors (?aptpvs=off)
- world/src/pvs.js bakePVS: the clustered apartment is static, so what can be seen from where is baked on the GPU at load: per 1.5 m cell of the plan, on each floor, at two eye heights (1.0 / 1.85 m above the floor), every chunk drawn into a 6-face ID cube (256 px per face, one colour per chunk, the batches' own buffers with a per-vertex id), read back without stalling (readRenderTargetPixelsAsync, 3 in flight), a cell's set = itself + its 8 neighbours (any spot inside the cell is covered). Cached in localStorage world.pvs (key: chunk count, grid, a signature of the chunk spheres); paced slower while the game runs (TICK_AT).
- Run time (aptPvsCull from clusters.js' onBeforeRender, userData.pvsCull): only the game's own view (desktop camera or a headset eye; never mirrors, stills, shadow passes) hides frustum-visible chunks outside the camera cell's set; between the floors (stair) both floors' sets; chunks within 3.5 m always draw; outside the plan or before the bake, everything draws. Doors, props, men are not chunks, so they never occlude. A wall-damage hole of 4 cm radius or more (see-through blob) turns it off for that visit (room.aptPvsOff).
- Headless A/B (same frame, culling on vs off, full 1.5 m / 256 px bake, 312 cells, ~6 min in SwiftShader, seconds on a GPU): library 802k -> 547k triangles, great hall 422k -> 250k, bedroom 567k -> 401k. The ID buffer was drawn as the game draws (same side and polygonOffset: the stair hall's greige plaster and paint bands lost depth ties without it; cut-out materials mark but do not occlude). Left: 1-4 differing pixels of 32,400 per view, all on chunks drawn in both (an ID render from the camera at those pixels: z-fighting coplanar faces whose winner flips with draw order), none from a hidden chunk. Cache key pvs2.
- Visual: apartment textures use the GPU's maximum anisotropy on the computer (16): floors at a grazing angle stay sharp.
- Loose things in the PVS (cache key pvs3): bakePVS opts.extras gives each furniture prop (room.aptProps), breakable fitting / vase (aptx list) and mirror its own id, drawn after the chunks with no depth write (marked where the chunks leave it in view, never an occluder). aptPvsExtrasTick (aptViewTick, before the mirror tick) turns layer 0 off on its meshes while it is outside the camera cell's set, farther than 3.5 m and still where it was baked (a prop moved over 30 cm or held, a fitting broken / dropped / falling = always drawn); off when you are not in the apartment or a wall is breached. A culled mirror also skips its reflection pass.
- Headless A/B with the loose things (same frame, all culling on vs off; 747 ids = 678 chunks + 69 loose): library 250 -> 197 draw calls, 837k -> 564k triangles (51 of 69 loose culled); great hall 88 -> 64, 424k -> 243k (55); bedroom 173 -> 94, 564k -> 325k (61). Differing pixels unchanged (1-4, the pre-existing z-fight on chunks drawn in both).
